Result: 实验结果表明，语言标签提示带来的提升最为显著；词汇匹配方法在匹配子集上取得了稳定且一致的改进效果；RL微调也带来了可懂度的进一步提升。

Conclusion: 语言提示是提升跨语种语音克隆性能的关键手段，词汇匹配方法在特定场景下为领域术语发音提供了有效补充，整体方案在IWSLT 2026跨语种语音克隆任务中展现出良好潜力。

Abstract: Cross-lingual voice cloning aims to generate speech in a target language while preserving speaker identity from a source-language reference. This task is central to speech translation and is the focus of the IWSLT 2026 Cross-Lingual Voice Cloning track. A key challenge is maintaining intelligibility and naturalness in the presence of accent variation and domain-specific vocabulary. We build on a multilingual text-to-speech model, FishAudio-S2-Pro, and introduce language tag prompting to improve language control and reduce accent leakage. We further apply reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning for task adaptation and observe improvements in intelligibility. Finally, we propose a reference-conditioned lexical matching method that improves pronunciation of domain-specific terms when lexical overlap is present. Results show that language prompting provides the largest gains, while lexical matching yields consistent improvements on matched subsets.

</details>


### [144] [Phun-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Phonological Understanding in Chinese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07300)
*Xing Yue,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Phun-Bench，一个专门用于评估大语言模型语音理解能力的中文基准测试，涵盖同音、押韵和语音相似性三个维度。结果表明LLM在灵活运用语音知识方面远逊于人类，并提出了关于LLM语音"感知"机制的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM研究主要关注语义和拼写，忽视了语音（声音）维度；现有语音相关基准测试要么可通过死记硬背解决，要么与其他能力混淆，无法真正衡量LLM的语音理解能力。

Method: 构建Phun-Bench中文基准，设计同音（Homophony）、押韵（Rhyme）和语音相似性（Phonetic Similarity）三个维度的多样化任务，系统评估LLM在语音理解上的表现，并进行详细的机制分析。

Result: LLM在回忆正确发音方面表现优异，但在以人类说话者那种灵活、直观的方式运用语音知识方面普遍困难；揭示了LLM语音理解能力的根本局限。

Conclusion: LLM的语音理解能力存在显著不足，其内在机制可能与人类的语音感知方式有本质差异；该领域是一个值得深入探索的前沿方向。

Abstract: Language is a vehicle for thought, intricately tied to sounds, symbols, and meaning. However, most large language model (LLM) research focuses on meaning (semantics) and symbols (spelling) while largely overlooking sounds. Existing benchmarks on LLMs' phonological abilities are either solvable through rote memorization or intertwined with other abilities, making them inadequate to measure LLMs' genuine ability in phonological understanding. Here, we present Phun-Bench, a purpose-built Chinese benchmark with diverse tasks and settings across three dimensions (Homophony, Rhyme, and Phonetic Similarity), designed to systematically evaluate LLMs' phonological understanding. Our results show that while LLMs excel at recalling correct pronunciations, they generally struggle to leverage phonological knowledge in the flexible and intuitive way that human speakers do. Moreover, through detailed analyses, we propose a hypothesis regarding the underlying mechanism of LLMs' phonological understanding and "perception", highlighting an underexplored frontier for future research.

</details>


### [145] [SV-Detect: AI-generated Text Detection with Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07313)
*Mikhail Vishnyakov,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于冻结语言模型隐藏表征中"引导向量"（steering vectors）的机器生成文本检测方法。该方法在每层构建区别人工与机器文本的方向，利用层间对齐特征训练轻量分类器，在域迁移、源模型迁移和编辑攻击等分布偏移下均表现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器生成文本检测方法在分布偏移（如跨域、跨模型、编辑攻击）下性能显著下降，亟需一种对分布偏移鲁棒的检测方案。作者希望利用预训练语言模型内部的表征几何结构来解决这一问题。

Method: （1）冻结一个语言模型，在每层提取人工文本与机器生成文本的隐藏表征，通过两者表征差构建"引导向量"方向；（2）对任意输入文本，计算其每层表征在该方向上的投影对齐度，形成层级投影特征；（3）在此特征上训练一个轻量级分类器（如逻辑回归）输出最终检测分数。

Result: 该方法在分布内数据和多种分布偏移场景（跨域、跨源模型、润色/重写等机器编辑变换）下均取得优异检测性能。可解释性分析表明，学到的方向既捕捉了可识别的风格线索，也包含了超越表层特征的额外判别信号。

Conclusion: 论文将机器生成文本检测重新定位为表征空间探测问题，并证明引导向量是一种简单有效的解决方案。该视角为构建鲁棒的假文本检测器开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Detecting machine-generated text is especially difficult under distribution shift, such as transfer across domains, source models, and editing attacks. We propose a fake-text detector based on steering vectors extracted from the hidden representations of a frozen language model. At each layer, we construct a direction that separates human-written from machine-generated text, and represent each input by its layer-wise alignment with these directions. A lightweight classifier trained on these projection features yields the final detection score. Our method achieves strong performance both in-distribution and under distribution shift, including across domains, source models, and machine-editing transformations such as polishing and rewriting. Interpretation analyses show that the learned directions align with recognizable stylistic cues while capturing substantial additional signal beyond surface features. These results position fake-text detection as a representation-space probing problem and show that steering vectors provide a simple and effective solution.

</details>


### [146] [LLM-Guided Evolution for Medical Decision Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07342)
*Ivan Sviridov,Artem Oskin,Ivan Panin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry Dylov,Ivan Oseledets,Aleksandr Nesterov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用LLM引导的MAP-Elites进化算法，在推理阶段自动发现医疗决策策略，在分诊、交互式咨询和医学图像分类三个任务上均优于人工设计的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型适配到临床工作流程通常需要昂贵的微调或人工提示/流水线工程，研究者希望找到一种推理阶段的替代方案，以更低的成本发现有效的医疗决策策略。

Method: 采用LLM-guided MAP-Elites进化算法，将医疗任务（紧急分诊、交互式咨询、医学图像分类）形式化为可执行工件的进化搜索，通过任务特定的适应度函数进行优化。

Result: 分诊任务中，Semigran准确率从77.3%提升至87.1%，紧急召回率从0.60提升至0.97；交互式咨询中，进化策略在Llama-3、Qwen-3.5、Gemma-4上均改善了准确率-成本前沿；PneumoniaMNIST上，仅通过提示进化即可提升冻结的MedGemma VLM性能，同时保持严格的JSON输出。

Conclusion: 性能提升源于可解释的程序级机制（校准的分诊边界、针对性证据获取、选择性承诺和面向发现的视觉决策规则），而非表面的提示改写，证明了LLM引导进化在医疗AI中的实用价值。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to clinical workflows often requires costly fine-tuning or manual prompt and pipeline engineering. We study LLM-guided MAP-Elites evolution as an inference-time alternative for discovering medical decision strategies and provide an implementation repository at https://github.com/univanxx/llm_guided_evo_medical. We formulate urgency triage, interactive consultation, and medical image classification as evolutionary searches over executable artifacts optimized by task-specific fitness functions.
  Across all three settings, evolution improves over manually designed baselines under practical constraints. In triage, evolved programs increase Semigran accuracy from $77.3\%$ to $87.1\%$ and emergency recall from $0.60$ to $0.97$, while improving safety-weighted held-out MIMIC-ESI performance. In interactive consultation, evolved policies improve the accuracy--cost frontier across Llama-3, Qwen-3.5, and Gemma-4 and transfer to held-out iCRAFTMD. In PneumoniaMNIST, prompt-only evolution improves frozen MedGemma VLMs while preserving strict JSON outputs. Qualitative analysis shows that the gains come from interpretable program-level mechanisms, calibrated triage boundaries, targeted evidence acquisition, selective commitment, and finding-oriented visual decision rules, rather than superficial prompt rewording alone.

</details>


### [147] [M$^3$Exam: Benchmarking Multimodal Memory for Realistic User-Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07402)
*Zhengjun Huang,Wenxuan Liu,Zhoujin Tian,Wei Chen,Junle Chen,Yuqian Wu,Fangyuan Zhang,Qintian Guo,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出M³Exam基准与M³Proctor方法，前者评估多模态对话记忆中的跨模态 grounding 与隐式信息推理，后者通过按需消费原始视觉源，以降低70%索引构建开销换取13%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准假设人-人交互、视觉稀疏且内容直白，无法评估智能体在真实多模态文件交互中的记忆能力，尤其是跨模态 grounding 和隐式用户信息推断，亟待一个查询驱动的多模态对话记忆评测框架。

Method: 构建M³Exam基准：基于真实用户-智能体交互，设计跨模态 grounding、跨会话推理等多维评测指标。提出M³Proctor方法：检测查询模态偏置，仅在需要时消费原始视觉源，从而压缩多模态记忆的索引构建与检索开销。

Result: MLLM及记忆系统在跨模态 grounding 与跨会话推理上存在明显差距，且累积多模态上下文带来效率代价。M³Proctor实现13%准确率提升，同时将索引构建时间和检索 token 量分别降低超过70%。

Conclusion: 多模态对话记忆亟需更真实的评测与更高效的记忆机制；按需视觉消费是兼顾精度与效率的可行路径。

Abstract: Language agents are increasingly deployed over accumulating multimodal information, yet existing benchmarks assume a human-human form with sparse visuals and straightforward content, evaluating neither reasoning over authentic multimodal file interaction nor the interpretation of concealed user information. We therefore introduce M$^3$Exam, a query-centric multimodal conversational memory benchmark built on realistic user-agent interaction, with multi-dimensional evaluation spanning cross-modal grounding and implicit information inference. Benchmarking MLLMs and memory systems reveals persistent gaps in cross-modal grounding, cross session reasoning, and the efficiency cost of accumulating multimodal context. We further propose M$^3$Proctor, a multimodal memory method that detects query modality bias and consumes raw visual sources only on demand, improving accuracy by 13% while cutting index-construction time and retrieved tokens by over 70%.

</details>


### [148] [The Masked Advantage: Uncovering Local-Language Access to Cultural Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07422)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Fei,Amr Mohamed,Sarah Almeida Carneiro,Mersin Konomi,Mingmeng Geng,Ahmed Asaad,Guokan Shang,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个受控评估框架，通过区分文化无关与文化特定问题、英语与本地语言查询，并采用IRT模型分离语言能力与文化知识获取，发现在考虑英语能力优势后，本地语言在获取本地文化知识方面普遍具有正向优势，这一优势在原始准确率中常被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估存在两大局限：(1)依赖模板化平行问题，无法反映文化知识的自然涌现方式；(2)原始准确率混淆了通用语言能力与语言条件化的知识获取。因此需要一种能清晰分离二者的方法，以回答"本地文化知识通过英语还是本地语言更容易获取"这一核心问题。

Method: 作者从区域基准和本地来源收集真实世界文化问题，构建2×2交叉设计（文化无关/文化特定 × 英语/本地语言），并采用共享1PL项目反应理论(IRT)模型估计模型能力，从而将通用语言能力与文化条件化的知识获取分离开来。研究覆盖13个地区和约80个模型。

Result: 在文化无关问题上，英语始终表现更优，反映出更强的英语能力；但在控制能力差异后，几乎所有地区-模型设置中本地语言均显示出正向的文化知识获取优势。这种优势在原始准确率中常被掩盖，但在前沿模型、区域对齐模型或语言适配模型中更为可见。

Conclusion: 本地语言表现较差并不必然意味着文化知识薄弱；本地文化知识可能通过本地语言更易获取，只是被有限的语言能力所掩盖。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to answer culturally grounded questions across languages, yet it remains unclear whether local cultural knowledge is better accessed through English or the local language. Existing evaluations face two key limitations: many rely on parallel template-based questions that may not reflect how cultural knowledge naturally appears, and raw accuracy conflates general language proficiency with language-conditioned knowledge access. We address these issues with a controlled framework built on real-world cultural questions collected from regional benchmarks and local sources. By crossing question type (culture-agnostic vs. culture-specific) with query language (English vs. local language), and estimating ability with a shared 1PL item response theory model, we separate proficiency from localized knowledge access. Across 13 locales and roughly 80 models, we find a consistent English advantage on culture-agnostic questions, indicating stronger English proficiency. However, after accounting for this proficiency gap, local languages show a positive knowledge-access advantage in nearly all locale-model settings. This advantage is often masked in raw accuracy but becomes more visible for frontier, regionally aligned, or language-adapted models. Our results suggest that weaker local-language performance does not necessarily imply weaker cultural knowledge; rather, local cultural knowledge may be more accessible through the local language but hidden by limited language proficiency.

</details>


### [149] [Sycophantic Praise: Evaluating Excessive Praise in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07441)
*Daniel Vennemeyer,Phan Anh Duong,Meryl Ye,Ruihong Huang,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化框架来量化语言模型中的谄媚性赞美，发现其在社交和解释性领域远比客观推理任务中常见，揭示赞美校准是一个独特的对齐挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对语言模型谄媚（Sycophancy）的研究主要集中于过度同意或附和，而显性赞美和奉承行为被相对忽视。作者认为谄媚性赞美是一个独特的对齐问题，且现有方法无法可靠地测量该现象。

Method: 引入一个参数化框架，通过比较模型输出的赞美程度与（1）用户贡献质量（2）预期用户能力水平，来判定赞美是否过度。同时将该框架与通用LLM评判器进行人工标注一致性对比。

Result: 该框架在与人工标注的一致性上显著优于通用LLM评判器；谄媚性赞美在社交和解释性领域中的出现频率远高于客观推理场景。

Conclusion: 谄媚性赞美是不同于过度同意的对齐挑战，赞美校准应被视为独立的对齐研究方向，需要专门的方法和评估标准。

Abstract: Sycophancy in language models is typically studied as excessive agreement or validation, while explicit praise and flattery have received comparatively little attention. We argue that sycophantic praise is a distinct alignment problem that cannot be reliably measured using current methods. We introduce a parameterized framework that measures whether praise is excessive relative to contribution quality and expected user ability. We show that our framework substantially outperforms generic LLM judges in agreement with human annotations, and that sycophantic praise occurs far more frequently in social and interpretive domains than in objective reasoning settings. Together, these findings position praise calibration as a distinct alignment challenge.

</details>


### [150] [Supervision versus Demonstration-Based In-Context Learning for Multiword Expression Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07479)
*Sercan Karakaş,Yusuf Şimşek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究土耳其语惯用轻动词构式的自动检测，将其构建为二分类任务，在人工标注的受控数据集上对比了监督基线模型（BERTurk）与三种指令微调大语言模型在不同提示策略下的表现。结果发现零样本下LLM召回率极低，单样本提示虽显著提升检测能力但引入模型特异性偏差，而多样本提示能改善校准并实现稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土耳其语惯用轻动词构式（LVC）与完全字面义的动宾组合常共享相同的表层形式，对多词表达处理构成严峻挑战。现有方法在此类语言的惯用性分类上缺乏系统评估，尤其是大语言模型在少资源语言中的提示敏感性尚不清晰，亟需探索LLM在土耳其语元语言分类任务中的能力边界。

Method: 将土耳其语LVC检测构建为二分类任务（字面义 vs. 惯用义），构建包含147个样本的受控测试集，包括域外随机句和域内字面控制句作为负例，LVC实例作为正例。基线模型采用BERTurk加分类头进行监督微调。对比实验选取三个不同家族的指令微调LLM，分别在零样本、单样本和少样本提示条件下评估，系统分析提示样例如何改变错误分布模式。

Result: 零样本条件下，LLM在负例上表现良好但LVC召回率极低。单样本提示能大幅提升LVC检测能力，但会引入强烈的模型特异性偏差，导致模型过度预测或不足预测LVC。多样本提示改善了概率校准，GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen 2.5-14B获得稳健的整体性能。总体而言，监督基线仍具竞争力，而精心构建提示样例后LLM在LVC检测上可匹敌甚至超越基线。

Conclusion: 土耳其语惯用轻动词构式检测对提示策略高度敏感。监督基线BERTurk保持竞争力，但经精心设计少样本提示的LLM（尤其是GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen 2.5-14B）可在LVC检测上达到相当或更优的性能。提示样例的数量和质量对模型校准至关重要，单样本提示虽提升召回但需警惕其引入的预测偏差。

Abstract: Turkish idiomatic light verb constructions (LVCs) are challenging for multiword expression processing because they often share the same surface form as fully literal verb-object combinations while functioning as a single, partially idiomatic predicate. We frame Turkish LVC detection as a binary classification task (literal meaning vs. idiomatic meaning) and evaluate on a manually created controlled set (N=147) with matched negatives: out-of-domain random sentences and in-domain literal controls (NLVC), alongside LVC positives. We compare a supervised Turkish encoder baseline (BERTurk with a classifier head) to three instruction-tuned LLMs from different families under zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot prompting, and analyze how demonstrations shift error profiles. In zero-shot, LLMs perform well on negatives but show very low LVC recall. One-shot prompting sharply improves LVC detection but can induce strong, model-specific biases, leading models to overpredict or underpredict LVCs. A richer few-shot prompt improves calibration and yields robust overall performance for GPT-OSS-20B and Qwen 2.5-14B. Overall, the results highlight substantial prompt sensitivity in Turkish metalinguistic classification: the supervised baseline remains competitive, while prompted LLMs can match or exceed it on LVCs with carefully constructed demonstrations.

</details>


### [151] [Your UnEmbedding Matrix is Secretly a Feature Lens for Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07502)
*Songhao Wu,Zhongxin Chen,Yuxuan Liu,Heng Cui,Cong Li,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM的文本嵌入倾向于过度表达高频无信息token，削弱语义捕捉能力。本文提出EmbedFilter，通过滤除LLM中unembedding矩阵内编码高频token的子空间，抑制高频token影响，实现语义增强与内在降维，在保持嵌入质量的同时降低存储并加速检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到文本嵌入投影到词汇空间时，会与高频但无信息的token对齐。这种高频token的过度表达抑制了模型捕捉细粒度语义的能力，导致LLM作为现成嵌入模型时性能不佳。

Method: 提出EmbedFilter，一种简单的线性变换。核心思路是：LLM内部的unembedding矩阵编码了一个潜在空间，该空间将高频token主动写入嵌入表示中。通过滤除这一子空间，抑制高频token的影响，从而精炼文本嵌入。该方法同时实现了内在的降维效果。

Result: 在多个LLM骨干网络上实验表明，配备EmbedFilter的LLM在显著降低嵌入维度的情况下，仍能取得优越的零样本下游性能，同时降低了索引存储开销并加速了检索速度。

Conclusion: EmbedFilter有效解决了LLM文本嵌入中高频token主导的问题，通过简单的线性变换实现语义增强与降维。作者希望该发现能为LLM表征机制提供更深理解，并启发更原则性的嵌入训练设计。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit impressive zero-shot capabilities across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, they struggle to function as off-the-shelf embedding models, leading to suboptimal performance on massive text embedding benchmarks. In this paper, we identify a potential cause underlying this deficiency. Our motivation stems from an unexpected observation: text embeddings tend to align with frequent but uninformative tokens when projected onto the vocabulary space. We argue that this excessive expression of high-frequency tokens suppresses the model's ability to capture nuanced semantics. To address this, we introduce EmbedFilter, a simple linear transformation designed to refine text embeddings derived from LLMs directly. Specifically, we uncover that the unembedding matrix within LLMs encodes a latent space that is actively writing these frequent tokens into embedding space. By filtering out this subspace, EmbedFilter suppress the influence of high-frequency tokens, thereby enhancing semantic representations. As a compelling byproduct, this enables an inherent dimensionality reduction, lowering index storage and speedup retrieval while fully preserving the refined embedding quality. Our experiments across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that LLMs equipped with EmbedFilter achieve superior zero-shot downstream performance even with significantly reduced embedding dimensions. We hope our findings provide deeper insights into the mechanisms of LLM-based representations and inspire more principled designs to improve text embeddings training. Our code is available at https://github.com/CentreChen/EmbFilter.

</details>


### [152] [Agentopia: Long-Term Life Simulation and Learning in Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07513)
*Xintao Wang,Sirui Zheng,Hongqiu Wu,Weiyuan Li,Jen-tse Huang,Minghao Zhu,Can Zu,Qi Deng,Jiawei Wang,Qianyu He,Heng Wang,Xiaojian Wu,Yunzhe Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentopia 框架，在 100 个智能体、10 年模拟时长的多智能体社会中研究长期生命模拟，并利用生命奖励（life reward）通过拒绝采样训练 LLM，使其在模拟中表现出更丰富的涌现社会行为，并在下游角色扮演基准上提升 15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体社会模拟通常仅运行数天，社交交互深度和长期成长受限。本文旨在探索：LLM 能否从长达数年的模拟社会经验中学习，以更好地理解和复现人类行为，同时提升 LLM 在社交生活中的类人智能。

Method: 提出 Agentopia 框架：100 个智能体在 10 年模拟周期内自主追求个人成长、发展社交关系、满足需求与目标。定义“生命奖励”以镜像人类幸福感，并利用该奖励通过拒绝采样（rejection sampling）对底层 LLM 进行训练优化。

Result: 智能体展现出丰富的涌现社会行为；生命奖励训练有效增强了底层 LLM 能力，提升了模拟中智能体的幸福感指标，并在下游角色扮演基准上取得 +15.6% 的性能提升，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 长期生命模拟与生命奖励训练是提升 LLM 社交智能的有效路径。Agentopia 框架验证了通过多年模拟社会经验来培育 LLM 类人能力的可行性，为智能体社会仿真与 LLM 学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Humans learn from social life. Simulating this process with LLM-powered agents represents a promising research direction, raising a natural question: whether LLMs can learn from such simulated social experience to better understand and replicate human behavior. However, prior agent society simulations typically operate at the scale of days, limiting the depth of social interactions and long-term growth. In this paper, we study long-term life simulation and LLM learning in agent societies, with two goals: (1) investigating social behaviors that emerge from life-long simulation, and (2) developing anthropomorphic capabilities in LLMs, particularly intelligence in social life, through years of simulated social experience. Specifically, we present Agentopia, a comprehensive framework for long-term life simulation in multi-agent societies, where 100 agents autonomously pursue personal growth, develop social relationships, and fulfill their needs and goals over 10 simulated years. We define life reward to mirror human well-being, and leverage this reward to train LLMs via rejection sampling. Extensive experiments show that agents exhibit rich emergent social behaviors. Furthermore, life reward training effectively enhances the underlying LLM, which leads to improved agent well-being in simulation, and generalizes to downstream role-playing benchmarks with +15.6% improvement.

</details>


### [153] [How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07515)
*Luca Avena,Gianmarco Bet,Bernardo Busoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过受控基准测试研究大语言模型在离散概率问题上的推理能力，发现模型在标准问题上表现优异（准确率0.96），但在反直觉问题上大幅下降（0.59），且存在token偏差和易受误导等问题，表明当前LLM尚非真正的概率推理者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在高等数学问题上表现突出，但其是否真正具备概率推理能力尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对LLM在概率推理中启发式思维和偏差的系统性评估，因此需要通过受控实验来揭示模型在概率推理中的真实能力边界和脆弱性。

Method: 构建两个离散概率数据集：一组为标准练习题，另一组为设计用于触发启发式推理的反直觉问题。评估8个最先进模型，每个模型分别在有无思维链提示（Chain-of-Thought）条件下进行测试。进一步通过替换规范表述为伪装变体来测试token偏差，以及在提示中嵌入误导性建议来测试模型的抗干扰能力。

Result: 模型在标准问题上平均准确率达0.96，但在反直觉问题上仅为0.59。Token偏差导致性能下降超过20%。嵌入误导性建议使性能最多下降34%，且无一模型能够免疫。思维链提示未能从根本上消除上述问题。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型尽管在高等数学任务中表现优异，但尚未成为真正的概率推理者。它们在面对反直觉问题和表述变化时表现出显著的脆弱性，容易受到表面形式（token偏差）和误导性语境的影响，揭示了其推理更多依赖于模式匹配而非深层概率理解的本质。

Abstract: We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Optimal Rates for Generalization of Gradient Descent Methods with Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06764)
*Junyu Zhou,Puyu Wang,Yunwen Lei,Yiming Ying,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对深度ReLU网络在梯度下降和随机梯度下降下的泛化性能进行了全面分析，首次在宽度与深度和样本量呈多项式关系的假设下，建立了最小最大最优的 excess population risk 收敛速率，证明深度ReLU网络可达到与核方法相媲美的最优泛化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NTK框架下的泛化理论大多局限于浅层网络，深度神经网络在回归问题上的泛化理论存在明显空白，亟需填补这一理论缺口。

Method: 基于神经正切核（NTK）框架，对深度ReLU网络在GD和SGD训练下的泛化性能进行理论分析，假设网络宽度随深度和训练样本量呈多项式缩放，推导excess population risk的上下界。

Result: 首次证明了深度ReLU网络在GD和SGD下均可达到最小最大最优的excess population risk收敛速率，与核方法的最优泛化率持平，条件为网络宽度足够大（多项式缩放）。

Conclusion: 在足够宽度条件下，深度ReLU网络的梯度下降方法能够实现最优泛化性能，理论上不逊于核方法，为深度网络泛化理论提供了坚实支撑。

Abstract: Recent progress has been made in understanding the statistical generalization performance of gradient descent methods for overparameterized neural networks within the neural tangent kernel (NTK) regime. However, most of the existing work on regression problems is limited to shallow network architectures, leaving a notable gap in the theory of deep neural networks. This paper addresses this gap by presenting a comprehensive generalization analysis for deep ReLU networks trained using gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD). Specifically, we establish the first known minimax-optimal rates of excess population risk for both GD and SGD with deep ReLU networks, under the assumption that the network width scales polynomially with respect to the network depth and training sample size. Our results demonstrate that with sufficient width, gradient descent methods for deep ReLU networks can achieve optimal generalization rates on par with kernel methods.

</details>


### [155] [Generalization in Deep Neural Networks: Minimax Rates for Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06772)
*Junyu Zhou,Puyu Wang,Yunwen Lei,Marius Kloft,Yiming Ying*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了过参数化深度神经网络（DNN）与核方法在学习动力学上的联系，证明梯度下降/随机梯度下降训练的DNN可完全继承核方法的优良学习动态，并在此基础上推导出DNN在回归任务中关于超额总体风险的极小极大最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络的泛化性能是深度学习理论的核心问题。虽然NTK机制下的研究增进了对浅层架构的理解，但深度神经网络（尤其是回归任务）的统计泛化性质仍远未被充分理解，亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先，作者建立了光滑激活函数DNN在梯度方法下的学习动力学与核方法之间的关键联系；然后，基于该联系和核方法的最优性理论，在网络宽度关于样本量多项式增长的假设下，导出梯度下降(GD)和随机梯度下降(SGD)的超额总体风险收敛速率，并证明其极小极大最优性。

Result: 首次证明了过参数化DNN通过梯度方法训练可完全继承核方法的学习动力学；推导出GD和SGD在DNN回归任务中的首个已知极小极大最优超额风险收敛速率；证明在足够宽度条件下，DNN的泛化性能可与核方法媲美。

Conclusion: 本文显著缩小了DNN回归任务泛化理论中的空白：只要网络宽度随样本量多项式增长，GD/SGD训练的过参数化DNN即可达到与核方法相当的极小极大最优泛化性能，为深度学习的统计理论提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Understanding the generalization performance of over-parameterized neural networks has become a central topic in deep learning theory. While recent advances, particularly works under the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regime, have shed light on the behavior of shallow architectures, the statistical generalization properties of deep neural networks (DNNs), especially in regression tasks, remain far less understood. In this paper, we make significant progress toward closing this gap by providing a comprehensive generalization analysis of DNNs trained using gradient-based methods. First, we establish, for the first time, a crucial connection between the learning dynamics of a DNN with smooth activation functions trained via gradient-based methods and those of kernel methods, showing that gradient-based methods on over-parameterized DNNs can fully inherit the favorable learning dynamics of their kernel counterparts. Building on this connection and the well-established optimality of kernel methods, we derive the first known minimax-optimal rates for the excess population risk of both gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD), under the assumption that network width scales polynomially with the sample size. Our results demonstrate that, with sufficient width, DNNs trained by GD or SGD can achieve generalization performance comparable to kernel-based methods.

</details>


### [156] [Empirical Transfer Operators and Finite-Sample Change Detection for Noisy Expanding Interval Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06785)
*Aparna Rajput*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究一维噪声动力系统的有限样本变化检测，通过状态空间划分、转移矩阵估计和Doeblin正则化获得经验平稳分布，基于滑动窗口与基线分布的L1距离构建检测统计量，并给出有限样本下的错误报警保证和检测条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种在有限样本下具有理论保证的变化检测方法，用于检测一维噪声动力系统中平稳行为（不变密度/平稳律）的变化，而非所有转移动态变化。

Method: 对区间值过程观测进行状态空间划分；估计分区元素间经验转移矩阵；施加小Doeblin型正则化确保唯一平稳分布；从参考段计算基线经验平稳分布，对滑动窗口计算当前经验平稳分布，以两者L1距离作为检测得分；推导有限样本误差界（分解为采样误差、正则化偏差、划分近似误差和噪声偏差），进而得到单窗口误报保证和充分检测条件。

Result: 给出了经验平稳密度的有限样本界，将误差分解为采样误差、正则化偏差、划分近似误差和噪声偏差四个分量；获得单窗口误报保证；当不变密度变化超过估计误差时，统计量能够成功检测。

Conclusion: 所提方法在合成噪声beta映射变点实验中得到了验证，能够有效地在有限样本下检测平稳行为变化。

Abstract: We study finite-sample change detection for one-dimensional noisy dynamical systems using partition-based empirical approximations of stationary behaviour. Given observations from an interval-valued process, we partition the state space, estimate a finite transition matrix from observed transitions between partition elements, and apply a small Doeblin-type regularisation to ensure a unique stationary distribution.
  From an initial reference segment, we compute a baseline empirical stationary distribution \(\widehatπ_{0,ρ}\). For each later sliding window, we compute \(\widehatπ_{t,ρ}\) and define the score \[ S_t=\|\widehatπ_{t,ρ}-\widehatπ_{0,ρ}\|_1. \] Large values of \(S_t\) indicate a change in stationary behaviour relative to the baseline. The statistic detects changes in invariant density or stationary law, but not all possible changes in transition dynamics.
  Under explicit assumptions on empirical transition concentration, finite-state stationary distribution stability, partition approximation, regularisation bias, and noise stability, we derive a finite-sample bound for the empirical stationary density. The bound separates sampling error, regularisation bias, partition approximation error, and noise bias. We then obtain a single-window false-alarm guarantee and a sufficient detection condition when the invariant density changes by more than the estimation error. We illustrate the method on synthetic noisy beta-map change-point experiments.

</details>


### [157] [The Effect of Training Task Diversity on In-Context Learning through the Lens of Low-Dimensional Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06814)
*Soo Min Kwon,Alec S. Xu,Can Yaras,Dogyoon Song,Laura Balzano,Qing Qu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最小分析模型，将训练任务向量建模为低秩高斯混合，从理论上解释了训练任务多样性（定义为协方差矩阵子空间间非重叠列数）如何改善线性注意力ICL的泛化能力和优化轨迹，包括缩短ICL平台期和实现分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL研究对"任务多样性"存在两种定义——训练任务向量数量 vs. 函数类数量。基于后者的许多实证观察缺乏理论解释。本文旨在构建一个可解析的框架，从训练数据的统计性质出发，统一解释这些现象。

Method: 将训练任务向量建模为低秩高斯的混合分布，以协方差矩阵参数化子空间之间的非重叠列数来定义任务多样性。在此模型下分析线性注意力Transformer的ICL学习动态和泛化行为，并推导出多样性如何影响优化轨迹和泛化误差的理论结果。

Result: 理论证明：(i) 任务多样性可缩短ICL训练中的平台期；(ii) 多样性使ICL实现分布外泛化。进一步通过实验将结果扩展至非线性Transformer和非线性函数类，验证了理论框架的普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可操作的统一框架，将任务多样性与ICL的学习动态和泛化能力联系起来，为理解ICL的涌现机制奠定了理论基础，并连接了此前分散的实证观察。

Abstract: The transformer's emergent ability to perform in-context learning (ICL) has sparked a wide range of studies designed to understand its underlying mechanisms. Existing works often study how training task diversity, defined either as the number of ICL training task vectors or as the number of function classes from which the task vectors are drawn, shapes both the learning dynamics and generalization capabilities of ICL. While both definitions have uncovered many interesting phenomena, many observations under the latter definition remain theoretically unexplained. This paper presents a minimal analytical model under which these phenomena provably emerge from the properties of the training data. By modeling the training task vectors as a mixture of low-rank Gaussians, we show how training task diversity, defined by the number of non-overlapping columns between subspaces that parameterize the covariance matrices, improves both the generalization and optimization trajectory of ICL with linear attention. In particular, we show that our model can explain (i) why training with task diversity shortens the ICL plateau and (ii) why ICL appears to achieve out-of-distribution generalization. We conclude by empirically demonstrating how our results extend to nonlinear transformers and nonlinear function classes. Overall, our work presents a tractable framework to unify existing observations.

</details>


### [158] [Stability beyond Bounded Differences: Sharp Generalization Bounds under Finite $L_p$ Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06855)
*Qianqian Lei,Soham Bonnerjee,Yuefeng Han,Wei Biao Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需有限$L_p$矩条件的稳定性分析框架，推导了$L_p$约束下的尖锐集中不等式（推广McDiarmid有界差分技术），并据此为经验风险最小化、转导回归和元学习等场景建立了高概率泛化界，证明在无界/重尾损失下$L_p$稳定性仍足以保证鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于算法稳定性的高概率泛化保证通常依赖一致有界性或亚高斯/亚Weibull尾假设，这对于现代机器学习中常见的重尾或无界损失而言过于严苛。因此需要一种在更弱条件下仍能成立的理论框架。

Method: 作者首先建立了$L_p$约束下独立随机变量函数的尖锐集中不等式，将McDiarmid有界差分技术推广到经典有界情形之外；然后利用这一工具，为多种学习范式（经验风险最小化、转导回归、元学习）推导了基于$L_p$稳定性的高概率泛化上界。

Result: 得到了仅需有限$L_p$矩条件的尖锐高概率泛化界，证明即使一致有界性不成立，只要算法具备$L_p$稳定性，就能实现鲁棒泛化。

Conclusion: 该框架显著弱化了稳定性文献中的标准假设，将算法稳定性的泛化理论拓展到重尾和无界损失等更广泛的现代学习场景，为后续研究提供了更普适的理论工具。

Abstract: While algorithmic stability is a central tool for understanding generalization of learning algorithms, existing high-probability guarantees typically rely on uniform boundedness or sub-Gaussian/sub-Weibull tail assumptions, which can be overly restrictive for modern settings with heavy-tailed or unbounded losses. We develop a stability-based framework that requires only a finite $L_p$ moment condition. Our first contribution is sharp concentration inequalities for functions of independent random variables under $L_p$ constraints, extending McDiarmid's bounded-differences techniques beyond the classical regime. Leveraging these results, we derive sharp high-probability generalization bounds across a range of learning paradigms, including empirical risk minimization, transductive regression, and meta-learning. These guarantees show that $L_p$ stability suffices for robust generalization even when boundedness fails, substantially weakening the standard assumptions in the stability literature.

</details>


### [159] [Deep Single-Index Fréchet Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06957)
*Muqing Cui,Yidong Zhou,Su I Iao,Hans-Georg Müller*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DeSI 是一种半参数框架，它结合深度神经网络估计单指数方向，并在该一维指数上进行 Fréchet 回归，用于预测概率分布、网络、对称正定矩阵等度量空间值输出，既缓解维度灾难又保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据分析中，输出常位于非欧几里得空间（如概率分布、网络、对称正定矩阵），且输入往往为高维。传统深度神经网络缺乏可解释性，而直接在度量空间进行非参数回归面临维度灾难。因此需要一种既能处理高维输入、又能保持可解释性并避免维度灾难的度量空间回归方法。

Method: 提出 DeSI（Deep Single-Index Fréchet Regression）框架：假设条件 Fréchet 均值具有单指数结构，利用深度神经网络估计一个可解释的指数方向（量化各输入的相对重要性），然后沿该一维指数在目标度量空间中执行 Fréchet 回归。该结构将高维输入压缩为单一指数，从而缓解维度灾难。

Result: 理论上建立了 DeSI 的一致逼近和收敛速率保证。在概率分布、网络、对称正定矩阵的模拟实验中展示了强预测性能，并在新泽西州情绪成分数据的实际应用中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: DeSI 成功地将深度学习的表达能力与单指数模型的可解释性相结合，为度量空间值输出的回归问题提供了一种兼具理论保证和实用价值的解决方案，在多个复杂数据类型上均表现优异。

Abstract: Predicting outputs that are located in non-Euclidean spaces, such as probability distributions, networks, and symmetric positive-definite matrices, is becoming increasingly important in modern data analysis, particularly when inputs are high-dimensional. We propose DeSI (Deep Single-Index Fréchet Regression), a semiparametric framework for regression with metric space-valued outputs and multivariate inputs that assumes a single-index structure for the conditional Fréchet mean. DeSI estimates an interpretable index direction, which quantifies the relative importance of inputs, using a deep neural network, and performs Fréchet regression along the resulting one-dimensional index in the target metric space. This structure mitigates the curse of dimensionality while retaining interpretability, which stands in contrast to standard deep neural networks. We establish theoretical guarantees for DeSI, including uniform approximation and convergence rates, and demonstrate its strong predictive performance through simulations on distributions, networks, and symmetric positive-definite matrices, as well as an application to compositional mood data from New Jersey.

</details>


### [160] [Automatic, Debiased, and Invariant Counterfactual Generation under General Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07399)
*Raphael C Kim,Jingsen Zhu,Ramin Zabih,Michele Santacatterina*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ADIGen 是一个面向通用干预的反事实生成框架，通过 Riesz 回归、因果不变性和正交统计学习，实现自动去偏且环境不变的估计，并提供双重稳健保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实生成模型存在估计不稳定、跨环境泛化差、以及干扰模型误设导致偏差等问题，亟需一个在通用干预下具有稳健性和不变性的生成框架。

Method: ADIGen 框架整合三大技术：(1) Riesz 回归，避免不稳定的密度比估计；(2) 因果不变性，提升分布偏移下的泛化能力；(3) 正交统计学习，获得对抗干扰模型误设的双重稳健保证。

Result: 论文提供了超额风险界（excess-risk bounds），证明 ADIGen 在通用干预下能控制反事实风险，并且具有积式偏差干扰余项（product-bias nuisance remainder）以及跨环境的不变风险界。

Conclusion: ADIGen 为复杂干预下的反事实生成提供了一种兼具理论保证和实际稳健性的解决方案，在高维干预和分布偏移场景中具有优势。

Abstract: Generative models for counterfactual outcomes have great potential to support decision-making under complex interventions, but existing approaches are limited by unstable estimation, poor generalization across environments, and bias from nuisance model misspecification. We introduce ADIGen, a framework for automatic, debiased, and invariant counterfactual generation under general interventions, including high-dimensional interventions and outcomes. ADIGen combines Riesz regression to avoid unstable density-ratio estimation, causal invariance to improve generalization under distribution shift, and orthogonal statistical learning to obtain doubly robust guarantees against nuisance model misspecification. We provide excess-risk bounds showing that ADIGen controls counterfactual risk under general interventions, with a product-bias nuisance remainder and an invariant risk bound across environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Elmes*: Automated Construction of Fine-Grained Evaluation Rubrics for Large Language Models in Long-Tail Educational Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06546)
*Tao Liu,Ye Lu,Ruohua Zhang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Elmes*框架，通过多智能体引擎与自演化模块构建细粒度教学场景评估标准，构建Edu-330数据集(330场景/1000+二级指标)，发现教育能力是多维的，LLM裁判与人类排名一致但存在自偏好偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM教育评测基准要么强调通用领域正确性，要么依赖难以规模化覆盖长尾教学场景的人工评分标准，缺乏对模型"如何教"的细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 提出Elmes*端到端框架：(1) 声明式多智能体引擎实现教师-学生-裁判三方交互；(2) SceneGen自演化模块从专家定义的教学维度联合优化评估标准与测试数据；(3) 构建Edu-330数据集，覆盖11学科、3学段、10任务类型，含1000+二级指标；(4) 通过专家标注的少样本锚定、推理强制与贪婪解码等消融实验验证设计选择。

Result: (1) 教育能力是多维的：顶级LLM在创造力和价值观整合上差异显著；(2) 知识强模型在苏格拉底式脚手架教学上表现不佳；(3) 教育专用模型InnoSpark在人工评估中平均分最高；(4) LLM裁判保持与人类一致的排名且评分方差更小，但存在自偏好偏差；(5) 专家评分的少样本锚定提升了人-LLM对齐度，推理强制与贪婪解码的效果因模型而异。

Conclusion: Elmes*为教育场景LLM评估提供了可规模化的诊断基础设施，揭示了教育能力的多维本质，建议评估需超越知识正确性，关注教学互动质量。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for education requires measuring how models teach, not only what they know. Existing benchmarks emphasize domain-general correctness or depend on manually designed rubrics that scale poorly to long-tail pedagogical scenarios. We introduce Elmes*, an end-to-end framework for constructing, refining, and applying fine-grained scenario-specific rubrics. Elmes* combines a declarative multi-agent engine for teacher--student--judge interactions with SceneGen, a self-evolving module that co-optimizes evaluation criteria and test data from expert-defined pedagogical dimensions. Using Elmes*, we build Edu-330, covering 330 scenarios across 11 subjects, 3 grade bands, and 10 task types, with over 1{,}000 second-level indicators. Experiments on Edu-330 and four expert-authored gold-standard scenarios show that educational capability is multidimensional: top-tier LLMs differ mainly in creativity and values integration, knowledge-strong models may fail at Socratic scaffolding, and the education-specialized InnoSpark achieves the best human-evaluated average score. LLM judges preserve human-comparable rankings with much lower scoring variance, but exhibit judge-specific biases such as self-preference. Ablations show that expert-scored few-shot anchoring improves human--LLM alignment, while reasoning enforcement and greedy decoding are model-dependent. Elmes* thus provides scalable diagnostic infrastructure for pedagogically grounded LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [162] [FAIR-Calib: Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration for Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06547)
*Haoyu Huang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Boyu Liu,Guodong Guo,Zhongqian Fu,Hang Zhou,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中因迭代精炼机制导致的"稳定性滞后"问题——早期决策在提交后仍脆弱，PTQ误差会轻易翻转这些边界决策并被永久锁定放大。为此提出FAIR-Calib双阶段量化框架：第一阶段利用全精度教师估计位置先验，第二阶段通过重加权隐状态MSE进行逐层校准，优先保护脆弱的边界状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）以迭代方式精炼token但一旦提交即不可逆，造成"稳定性滞后"（stability lag）：早期决策在写入后仍处于脆弱状态，而PTQ误差极易在写入前沿（write frontier）翻转这些边界决策，并因不可逆性被永久锁定和放大，严重损害量化后模型性能。

Method: 提出FAIR-Calib，一个包含两阶段的后训练量化框架：Stage I 使用全精度教师模型进行探测，估计一个融合了"前沿命中"（frontier hits）与掩码阶段可靠性（masked-stage reliability）的位置先验（position prior）；Stage II 执行离策略（off-policy）逐层校准，通过最小化重加权隐状态MSE来优先保护脆弱的前沿状态，无需昂贵的端到端扩散展开。此外，从理论上证明了该加权目标可作为输出KL散度的代理。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型上以W4A4量化设置进行评估，FAIR-Calib一致地优于现有最先进的基线方法，显著减少了前沿决策翻转，并有效抑制了提交后不匹配（post-commit mismatches）现象。

Conclusion: FAIR-Calib通过识别并优先保护扩散LLM中脆弱的写入前沿状态，有效克服了稳定性滞后带来的量化鲁棒性挑战，为dLLMs的高效部署提供了理论支撑与实践方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) refine tokens iteratively but commit them irreversibly, leading to a "stability lag" where early decisions remain fragile even after being written. We reveal that Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) error easily flips these borderline decisions at the write frontier, which are then permanently locked in and amplified. To address this, we propose Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration (FAIR-Calib), a two-stage PTQ framework for dLLMs. Stage I probes a full-precision teacher to estimate a position prior that combines frontier hits and masked-stage reliability. Stage II performs off-policy, layer-wise calibration by minimizing a reweighted hidden-state MSE, effectively prioritizing the protection of fragile frontier states without requiring expensive end-to-end diffusion rollouts. We further theoretically justify our weighted objective as a surrogate for output KL divergence. Empirically, FAIR-Calib consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on LLaDA and Dream (W4A4), significantly reducing frontier decision flips and suppressing post-commit mismatches across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification using THz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554)
*Roshni Mahtani,Ilán Carretero,Laura Monroy,Aldo Moreno-Oyervides,Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique,Rocío del Amor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合太赫兹双梳光谱（THz-DCS）与多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN）的聚合物分类方法，在12种聚合物上达到85.2%的分类准确率，优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的聚合物识别对再生塑料的质量与安全至关重要，但传统分选和光谱技术在稳健区分方面存在不足。太赫兹双梳光谱虽具有快速、高分辨率、无损测量的优势，但其光谱信号复杂度高，需要更强大的数据分析方法来充分挖掘其分类潜力。

Method: 作者提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），专为THz-DCS数据设计。该架构包含三个核心组件：(1) 特征门控机制用于信号重校准；(2) 多尺度并行卷积以捕获不同频率模式；(3) 交叉特征注意力与注意力池化层，使模型能够自适应地聚焦于最具信息量的太赫兹频段。实验涵盖12种聚合物，包括纯聚合物、多层膜、商业共混物和生物聚合物。

Result: MSFAN在12类聚合物分类任务上达到85.2%的准确率，一致性地超越了现有最先进模型。模型具备内在的可解释性，能够自动突出对分类贡献最大的太赫兹光谱区域。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了THz-DCS与深度学习技术相结合在聚合物分类中的有效性、可扩展性和可解释性，为再生塑料的高效、规模化分选提供了一种有前景的技术路线。

Abstract: Reliable polymer identification is essential for ensuring the quality and safety of recycled plastics, yet conventional sorting and spectroscopic techniques often struggle to deliver robust discrimination. Terahertz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy (THz-DCS) offers a promising alternative, providing rapid, high-resolution, and non-destructive measurements. In this work, we leverage THz-DCS to classify 12 types of polymers, including pure polymers, multilayer films, commercial blends, and biopolymers. To handle the complexity of these spectral signals, we propose the Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network (MSFAN), a novel deep learning architecture tailored for THz-DCS data. The framework integrates feature gating for signal recalibration and multi-scale parallel convolutions to capture diverse frequency patterns. These features are further refined through cross-feature attention and attention pooling, enabling the model to intrinsically highlight the most informative THz regions. MSFAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, reaching a classification accuracy of 85.2%. This study demonstrates the potential of combining THz-DCS with deep learning techniques for effective, scalable, and interpretable polymer classification.

</details>


### [164] [MacArena: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on an Online macOS Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06560)
*Victor Muryn,Maksym Shamrai,Sofiia Mazepa,Yehor Khodysko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacArena 是一个面向 macOS 的计算机使用代理（CUA）基准测试，包含 421 个手工验证任务，覆盖 50 个应用程序，运行在 Apple Silicon 原生虚拟化框架上。研究发现，现有模型在跨平台 GUI 任务上的表现受任务分布熟悉度影响显著，模型排名在移植任务和 macOS 原生任务之间出现反转，macOS 对当前 GUI 代理构成了更严峻的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 CUA 评测基准（如 OSWorld）主要面向 Linux，macOS 生态严重缺乏覆盖。现有的 macOSWorld 基准仅覆盖少量第一方应用、任务简单，且依赖 x86 虚拟机，与 Apple Silicon 不兼容。研究者认为 macOS 存在 Linux 基准无法捕捉的独特 GUI 挑战，需要专门的原生评测平台来推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建 MacArena 基准，包含三类任务来源：从 OSWorld 精选移植的任务、macOSWorld 内容，以及 49 个全新设计的 macOS 原生任务，总计 421 个手工验证任务，覆盖 50 个应用程序。所有任务运行在 Apple Silicon 的原生 Virtualization 框架上，确保环境保真度。通过对多个前沿模型在移植任务与原生任务上的表现进行对比评估。

Result: 在移植任务（OSWorld ported）上表现强劲的模型，在 macOS 原生任务上性能大幅下降。模型排名在两类任务之间出现反转：某领先模型在 MacArena 原生子集上的性能落后超过 26%，表明模型在现有基准上的高分可能源自对任务分布的熟悉，而非真正的跨平台 GUI 能力。

Conclusion: macOS 对当前 GUI 代理构成了比 Linux 基准所反映的更为困难的环境。MacArena 揭示了仅依赖现有 Linux 基准可能产生误导性结论——模型性能高度依赖于对特定任务分布的熟悉度。该基准为社区提供了更全面、更具挑战性的跨平台 GUI 代理评测手段。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate graphical user interfaces (GUIs) through vision and control primitives, and their capabilities have advanced rapidly, driven in part by standardized online evaluation benchmarks such as OSWorld, which serve both as evaluation tools and as training environments for reinforcement learning. However, macOS remains underserved in this landscape: the only existing benchmark, macOSWorld, covers a narrow slice of first-party applications with simpler tasks, and runs on x86 virtual machines incompatible with Apple Silicon. We introduce MacArena, a benchmark of 421 manually verified tasks spanning 50 applications that combines a curated port of OSWorld tasks, content sourced from macOSWorld, and 49 new macOS-native tasks, all running on Apple's native Virtualization framework on Apple Silicon. We argue that macOS presents distinct GUI challenges beyond what Linux-based benchmarks capture, and our evaluation supports this claim: strong model performance on existing benchmarks can reflect familiarity with task distributions rather than genuine cross-platform GUI competence. Notably, model rankings invert between ported and macOS-native tasks, with a leading model trailing by over 26% on the MacArena subset, suggesting that macOS poses a genuinely harder environment for current GUI agents.

</details>


### [165] [Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression for Low-Data, High-Dimensional Output Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06576)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Eric T. Wolf,Mei Ting Mak,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPLFR 提出将高维输出表示为低维潜在状态（来自高斯过程先验）的线性-高斯解码，并通过解析地边缘化解码权重，将压缩与预测耦合到单一目标中，解决了传统 PCA-GP 管道中基优化目标与预测目标不一致的问题，并成功构建了首个岩石系外行星全球气候模型的空间分辨仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中常需从少量训练样本预测高维输出。多输出高斯过程虽擅长小样本学习，但难以处理高维输出。现有"先压缩后预测"管道（如 PCA-GP）虽能应对高维问题，但其压缩基是为重构而非预测任务优化，导致次优预测性能。

Method: 提出高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）：将每个输出建模为低维潜在状态的线性-高斯解码，其中潜在状态服从高斯过程先验。通过解析地边缘化（积分掉）解码器权重，将降维压缩与高斯过程预测统一在单个优化目标中，从而学习面向预测任务的压缩表示，且能扩展至高维输出。

Result: 基于 GPLFR 构建了首个针对岩石系外行星全球气候模型的空间分辨仿真器（emulator），证明该方法在高维科学仿真器构建中的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: GPLFR 通过将压缩与预测联合优化，克服了 PCA-GP 两阶段方法的根本局限，为高维小样本回归任务提供了一个统一的概率框架，在气候科学等高维仿真领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: In the sciences, regression tasks often require predicting high-dimensional outputs from few training examples. Multi-output Gaussian processes excel in low-data regimes but typically struggle with high-dimensional outputs. Compress-then-predict pipelines such as PCA-GP (principal component analysis plus Gaussian process regression) handle high dimensionality, but rely on bases optimized for reconstruction rather than prediction. To address this gap, we propose a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state drawn from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, we couple compression and prediction in a single objective that scales to high-dimensional outputs. We refer to this model as Gaussian process latent factor regression (GPLFR). We demonstrate GPLFR by building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets.

</details>


### [166] [WAV: Multi-Resolution Block Residual Routing for Deep Decoder-Only Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06564)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAV v1 提出一种轻量级多分辨率残差路由方法，在标准块级残差摘要基础上，引入相位基和分裂基两个方向性细节基，分别刻画注意力与MLP更新对比、以及块内早期与晚期子层动态。在48层TinyStories和Text8语言建模上，该方法以可忽略的参数量代价显著超越Block Attention Residuals基线，表明方向性残差细节对深层Transformer的残差路由扩展至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Block Attention Residuals仅用单一标量块摘要存储块内低频总残差位移，丢失了方向性结构信息，如注意力与MLP更新的不平衡、块内早期与晚期子层动态差异。作者认为这些方向性细节对更深层Transformer的残差路由同样重要。

Method: 提出WAV v1方法：在每个块的标准残差摘要基础上，增加两个方向性细节基——相位基（phase basis，对比注意力与MLP更新）和分裂基（split basis，对比早期与晚期子层更新）。这些基与块摘要一同通过深度softmax混合器进行路由。训练稳定性通过负细节源初始化和detached RMS匹配来保证。

Result: 在字符级TinyStories和Text8语言建模上，WAV v1呈现明显的深度依赖优势：12层时不总有利，24层时具有竞争力，48层时全面超越所有基线。48层下，TinyStories验证损失从Block AttnRes的0.4960降至0.4738，Text8从0.9363降至0.9305，额外参数可忽略。

Conclusion: 方向性残差细节（不仅是块级总和）对深层Transformer中残差路由的扩展至关重要，WAV v1以极小代价实现了显著的深度扩展收益。

Abstract: Residual connections are central to training deep Transformers, but standard PreNorm residual streams aggregate sublayer updates with fixed unit weights. Recent Attention Residuals replace this fixed accumulation with content-dependent depth-wise routing, and Block Attention Residuals make the mechanism efficient by routing over block-level residual summaries. However, a single block summary stores only the low-frequency total residual displacement inside a block, discarding directional structure such as attention-vs-MLP imbalance and early-vs-late block dynamics. We propose WAV v1, a lightweight multi-resolution residual routing method for decoder-only Transformers. Instead of representing each block only by its accumulated residual sum, WAV v1 augments every block with two directional detail bases: a phase basis that contrasts attention and MLP updates, and a split basis that contrasts early and late sublayer updates. These bases are routed together with standard block summaries through the same depth-wise softmax mixer, while negative detail-source initialization and detached RMS matching stabilize training. On character-level TinyStories and Text8 language modeling, WAV v1 shows a clear depth-dependent benefit. Although it is not consistently beneficial at 12 layers, it becomes competitive at 24 layers and outperforms all baselines at 48 layers. At 48 layers, WAV v1 reduces validation loss relative to Block AttnRes from 0.4960 to 0.4738 on TinyStories and from 0.9363 to 0.9305 on Text8, with negligible additional parameters. These results suggest that directional residual details, not only block-level sums, are important for scaling residual routing in deeper Transformers.

</details>


### [167] [TorchKM: A GPU-Oriented Library for Kernel Learning and Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06742)
*Yikai Zhang,Gaoxiang Jia,Jie Ding,Boxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TorchKM 是一个基于 GPU 加速的核方法开源库，支持 SVM、核逻辑回归和核分位数回归，提供类 scikit-learn 接口，通过矩阵运算复用实现全流程加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法库（如 scikit-learn）主要依赖 CPU 计算，在大规模数据上训练和模型选择效率低下。利用 GPU 并行计算和线性代数优化，可以显著加速核方法的训练与超参数搜索全流程。

Method: 基于 PyTorch 实现 GPU 友好的线性代数运算；采用智能矩阵运算复用策略，在训练和模型选择（如交叉验证）过程中避免重复计算核矩阵；设计 scikit-learn 兼容 API 以降低用户迁移成本；通过 PyPI 分发并开源至 GitHub。

Result: 基准测试显示，TorchKM 在保持有竞争力的预测性能的同时，相比标准基线方法实现了显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: TorchKM 是一个兼具高性能和易用性的核方法工具库，能够有效利用 GPU 加速核机器全流程，适合需要高效处理大规模核方法任务的场景。

Abstract: TorchKM is an open-source library for kernel machines, including support vector machines, kernel logistic regression, and kernel quantile regression, with GPU acceleration. The library features a scikit-learn-style API and is designed to exploit GPU-friendly linear algebra, accelerating the full training and model-selection pipeline through intelligent reuse of matrix operations. Benchmarks show competitive predictive performance together with substantial speedups over standard baselines. Code and documentation are available at https://github.com/YikaiZhang95/torchkm, and the package can be easily installed via PyPI.

</details>


### [168] [Are you sure? A Comprehensive and Comprehensible Survey of Uncertainty Quantification in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06567)
*Julia Reuter,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了符号回归(SR)中不确定性量化(UQ)的研究现状，将现有方法归纳为频率学派、贝叶斯方法和模型选择三个方向，并指出该领域仍处于探索不足的阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管符号回归在发现数据潜在数学关系方面取得了进展，但缺乏不确定性量化支持限制了其在现实决策中的应用。UQ能提供模型可靠性信息，帮助避免过拟合并辅助决策，而此前尚无综述专门讨论SR中的UQ问题。

Method: 本文为一篇综述性论文，系统梳理了符号回归中不确定性量化的现有文献，将已有研究方法归纳为三大类别：频率学派方法(frequentist)、贝叶斯方法(Bayesian)和模型选择方法(model selection)，并介绍了UQ的基本概念。

Result: 研究发现符号回归中的不确定性量化研究可分为三个主要方向：频率学派方法、贝叶斯方法和模型选择方法。然而，该领域总体上仍处于探索不足(underexplored)的状态。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化对符号回归的可靠性至关重要，但目前该方向研究仍然不足，亟待更多面向SR的可靠UQ方法研究。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is a class of methods that systematically explore the space of mathematical functions to discover models that accurately capture the underlying relationships in a dataset. Despite recent advances in the field, a lack of support for uncertainty quantification (UQ) limits its adoption in real-world decision processes. In regression analysis, UQ provides important information about the model reliability, which can both help to avoid overfitting by accounting for uncertainty in the data, and provide insights for decision-making. This survey is the first to clearly address this issue, with the objective of introducing essential UQ concepts and reviewing the current literature on UQ in SR, which can be broadly organized into three research directions: frequentist, Bayesian, and model selection. Despite its importance, UQ in SR is still underexplored, which motivates further research into reliable UQ methods for SR.

</details>


### [169] [Constructing VAE Latent Spaces with Prescribed Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07058)
*Jilles S. van Hulst,Jakub M. Tomczak,W. P. M. H. Heemels,Duarte J. Antunes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套数学框架，用于解决变分自编码器（VAE）中高斯先验与数据流形拓扑不匹配的问题。作者为所有允许乘积覆盖空间的流形（如柱面、环面、莫比乌斯带、克莱因瓶、实射影空间）设计了拓扑匹配的先验分布。通过将分布分解为基本因子（圆、区间、直线）上的乘积，得到闭合形式的解耦KL散度，使每个隐因子可独立塑形。对于商流形，解码器接收覆盖空间坐标的群不变特征。实验表明，拓扑感知模型在所有实用的正则化强度下均优于高斯基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE使用高斯先验假设隐空间为欧几里得拓扑，但当真实数据位于非欧拓扑流形上时，会产生拓扑失配，导致重建质量下降和表征不忠实。作者旨在系统性地解决这一拓扑失配问题。

Method: 作者构建了一个针对所有允许乘积覆盖空间流形的统一数学框架。具体方法包括：(1) 将流形表达为基本因子（圆S¹、区间I、直线R）的乘积或此类乘积模有限对称群的商；(2) 在基本因子上定义分解分布，得到乘积拓扑及闭合形式、解耦的KL散度；(3) 为周期、有界和无界支撑设计可重参数化的编码器-先验对，并提供坐标变换使标准神经网络可输出非欧参数且保持平滑梯度；(4) 对商流形，解码器接收覆盖空间坐标的群不变特征，确保等价的点产生相同输出；(5) 通过锚点约束将坐标系相对于数据固定或创建软拓扑孔。

Result: 在合成流形和真实图像数据集（旋转和循环平移MNIST）上的实验表明：(1) 拓扑匹配的先验使KL正则化与数据流形对齐；(2) 拓扑感知模型在所有实际相关的正则化强度下均优于高斯基线VAE。

Conclusion: 所提出的拓扑匹配VAE框架有效解决了非欧数据流形上的先验失配问题，在保持训练可处理性的同时，显著提升了表征质量和重建性能。该框架统一覆盖了广泛的有向和无向非欧流形类别。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) learn low-dimensional latent representations of high-dimensional data. When the data lies on a manifold with non-Euclidean topology, the standard Gaussian prior introduces a topological mismatch that degrades reconstruction quality and prevents faithful representation. We present a constructive mathematical framework that resolves this mismatch for all manifolds that admit a product covering space. These are manifolds expressible as products of elementary factors (circles, intervals, or lines) or as quotients of such products by a finite symmetry group. The class includes cylinders, tori, Möbius strips, Klein bottles, and real projective spaces. Factorized distributions over the elementary factors yield product topologies with closed-form, decoupled KL divergences, so that each latent factor can be shaped independently while keeping training tractable. We catalogue reparametrizable encoder-prior pairs for periodic, bounded, and unbounded supports, and provide coordinate transformations that allow standard neural networks to output non-Euclidean parameters with smooth gradients. For quotient manifolds, the decoder receives group-invariant features of the covering-space coordinates, so that identified points produce identical outputs. Anchor constraints fix the coordinate system relative to the data or create soft topological holes. Experiments on synthetic manifolds and real-image datasets (rotated and cyclically shifted MNIST) confirm that a topology-matched prior aligns KL regularization with the data manifold. The resulting topology-aware models outperform the Gaussian baseline at all practically relevant regularization strengths. The code is available at https://github.com/JvHulst/VAE-Topology.

</details>


### [170] [Generative Models Erode Human Temporal Learning Through Market Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06572)
*Wenjun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出，即使当前亚通用人工智能水平下，生成模型也会通过"价值坍塌"机制对依赖人类时序学习（HTL）的知识与文化生产造成结构性风险——当验证AI输出是否反映真实人类学习的成本超过收益时，市场会倾向于奖励廉价生成而非长期积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示生成式AI对知识生产体系的深层威胁：问题不在于AI输出质量本身，而在于验证来源的经济学机制瓦解后，依赖长期学习积累的生产者将与近乎零成本的AI输出进行不公平的价格竞争。

Method: 定义了"人类时序学习"（HTL）概念，构建了"代价高昂的检验框架"（costly-inspection framework）来形式化价值坍塌的传导路径，并提供了学术出版、法律实践、内容平台和软件安全四个领域的跨域证据来映射验证侵蚀的四个阶段。

Result: 发现价值坍塌遵循四个阶段：验证侵蚀→评估者放弃验证并奖励输出本身→HTL生产者与零成本生成竞争→知识生产激励崩溃。对齐成功反而会加剧问题，因为更好的对齐缩小了人机输出的可观测差距，使来源验证更难。

Conclusion: 对齐成功与价值坍塌风险是正交的——即使单个AI输出持续改善，模型越好反而越加速HTL密集型工作的竞争压力。这构成了一种独立于对齐问题的结构性威胁。

Abstract: We argue that modern generative models create structural risks for knowledge and cultural production at current, sub-AGI capability levels. We define Human Temporal Learning (HTL) as path-dependent knowledge accumulation through sustained engagement with problems over time. Generative outputs increasingly resemble HTL-intensive work in surface features, so verifying whether a given output reflects genuine human learning grows costly relative to its expected benefit. Once verification loses economic justification, evaluators reward outputs regardless of production mode, and producers who invested years of learning compete on price against outputs that cost almost nothing to generate. We call this pathway value collapse and formalize it through a costly-inspection framework. Cross-domain evidence from academic publishing, legal practice, content platforms, and software security maps onto four stages of verification erosion. Alignment success is orthogonal. Better-aligned models narrow observable gaps between human and AI outputs, making source verification harder and intensifying competitive pressure against HTL-intensive work even when individual AI outputs improve.

</details>


### [171] [Skip a Layer or Loop It? Learning Program-of-Layers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06574)
*Ziyue Li,Yang Li,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现预训练LLM的层可以动态打包成模块，对每个输入跳过或循环执行，形成灵活的程序化推理（PoLar）。通过轻量预测网络生成定制执行程序，可在减少执行层数的同时提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM采用固定深度和顺序的前向传播方式，这种"一刀切"策略可能浪费计算资源，且未充分挖掘模型潜在的多样化推理路径。作者旨在揭示并利用LLM中存在的训练无关、灵活的层执行方式，以更少的计算实现更好或同等的推理效果。

Method: 提出PoLar（Program-of-Layers）框架：将预训练层打包为可复用模块，允许对每层进行跳过或循环操作，为每个输入动态生成定制化的层执行程序。实践中，训练一个轻量级PoLar预测网络，学习为每个输入生成最优的执行路径（哪些层跳过、哪些层重复），无需微调原模型参数。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，PoLar相比标准推理和现有动态深度方法均一致提升准确率，且通常执行更少的层数。这些收益在分布外评估下仍然保持。

Conclusion: 固定深度执行仅捕获了LLM潜在推理能力的狭窄子集。PoLar揭示了LLM中存在多种有效的潜在计算路径，动态层编程是一种高效利用预训练模型能力的新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform inference by following a fixed depth and order, non-recurrent execution of all layers. We reveal the wide existence of training-free, flexible, dynamic program-of-layers (PoLar), where pretrained layers can be packed as modules and then skipped or looped to form a customized program for each input. For most inputs, substantially shorter program executions can achieve the same or better accuracy, while incorrect predictions of the original LLM can be corrected by alternative programs with fewer layers. These observations indicate that inference admits multiple valid latent computations beyond the standard forward pass. To efficiently achieve PoLar in practice, we propose a lightweight PoLar prediction network, which learns to generate execution programs that dynamically skip or repeat pretrained layers for each input. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PoLar consistently improves accuracy over standard inference and prior dynamic-depth methods, often while executing fewer layers, and that these gains persist under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results suggest that fixed-depth execution captures only a narrow subset of an LLM's latent reasoning capacity.

</details>


### [172] [Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07382)
*Mathieu Chalvidal,Florentin Coeurdoux,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高维协方差估计的经典收缩方法重新表述为在源分布和目标分布之间参数化随机插值上的经验风险最小化，揭示了调度、流映射/耦合和早停三种降低统计风险的机制，并提出了神经插值估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典收缩估计器缺乏统一的理论框架，其风险降低机制未被充分理解。本文旨在通过随机插值的视角统一现有收缩方法，揭示风险降低的根本机制，并为引入非线性正则化（如特征向量正则化）提供理论支撑。

Method: 将收缩估计重铸为源分布与目标分布之间参数化随机插值上的经验风险最小化。通过分析插值调度、最优传输耦合与流映射、以及回归向量场积分与早停三种机制来控制风险。提出基于神经网络的插值估计器，并推导其二次风险上界。

Result: 该框架恢复了已知收缩估计器作为特例；通过最优传输耦合可降低经验风险；非线性流映射释放了特征基约束，实现特征向量正则化；神经估计器在合成实验中得到验证，并在真实神经影像数据上展示了额外的正则化能力。

Conclusion: 通过随机插值统一了收缩估计，揭示了调度、耦合与早停三种互补的风险降低机制，提出的神经估计器在实践中提供了超越经典方法的正则化能力。

Abstract: We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.

</details>


### [173] [Principles and Practice of Deep Representation Learning: or a Mathematical Theory of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06624)
*San Buchanan,Druv Pai,Peng Wang,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书试图从表示学习的视角"打开黑箱"，通过优化与信息论原理阐释现代神经网络架构的设计，将架构设计从"炼金术"还原为可理解的数学练习，并展示如何据此设计高效、可解释且可控的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模生成模型被视为不透明的黑箱，内部机制难以理解，导致可解释性、可靠性和可控性方面的困难，进而引发炒作与恐惧。作者旨在通过揭示深层网络的内部机制来应对这一根本性问题。

Method: 全书以表示学习为核心视角，借助优化理论与信息论作为分析框架，将神经网络架构的设计原则系统化地分解为本科水平的线性代数与微积分练习，并进一步将其应用于新方法与模型的设计中。

Result: 书中论证了通过上述原理，可以设计出高效、可解释、可控且性能不逊于甚至超越黑箱模型的新型模型，并在第7-8章展示了相关应用成果。

Conclusion: 表示学习是深度模型经验能力的核心因素；通过优化与信息论原理可系统理解并改进深度网络架构，摆脱"炼金术"式的试错模式，从而实现更透明、可靠的深度学习系统。

Abstract: In the current era of deep learning and especially generative models, there is significant investment in training very large generative models. Thus far, such models have been "black boxes" that are difficult to understand in the sense that they have opaque internal mechanisms, leading to difficulties in interpretability, reliability, and control. Naturally, this lack of understanding has led to both hype and fear.
  This book is an attempt to "open the black box" and understand the mechanisms of large deep networks, through the perspective of representation learning, which is a major factor - arguably the single most important one - in the empirical power of deep learning models. A brief outline of this book is as follows. Chapter 1 will summarize the threads that underlie the whole text. Chapters 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6 will explain the design principles of modern neural network architectures through optimization and information theory, reducing the process of architecture development (long having been described as a sort of "alchemy") to undergraduate-level linear algebra and calculus exercises once the underlying principles are introduced. Chapters 7 and 8 will discuss applications of these principles to solve problems in more paradigmatic ways, obtaining new methods and models which are efficient, interpretable, and controllable by design, and yet no less - sometimes even more - powerful than the black-box models they resemble. Chapter 9 will discuss potential future directions for deep learning, the role of representation learning, as well as some open problems.

</details>


### [174] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对光伏发电预测的冷启动问题，提出一种零样本流水线方法：利用电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成历史发电量，使时间序列基础模型（TSFM）能够通过推理时条件化进行预测。在440个光伏电站上评估5种TSFM，结果显示协变量感知模型性能约为基准的1.7-2倍，且对合成历史来源不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏电站在投产初期缺乏现场观测数据，导致标准监督学习预测器无法直接应用。这一冷启动问题在实际部署中普遍存在，亟需一种无需目标站点历史数据的零样本预测方案。

Method: 提出零样本预测流水线：首先利用电站元数据（地理位置、装机容量等）和气象协变量生成合成历史发电量序列，然后将合成序列作为时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的上下文输入，在推理时进行条件化预测。评估了5种TSFM（包括TabPFN-TS、Chronos-2等），在Cold-Start Baseline、Real Feedback和Self-Forecast Feedback三种策略下与经典基线对比。

Result: 协变量感知基础模型性能约为经典基线的1.7-2倍。TabPFN-TS在Real Feedback策略下取得最低误差（MAE 0.514，RMSE 0.721 kWh kWp⁻¹ d⁻¹）；Chronos-2在Self-Forecast Feedback策略下最具鲁棒性。合成历史来源对预测精度影响不显著，表明关键因素是合理的时序上下文本身而非具体生成器的选择。

Conclusion: 零样本合成历史+TSFM推理时条件化的方案有效解决了光伏预测冷启动问题，协变量感知模型显著优于经典方法，且方法具有良好的鲁棒性和通用性。

Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

</details>


### [175] [The Identity Trap in EEG Foundation Models: A Diagnostic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06647)
*Jun-You Lin,Ying Choon Wu,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了EEG基础模型中的"身份陷阱"（Identity Trap）：在受试者互斥交叉验证下，模型高准确率可能源于识别受试者身份而非真正的临床生物标志物。作者提出FMScope——一种基于冻结表征的诊断协议，包含方差分解、受试者轴擦除、1/f非周期性成分消融、逐层标签探测和受试者内方向一致性五项诊断，并在三个预训练模型和四个数据集上验证了该陷阱的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在静息态临床脑电上报告了高准确率，但受试者互斥交叉验证下的高准确率存在歧义：它可能反映真正的临床生物标志物，也可能反映与标签相关的受试者身份特征。在微调前于表征层面诊断这种混淆的来源，对于可靠地评估模型是否学到了临床相关信息至关重要。

Method: 提出FMScope协议：①方差分解——量化冻结表征中受试者方差与标签方差的比值；②受试者轴擦除——通过移除主成分分析中的受试者方向，检验去除身份信息后标签解码是否改善；③1/f非周期性成分消融——移除EEG的幂律背景活动以测试其作为身份载体的作用；④逐层标签探测——在各层训练线性探针以追踪标签信息的演变；⑤受试者内方向一致性——检验同类样本在个体内的表征方向是否一致。实验设计为2×2布局：标签的受试者关系（受试者内变异 vs 受试者间变异）× 是否存在公认的跨受试者EEG标志物。

Result: (i) 身份陷阱具有普遍性：12/12对数据中冻结表征的受试者方差是随机基线的13-89倍，微调后全部12对均进一步上升（+10至+63个百分点）。擦除受试者轴后，标签在受试者内变异的情形下解码性能提升6-12个百分点（主实验），外部队列提升4-27个百分点。(ii) 1/f非周期性成分是受试者身份的重要载体：移除该成分后，LaBraM和CBraMod的受试者探测下降9-19个百分点；REVE则在不依赖可测量的非周期性成分情况下饱和了身份信息。(iii) 微调仅在具备文献共识的跨受试者标志物的条件下才放大标签方差。

Conclusion: 身份陷阱是一种具有生理基础的捷径学习实例：模型偏好的捷径线索具有可测量的生理成分（如1/f非周期性活动），仅靠受试者互斥切分无法排除其干扰。FMScope能够有效区分反映真实生物标志物的性能增益与反映受试者身份的性能增益，为EEG基础模型的可靠评估提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: Objective. EEG foundation models (FMs) report strong accuracy on clinical resting-state EEG. However, high accuracy under subject-disjoint cross-validation remains ambiguous: it can reflect a genuine clinical biomarker, or subject-identity features that correlate with the label. We name this the Identity Trap and ask whether it can be diagnosed at the representation level before fine-tuning.
  Approach. We propose FMScope, a frozen-representation protocol packaging five diagnostics: variance decomposition, subject-axis erasure, aperiodic 1/f ablation, layer-wise label probing, and within-subject direction consistency. We apply it to three pretrained FMs (LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE) across four datasets in a 2x2 layout: subject relation of label x presence of a consensus cross-subject EEG marker.
  Main results. (i) The Identity Trap is universal: frozen subject-variance is 13-89x a random null in 12/12 pairs, rising in all 12 under fine-tuning (+10 to +63 pp). This dominance is a removable linear axis: erasing it improves label decoding where the label varies within subject (+6 to +12 pp in primary cells; +4 to +27 pp across external cohorts). (ii) Aperiodic 1/f is one subject carrier: removing it drops the subject probe by 9-19 pp on LaBraM and CBraMod. REVE saturates subject identity without measurable aperiodic dependence. (iii) Fine-tuning amplifies label-variance only in cells with a literature-established cross-subject marker.
  Significance. The Identity Trap is a physically-grounded instance of shortcut learning: the preferred cue has a measurable physiological component, and subject-disjoint splitting alone cannot rule it out. FMScope separates gains reflecting a biological marker from those reflecting subject identity.

</details>


### [176] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CascadeNet 提出一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，无需预设扩散模型即可恢复隐藏的影响网络。通过构建灵活的转移函数估计器并结合 Neyman 正交去偏，实现 $\sqrt{n}$ 一致性和渐近正态性，支持网络结构的正式推断。在模拟和西班牙 COVID-19 传播实证中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法通常依赖特定扩散模型假设，当模型设定错误时性能严重下降。作者旨在开发一个不依赖特定扩散机制假设、且能提供正式统计推断的网络恢复方法。

Method: CascadeNet 核心思路是用一步转移函数的雅可比矩阵刻画潜在影响结构。方法分两步：(1) 构建转移函数的灵活估计器；(2) 通过 Riesz 表示元（representer）进行 Neyman 正交去偏，使去偏雅可比矩阵达到 $\sqrt{n}$ 一致性和渐近正态性，从而支持对网络结构的假设检验与置信区间构造。

Result: 模拟实验中，CascadeNet 在九种常见数据生成过程下均取得最高的网络恢复准确率。在西班牙 52 个省份 COVID-19 传播的实证中，CascadeNet 恢复的传播网络与真实的跨省流动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络与真实流动无显著关联。

Conclusion: CascadeNet 为动态级联中的网络恢复提供了一种不依赖模型假设、且具备推断能力的通用框架，在模拟和实际数据中均表现优越，尤其当真实扩散机制未知或复杂时具有明显优势。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [177] [Capturing non-Markovian dynamics in non-equilibrium stochastic systems using flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06658)
*Bhargav Sriram Siddani,John B. Bell,Alejandro L. Garcia,Ishan Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式流匹配方法，直接对粒子模拟中的通量概率分布进行建模，以显式捕捉非马尔可夫和非高斯效应，解决了粗粒化SPDE（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）在短时动力学和低密度区域预测不准确的问题，并在Kramers首通时间问题上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的粗粒化随机偏微分方程（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）在描述随机粒子系统的流体动力学时，存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 无法准确捕捉由非马尔可夫效应主导的短时系统动力学；(2) 在低粒子密度区域，分布高度非高斯，SPDE模型失效。因此需要一种能显式建模非马尔可夫和非高斯效应的新方法。

Method: 开发了一种生成式流匹配（generative flow matching）方法，直接从粒子模拟数据中学习并建模通量（fluxes）的概率分布，从而显式地融入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。该方法不依赖于传统的粗粒化SPDE框架，而是以数据驱动的方式捕捉系统的真实统计特性。

Result: 以非相互作用布朗粒子的Kramers首通时间问题为验证案例：(1) 模型准确捕捉了系统的短时行为；(2) 与马尔可夫基线模型（正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）相比，在数密度统计矩的预测上表现更优。

Conclusion: 生成式流匹配方法能够有效克服传统粗粒化SPDE在短时尺度和低密度条件下的局限性，为随机粒子系统的流体动力学建模提供了一种更精确的数据驱动替代方案。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic models of stochastic particle systems represented by coarse-grained stochastic partial differential equations (SPDE), such as the regularized Dean-Kawasaki (DK) equation, do not accurately capture the short-time system dynamics that is dominated by non-Markovian effects, and low particle density regimes where the distributions are highly non-Gaussian. We develop a generative flow matching method that directly models the probability distribution of fluxes from particle simulations that explicitly incorporates non-Markovian and non-Gaussian effects. As a demonstration, we use this method to simulate the Kramers first passage time problem for a system of non-interacting Brownian particles. We show the model accurately captures the short-time behavior and provides better predictions of the statistical moments of the number density when compared against the solution of the Markovian baseline, regularized DK equation.

</details>


### [178] [Explainable Runtime Dependency Tracking for AI-RAN Conflict Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06663)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于布尔矩阵分解的轻量级监测原语，通过滑动窗口推理程序跟踪参数-KPI依赖关系，用于AI-RAN中实时冲突检测与依赖结构变化识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来AI-RAN中多个学习型xApp/rApp共享参数和KPI，仅知道部署了哪些应用不足以进行冲突监测；系统还需判断运行时诊断所依赖的参数-KPI关系在当前运行状态下是否依然有效。因此需要一种轻量级、可解释的依赖跟踪机制。

Method: 采用布尔矩阵表示活动依赖关系，利用布尔矩阵乘法检查近期参数活动事件与KPI响应事件是否与当前依赖估计一致。提出滑动窗口推理程序：当近期观测与当前估计一致时复用原估计，检测到结构变化时重新计算依赖矩阵。

Result: 在受控布尔事件流上的实验表明，该方法在依赖关系变化和布尔观测噪声条件下，能够高效且准确地跟踪依赖结构。

Conclusion: 该跟踪器定位为冲突诊断和慢循环模型刷新的可解释信号，而非自主缓解机制，为AI-RAN的运行时依赖监测提供了有效的轻量级方案。

Abstract: Future AI-integrated Radio Access Networks (AI-RAN) will combine open programmability with learning-enabled xApps, rApps, and control functions that act on shared parameters and key performance indicators (KPIs). For conflict monitoring, it is not enough to know which applications are deployed; the system must also know whether the parameter--KPI dependencies assumed by runtime diagnosis remain valid under the current operating regime. This paper studies a lightweight monitoring primitive for that purpose: tracking an interpretable dependency representation from streaming telemetry events.
  We represent active dependencies by a Boolean matrix and use Boolean matrix multiplication to check whether recent parameter-activity and KPI-response events are consistent with the current estimate. We propose a sliding-window inference procedure that reuses the estimate when it remains consistent and recomputes it when recent observations indicate structural change. The tracker is intended as an explainable signal for conflict diagnosis and slow-loop model refresh, not as an autonomous mitigation mechanism. Experiments on controlled Boolean event streams show efficient and accurate tracking under dependency changes and Boolean observation noise.

</details>


### [179] [Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07379)
*Thanawat Lodkaew,Johannes Ackermann,Soichiro Nishimori,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Masashi Sugiyama,Takashi Ishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CapCode框架，通过构造带有随机化测试且将非作弊最佳性能设上限的编码数据集，来检测模型评估中的作弊行为；同时提出CapReward奖励设计以抑制超越上限的优化，从而提升模型对任务规范的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体评估与训练中，模型可通过利用捷径（shortcuts）而非真正解决任务来获得高分，导致评估分数不可靠，这种欺骗性表现成为日益严重的失效模式。需要一种能检测并抑制此类作弊行为的方法。

Method: 提出CapCode框架：构建编码数据集时引入随机化测试，并将非作弊情况下可达到的最优性能人为设上限（cap）。若模型得分显著超过该上限，则构成作弊证据。进一步提出CapReward奖励机制，基于CapCode原理设计，抑制模型优化超越性能上限的行为。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明：CapCode能够有效检测作弊行为，同时保持模型性能排名的保真度；CapReward则成功减少作弊行为，使模型更好地遵循预期的任务规范。

Conclusion: CapCode提供了一种可解释性更强的评估范式——通过性能上限使作弊行为可被检测，CapReward则从训练端抑制了捷径利用，二者共同提升了智能体评估的可靠性和模型行为的合规性。

Abstract: A growing failure mode in agent evaluation and training is that models can achieve high evaluation scores by exploiting shortcuts instead of solving the intended task, producing deceptive performance. This makes evaluation scores unreliable as measures of true task-solving ability. We propose CapCode, a framework for constructing coding datasets with randomized tests whose best achievable non-cheating performance is deliberately capped below one. This capped-performance design gives evaluation scores a clearer interpretation: scores substantially above the cap are implausible and therefore provide evidence of cheating. To prevent cheating, we propose CapReward, a reward design based on the CapCode principle to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets show that CapCode detects cheating while preserving performance ranking of models, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, yielding models that better follow the intended task specification.

</details>


### [180] [Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06673)
*Ujjwal Bhatta,Utsabi Dangol,Sumaly Bajracharya,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ULPS 框架将校准后的大语言模型集成到 RL 训练循环中，利用 A* 合成的符号轨迹微调 BERT 模型，通过 MC Dropout 估计的认知不确定性来调制引导强度，并结合熵基混合机制在 PPO 策略与 LLM 建议之间自适应平衡，在 MiniGridUnlockPickup 基准上取得了成功率、奖励效率和样本复杂度方面的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在稀疏奖励和异构任务序列场景中面临收敛缓慢、泛化能力弱和探索低效等持久性挑战。现有方法缺乏一种将符号规划先验与不确定性感知控制相结合的方式，导致无法在保持适应性的同时有效利用结构化先验知识。

Method: ULPS 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用 A* 搜索算法生成最优符号轨迹，作为监督信号微调 BERT 语言模型，使其具备任务相关的行为先验；(2) 训练过程中，通过 MC Dropout 估计认知不确定性，用于调制 LLM 建议的置信度；(3) 设计熵基自适应混合机制，根据当前策略熵动态平衡 LLM 引导与 PPO 学习策略的权重，确保在不确定性高时依赖先验、不确定性低时保持自主探索能力。

Result: 在 MiniGridUnlockPickup 基准上，ULPS 相比无引导、未校准及标准 RL 基线，微调后执行准确率提升超过 9%，所需环境交互次数更少，奖励 AUC 更高，在成功率、奖励效率和样本复杂度三个维度上均取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 融合符号 A* 轨迹、预训练语言先验与不确定性感知控制的 ULPS 框架，为稀疏奖励域中的多任务强化学习提供了一种原则性强且有效的方法，并具备向部分可观测和多智能体场景扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Sparse rewards and heterogeneous task sequences remain persistent challenges in Reinforcement Learning (RL), often resulting in slow convergence, weak generalization, and inefficient exploration. We propose Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping (ULPS), a novel framework that integrates a calibrated Large Language Model (LLM) into the RL training loop to provide structured, uncertainty-modulated behavioral guidance. ULPS employs an A*-based oracle to synthesize optimal symbolic trajectories, which are used to fine-tune a BERT-based language model. During training, this model supplies action suggestions whose influence is conditioned on epistemic uncertainty estimated via Monte Carlo (MC) dropout. An entropy-based blending mechanism adaptively balances LLM guidance and the learned policy (via Proximal Policy Optimization, PPO), allowing the agent to prioritize reliable priors while preserving adaptability. We evaluate ULPS on the MiniGridUnlockPickup benchmark and observe consistent improvements in success rate, reward efficiency, and sample complexity over unguided, uncalibrated, and standard RL baselines. ULPS achieves more than 9% improvement in execution accuracy after fine-tuning, requires fewer environment interactions, and yields higher reward AUC. Our results demonstrate that integrating symbolic A* trajectories, pretrained language priors, and uncertainty-aware control offers a principled and effective approach to multi-task reinforcement learning in sparse-reward domains, with potential extensibility to partially observable and multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [181] [Spatiotemporal Imputation with Graph-Informed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06682)
*Zepeng Zhang,Aref Einizade,Jhony H. Giraldo,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG
