TL;DR: GiFlow 是一种基于图的流匹配框架，用于时空数据插补。它用从可观测信号构建的图信息先验替代传统高斯先验，简化生成轨迹；并通过混合向量场模型联合建模时空依赖，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空系统中的缺失数据问题普遍存在（如空气质量监测、城市交通管理）。传统方法（RNN、GNN）依赖迭代传播会累积误差，而扩散方法虽缓解了误差传播但需迭代采样且使用与问题无关的高斯先验，限制了效率和效果。因此需要一种既能避免误差累积、又能高效且针对性地利用时空结构信息的方法。

Method: 提出 GiFlow（Graph-Informed Flow Matching）框架：(1) 用时空滤波处理可观测信号，构建图信息先验替代高斯先验，使源分布与目标分布更对齐，简化生成轨迹；(2) 流场由混合向量场模型参数化，融合空间注意力、时间注意力和时空传播三种机制，联合建模空间与时间依赖关系。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上进行广泛实验，GiFlow 在时空插补任务上超越了现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GiFlow 通过图信息先验和混合向量场建模，有效解决了传统方法误差累积和扩散方法效率低下的问题，在时空插补任务中取得了最优性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Missing data is a common challenge in spatiotemporal systems, arising in applications such as air quality monitoring and urban traffic management. Traditional machine learning approaches, like recurrent and graph neural networks, rely on iterative propagation, which tends to accumulate errors over time and space. Recent diffusion-based methods mitigate error propagation but require iterative sampling and often depend on problem-agnostic Gaussian priors, limiting both efficiency and effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose GiFlow, a Graph-Informed Flow Matching framework for spatiotemporal imputation. GiFlow replaces the typical Gaussian prior with a graph-informed prior constructed via spatiotemporal filtering of observable signals, which better aligns the source distribution to the target and thereby simplifies the generation trajectory. The flow field is parameterized by a hybrid vector field model that integrates spatial attention, temporal attention, and spatiotemporal propagation, enabling joint modeling of spatial and temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed GiFlow outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in spatiotemporal imputation. The code is available at https://github.com/zepengzhang/GiFlow.

</details>


### [182] [Towards Serverless Semi-Decentralized Federated Learning with Heterogeneous Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06687)
*Su Wang,Mung Chiang,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SSD-FL提出一种无服务器半去中心化联邦学习框架，通过轻量级D2D初始化阶段进行聚类形成，利用"有效损失函数"和Cheeger不等式的共识间隙来迭代优化聚类，在多种网络图、数据集和优化器设置下均提升了收敛速度与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类研究集中在中心化联邦学习中，完全去中心化环境下的聚类形成尚未被探索。去中心化环境中，网络图结构、本地数据异构性和不同ML优化器之间存在复杂耦合，使得聚类优化极具挑战性。

Method: 提出SSD-FL方法：1）通过轻量级一次性D2D初始化阶段完成聚类形成，后续训练完全无服务器；2）将全局轮次分割为簇内和簇间两种机制，设计融合设备特定ML优化器与网络图正则化的"有效损失函数"来保证全局收敛与共识；3）利用Cheeger不等式的共识间隙构建迭代聚类算法，并基于推导的收敛与共识界进行优化，引入评分度量量化设备间的数据和优化器异构性。

Result: 在三种去中心化FL方法类别上的实验验证表明，SSD-FL在不同网络图、数据集和本地优化器设置下均提升了收敛速度和通信效率。

Conclusion: SSD-FL成功将聚类机制拓展到完全去中心化联邦学习中，在无持久服务器基础设施的条件下实现了更优的收敛与通信性能，为异构优化器环境下的去中心化FL提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate cluster formation, involving the number and composition of clusters, in decentralized federated learning (FL) with heterogeneous machine learning (ML) optimizers. While clustering in centralized FL has enabled scalability and resource savings, its value and development in fully decentralized environments have yet to be explored. Optimizing cluster formation in such environments is challenging, especially due to the complex coupling between network graph structures, local data heterogeneity, and different local ML model optimizers. To address these challenges, we propose serverless semi-decentralized FL (SSD-FL), a methodology requiring no persistent server infrastructure. In SSD-FL, cluster formation occurs via a lightweight, one-time device-to-device (D2D) initialization phase, after which actual ML model training (alongside consensus and convergence processes) is fully serverless. Functionally, SSD-FL segments global rounds into intra-cluster and inter-cluster regimes, ensuring global convergence and consensus through novel "effective loss functions" that integrate device-specific ML optimizers with network graph-based regularization. Next, SSD-FL leverages the consensus gap via the Cheeger inequality to develop an iterative clustering algorithm evaluated against our derived convergence and consensus bounds, which incorporate a unique scoring metric to quantify data and optimizer heterogeneity across devices. Finally, experimental evaluation against three categories of decentralized FL methodologies validate that SSD-FL improves both convergence speeds and communication efficiency across various network graphs, datasets, and local optimizer regimes.

</details>


### [183] [The Geography of Algorithmic Judgment: LLM Intermediaries, Place Identity, and Racial Steering in Housing Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06694)
*Hana Samad,Trung Lam,Christoph Mügge-Durum,Michael Akinwumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对七个大型语言模型在住房搜索中的种族导向行为进行了行为审计，发现在四个美国城市中，语言模型会根据用户种族身份和偏好表述产生不同的位置推荐，这种“导向”行为是模型解释许可、用户身份与空间逻辑交互下的涌现结果，而非静态属性，且不同城市间结果不可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正迅速通过对话界面整合房源平台，在住房搜索中充当中介角色。这一趋势引发了公平住房方面的担忧。先前研究已发现LLM中存在种族导向（racial steering）问题，但对其产生的机制——特别是用户偏好表述、种族身份与模型内部空间逻辑的交互——尚缺乏深入理解。本研究旨在系统性地审计LLM在不同城市、不同提示条件下的导向行为，揭示其涌现性本质。

Method: 采用行为审计方法，测试了七个LLM（含开源权重模型和闭源模型），覆盖四个美国城市。设计了三种迭代提示条件：基线条件、加入生活方式偏好、进一步细化偏好，模拟公平住房配对测试方法论。通过变换用户的种族身份信号与偏好表述，系统性地评估模型给出的位置推荐是否存在系统性差异。

Result: 研究发现：（1）导向是模型解释许可（interpretive license）的涌现行为，而非静态属性；（2）导向源于用户身份、偏好表述和模型内化的城市空间逻辑三者之间的交互；（3）导向行为在方向和程度上均不统一，偏好条件测试往往相对于基线增加或重新配置了表现出导向行为的模型数量；（4）LLM可能对相同住房偏好因用户种族身份不同而做出不同解读；（5）城市不是中性的评估单元，一个本地市场的结果不能泛化到另一个市场。

Conclusion: 在住房领域部署LLM工具时，必须具备本地和领域专业知识，以确保公平住房的法律和制度承诺不会因AI工具的中介作用而被削弱。不同城市间的结果无法简单泛化，需要因地制宜的评估和监管策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly assuming an intermediary role in housing search through the integration of listing platforms within conversational interfaces, mediating access to information, search, and recommendations within urban settings. We expand on prior work on racial steering in LLMs by conducting a behavioral audit of seven open-weight and closed-source LLMs across four U.S. cities, testing location recommendations across three iterative prompting conditions that progressively add lifestyle preference context and reflect fair housing paired-testing methodologies. We find that steering is an emergent behavior of the model's interpretive license rather than primarily a static property. Steering results from the interaction of a user's identity, preference articulation, and the spatial logic that a model has internalized about learned representations of place, preference, and opportunity in a given city, and how different types of users relate to it. While steering was present, it was not uniform in direction or magnitude across evaluated conditions. Preference-conditioned testing often increased or reconfigured the number of models that exhibited steering behaviors relative to baseline conditions, suggesting that LLMs may interpret what the same housing preference means differently depending on the racial identity of the user. Our findings also demonstrate that the city is not a neutral testing unit for LLM evaluation in place-based sectors, and results from one local market cannot be assumed to generalize to another. Local and domain expertise will be required in the housing sector to ensure that legal and institutional commitments to fair housing are not undermined while adopting AI tools that mediate spatial access.

</details>


### [184] [RECAP: Regression Evaluation for Continual Adaptation of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06698)
*Harsh Deshpande,Kushal Chawla,Sangwoo Cho,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAP是一个用于评估提示优化方法在主动适应范式下持续学习能力的基准，发现现有方法无法在仅给定约束规范、未见测试数据的情况下有效泛化，性能无明显提升且延迟更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的智能体系统面临持续演化的约束（如合规阈值变更、政策更新），必须从下一次交互起立即遵从，几乎没有容错空间。然而当前基准假设静态约束集或带评估反馈的被动协议，缺失对这种主动适应设置的评估。

Method: 提出RECAP基准，采用严格的"先适应后测试"（adapt-then-test）主动协议：提示优化方法仅接收约束规范，在未见任何测试数据前即需泛化。在约束层面衡量持续学习现象（遗忘、性能倒退、前向迁移），评估6种方法、4个LLM、3种约束演化调度下的表现。

Result: 所有方法在主动范式下均未表现出显著的性能提升，即使付出了更高的推理延迟。这些为离线或被动词协议设计的方法，无法胜任主动适应场景。

Conclusion: 研究强调了设计主动提示适应方法的迫切需求，模型必须在部署中面对不断演化的需求保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Production agentic systems routinely face evolving constraints and must comply from the very next interaction. Scenarios like a tool-call notification changing a compliance threshold or a policy update adding disclosure requirements fit this criteria, having close to no room for errors in production. This proactive adaptation setting is common in deployment, but absent from current benchmarks, which assume either static constraint sets or reactive protocols with evaluation feedback. We introduce RECAP, a benchmark that measures continual-learning phenomena (forgetting, regression, forward transfer) at the constraint level under a strictly proactive adapt-then-test protocol: prompt optimization methods receive only the constraint specification and must generalize before seeing any test data. Evaluating six methods across four LLMs and three schedules with evolving constraints, we find that these methods show no significant improvement in performance, even after incurring a higher latency. These methods, designed for offline or reactive settings, are inadequate for the proactive paradigm. Our work emphasizes the growing need for designing proactive prompt adaptation methods, where the models must remain robust to evolving needs in deployment.

</details>


### [185] [ShallowBench: Benchmarking Generative Drug Design Models on Shallow-Pocket Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06717)
*Saket Reddy,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShallowBench is a curated benchmark of 5,780 shallow-pocket targets from CrossDocked2020, created by comparing Alpha Shape "lid" volume with protein atom voxel volume to isolate low-concavity binding sites. It reveals that current generative models struggle with shallow-pocket targets like KRAS and MYC, highlighting the need for new architectural innovations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI模型在基于结构的药物设计中严重依赖深结合口袋，难以对低可口袋性靶标（如KRAS、MYC等"不可成药"肿瘤靶点）采样有效配体。需要一个专门的基准来系统评估和推动针对浅口袋靶标的生成模型研究。

Method: 从CrossDocked2020数据集中提取并严格筛选出5,780个浅口袋靶标；通过计算Alpha Shape"盖子"体积与底层蛋白质原子体素体积之间的差值，量化口袋凹度（concavity），从而分离出低凹度但具有足够结合表面积的目标。

Result: 在ShallowBench上评估多种SOTA生成模型，结果表明这些模型在低凹度结合界面上预测的结合亲和力显著较弱，验证了浅口袋靶标对现有方法的挑战性。

Conclusion: ShallowBench为生成生物学模型提供了一个严格的基准，揭示了当前模型在处理浅口袋靶标方面的不足，强调了需要新的架构创新或损失函数设计来应对这类挑战性靶标。

Abstract: While generative AI models have demonstrated remarkable success in structure-based drug design, they predominantly rely on deep binding pockets and struggle to sample effective ligands for challenging low-pocketability targets, such as the historically "undruggable" oncology targets KRAS and MYC. To address this gap, we introduce ShallowBench, a strictly curated benchmark of 5,780 shallow-pocket targets extracted from CrossDocked2020. By computing the difference between an Alpha Shape "lid" volume and the underlying protein atom voxel volume, we successfully isolated targets with low concavity while ensuring sufficient surface area for binding. Evaluating various state-of-the-art generative models reveals weaker predicted binding affinity on these low-concavity interfaces. ShallowBench therefore provides a rigorous benchmark for generative biology models and highlights the necessity of new architectural innovations or loss functions capable of navigating these challenging targets.

</details>


### [186] [MSAIC-Net: A Multi-Scale Attention and Imbalance-Aware Contrastive Network for ECG-Based Myocardial Substrate Abnormality Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06718)
*Canyu Lei,Fenglin Zhang,Derek Bivona,Cristiane Singulane,Jonathan Pan,Kenneth Bilchick,Amit R. Patel,Jianxin Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多尺度注意力增强卷积网络（MSAIC-Net），结合不平衡感知的监督对比学习策略，用于从心电图中检测心肌瘢痕和心肌梗死等心肌基质异常，在两个数据集上均优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌基质异常（如心肌瘢痕、心肌梗死）与不良心血管结局相关，ECG虽廉价易得，但基于ECG的检测面临异质性导联表现、高维多导联信号、类别不平衡及深度学习模型可解释性不足等挑战。

Method: (1) MSAIC-Net：并行空洞卷积分支提取多时间感受野特征 + 通道注意力自适应加权导联和特征通道；(2) 不平衡感知监督对比学习：增强同类样本特征紧凑性与异类样本分离性；(3) 导联排列重要性：量化各导联贡献以提升可解释性。

Result: 在UVA心肌瘢痕数据集（小样本）和PTB-XL心梗数据集（大规模公开数据）上评估，MSAIC-Net均优于基线模型，尤其在UVA小样本数据集上改善显著。

Conclusion: MSAIC-Net框架为基于ECG的心肌基质异常检测提供了一种有效且可解释的方法，在小样本场景下优势尤为突出。

Abstract: Myocardial substrate abnormalities, such as myocardial scar and myocardial infarction (MI), are associated with adverse cardiovascular outcomes. Electrocardiography (ECG) provides a low-cost and widely available tool for detecting these abnormalities, but ECG-based detection remains challenging due to heterogeneous lead-dependent manifestations, high-dimensional multi-lead signals, class imbalance, and the limited interpretability of deep learning models. We propose a multi-scale attention-enhanced convolutional network (MSAIC-Net) for ECG-based myocardial substrate abnormality detection. MSAIC-Net employs parallel atrous convolutional branches to extract ECG features across multiple temporal receptive fields. %, enabling the model to capture both local and longer-range temporal patterns. Channel attention is then used to adaptively reweight informative lead-wise and feature-channel representations. To address class imbalance and improve feature separability, we introduce a novel imbalance-aware supervised contrastive learning strategy that encourages samples from the same class to form compact representations while increasing separation between abnormal and normal samples. Lead-wise permutation importance is further incorporated to quantify the contribution of each ECG lead and improve model interpretability. The proposed method was evaluated on two complementary datasets: a low-data institutional cohort from the University of Virginia (UVA) Health System for myocardial scar classification and the large-scale public PTB-XL dataset from PhysioNet for MI identification. Experimental results show that MSAIC-Net outperforms baseline models, with particularly pronounced improvements in the low-data UVA cohort. Overall, the proposed framework provides an effective and interpretable approach for ECG-based detection of myocardial substrate abnormalities.

</details>


### [187] [Flatland: The Adventures of Gradient Descent with Large Step Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06722)
*Leonardo Galli,Curtis Fox,Wiebke Bartolomaeus,Mark Schmidt,Holger Rauhut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的"大步长"定义，仅需梯度局部Lipschitz连续性，并设计了自适应方法使得梯度下降在训练伊始即在稳定性边缘（EoS）运行，揭示了过早进入平坦区域可能损害收敛与泛化，通过自稳定机制可将失败训练转为成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练的损失函数通常不具备全局L-光滑性，在此条件下如何确定保证梯度下降收敛的最大步长是一个长期未解决的理论与实践难题。现有理论缺乏对大步长行为的统一理解。

Method: 作者提出了仅依赖梯度局部Lipschitz（乃至Hölder）连续性的"大步长"统一定义；设计了可证明实现大步长的一阶自适应方法；利用自稳定论证，允许梯度下降进入略尖锐的谷底，并通过EoS机制（步长与Hessian最大特征值之积保持在2以上）进行非单调训练与锐度最小化。

Result: 所提方法从训练开始即在EoS状态下运行，损失非单调下降；步长×锐度始终高于EoS阈值2；锐度可被最小化至全局最小值；发现过早遭遇全局平坦区域会减慢收敛并损害泛化；自稳定策略可将失败训练转为成功训练。

Conclusion: 通过局部光滑性条件下的大步长统一框架，梯度下降可在EoS下稳定训练，且适度避开过早平坦区域、借助自稳定进入略尖锐谷底反而有利于收敛速度与泛化能力。

Abstract: The training of neural networks often entails objective functions that are not globally $L$-smooth. For these functions, it is both theoretically and practically difficult to reply to the question: what is the largest possible step size that ensures the convergence of gradient descent (GD)? We address this longstanding open question in deep learning by providing a unifying definition of "large" step sizes that requires only local Lipschitz (or even Hölder) continuity of the gradient. We design first-order adaptive methods that provably yield large step sizes and show that they operate at the edge of stability (EoS) right from the start of the training. In particular, the loss decreases nonmonotonically and the product between the step size and sharpness, i.e., the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian, stays above the EoS threshold of 2 throughout training. Using our method, we are also able to minimize the sharpness all the way down to its global minimum. Contrary to expectation, we find that encountering globally-flat regions too early in the training may both slow down convergence and jeopardize the generalization ability of the network. Exploiting a self-stabilization argument, we allow GD to enter slightly sharper valleys and turn unsuccessful training runs into very successful ones.

</details>


### [188] [Synthics: Synthetic Physics-like Datasets for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06724)
*Jari Vepsäläinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法的合成回归数据生成方法，从物理方程语料库中采样结构相似的新方程，并通过适用域刻画与输入采样策略确保生成数据真实反映真实观测结构，实验表明生成数据在超参数调优任务中可媲美真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中代表性数据至关重要，但真实样本采集往往难以实现；合成数据生成是可行的替代方案，然而现有方法难以保证生成数据忠实地反映真实数据的结构特征，尤其是物理方程的结构约束。

Method: 利用贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法（Bayesian PCFG）捕捉方程语料库的代数结构，从中采样生成新方程；通过非侵入式探测（non-intrusive probing）为每个方程刻画适用域（applicability domain），恢复变量间约束；输入采样采用从有效域的随机子区间中混合均匀分布与截断正态分布的方式模拟真实实验条件；使用Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验对生成数据进行统计验证。

Result: 生成方程在全部8项研究的结构特征上与Feynman方程语料库匹配，而未平滑的纯概率文法仅匹配2项；在超参数调优的下游任务中，基于合成数据调优的梯度提升回归器在真实数据上平均选出20个配置中的第6佳，与直接在真实数据上调优的效果持平，显著优于随机表达式树（第10名）和噪声（第19名）。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯先验对于在小规模语料库下保持结构保真度至关重要；所提方法生成的合成数据可作为真实数据的有效替代品，尤其适用于数据稀缺场景下的超参数优化等下游任务。

Abstract: Representative data is fundamental in machine learning, as limited data hinders generalisation. Collecting sufficient real-world samples is often infeasible. Synthetic data generation offers a practical solution, but only if the generated data faithfully reflects the structure of real observations. In this paper, a method for generating synthetic regression datasets that structurally resemble physics equations from a given equation corpus is presented. The approach uses a Bayesian Probabilistic Context-Free Grammar to capture the underlying algebraic structure of the corpus, from which novel equations are sampled. To ensure the generated inputs lie within a physically meaningful domain, the applicability domain is characterised for each equation through non-intrusive probing, also recovering inter-variable constraints. Input sampling further mimics realistic experimental conditions by drawing from random sub-ranges of the valid domain with mixed uniform and truncated normal distributions. The generated data is statistically validated against the Feynman equation corpus using Kolmogorov-Smirnov tests. The generated equations match the corpus on all of the eight studied structural features, compared to only two for an unsmoothed purely probabilistic grammar, demonstrating that the Bayesian prior is essential for structural fidelity given the size of the corpus. In a downstream hyperparameter-tuning task, a gradient-boosted regressor tuned on the synthetic data picks, on average, the 6th-best configuration out of 20 on real data, matching the result of tuning on real data itself and substantially outperforming random expression trees (10th) and noise (19th).

</details>


### [189] [Learn to Match: Two-Sided Matching with Temporally Extended Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06744)
*Haijing Zong,Yancheng Liang,Boyang Zhou,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个动态匹配市场的强化学习框架Learn2Match，将双边匹配建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈，包含面试筛选、噪声观测、隐变量演化和匹配终止。实验表明PPO在累计社会福祉和遗憾值方面优于bandit基线，但在信息摩擦损失上仍显不足，揭示了MARL与结构化bandit方法之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的双边匹配市场（如招聘、婚恋）中，偏好信息并非一次性揭示，而是通过面试、重复互动、学习和分离逐步展开。现有匹配模型将这一过程过度简化为即时的次高斯反馈，忽略了信息渐进揭示对匹配决策的影响。作者旨在弥合这一建模鸿沟，提出能刻画时间延展反馈的通用框架。

Method: 首先，将双边匹配建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈（POMG），包含四个核心机制：有成本的匹配前筛选（面试）、匹配后的噪声观测、隐式偏好特征的动态演化、以及内生的继续/解除匹配决策。其次，基于该框架构建Learn2Match多智能体强化学习基准，支持去中心化决策。最后，使用独立PPO与CA-ETC（bandit风格基线）进行对比实验，评估指标包括累计社会福祉、累计遗憾值以及新提出的"信息摩擦损失"（衡量因隐式偏好不完全揭示导致的福祉损失）。

Result: 在时间延展反馈条件下，独立PPO在累计社会福祉和累计遗憾值上均优于CA-ETC基线，验证了MARL在动态匹配市场中的潜力。然而，PPO的信息摩擦损失更高，说明端到端MARL尚未具备匹配-bandit方法所特有的协调探索结构。

Conclusion: Learn2Match为下一代匹配市场算法开发提供了基准平台。理想算法需兼具RL的适应性、bandit算法的统计严谨性以及稳定匹配机制的结构感知能力。当前MARL方法在结构化探索方面仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: Two-sided matching markets often involve information that unfolds over time through interviews, repeated interaction, learning, and separation. Existing matching models typically reduce this process to immediate sub-Gaussian feedback about fixed preferences, missing settings where payoff-relevant information is revealed gradually and changes future matching decisions. We introduce a framework with temporally extended feedback, that formulates two-sided matching as a partially observable Markov game with costly pre-match screening, noisy post-match observations, evolving latent profiles, and endogenous continuation or dissolution. We instantiate this framework in Learn2Match, a multi-agent reinforcement-learning benchmark for dynamic matching markets. Learn2Match supports decentralized decision making over whom to interview, whom to match with, and when to dissolve a match, while evaluating policies using regret, social welfare, and an information-friction loss that measures the welfare gap caused by incomplete revelation of latent preferences. We find that independent PPO achieves higher cumulative social welfare and lower cumulative regret than the bandit-style CA-ETC baseline under temporally extended feedback, demonstrating the promise of MARL for dynamic matching markets. However, PPO still incurs higher information-friction loss, revealing that end-to-end MARL does not yet provide the coordinated exploration structure of matching-bandit methods. These results position Learn2Match as a benchmark for developing the next generation of matching-market algorithms: methods that are adaptive like RL agents, statistically disciplined like bandit algorithms, and structurally aware like stable-matching mechanisms.

</details>


### [190] [Performance Variation in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06746)
*Haruto Tanaka,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于百分位数的统计量和可视化方法（min-max IPR 和运行分位高亮），以更准确地评估深度强化学习算法的运行间鲁棒性，揭示传统均值和方差估计的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习算法普遍存在运行间鲁棒性差的问题（相同配置的独立运行中性能波动显著），但现有研究多报告均值性能的不确定性，缺乏针对运行间变异的有效评估工具，且传统方法存在与目标错位和低报风险。

Method: 提出两种基于百分位数的工具：(1) min-max IPR（最小-最大个体性能范围），一种利用样本分位数标准性质的统计量；(2) 运行分位高亮（run-wise percentile highlighting），一种可视化方法。两者均易于解读，能提供丰富的运行间性能变异信息。

Result: 通过三个案例验证：案例1——LayerNorm和倒数第二层归一化在PPO中缩小了性能变异，但在SAC中基本无变化；案例2——PPO、SAC、TD-MPC、TD-MPC2中，TD-MPC变异最小且数据效率最高；案例3——DQN和Rainbow在五个Atari环境中表现出相似的性能变异水平。

Conclusion: 基于百分位数的方法能更真实地反映运行间性能变异，建议RL研究社区采用此类工具以补充传统均值不确定性报告，从而更全面地评估算法鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms often suffer from low run-to-run robustness, manifesting as significant performance variation across independent runs of identically configured agents. Although this issue poses a spectrum of challenges across research and practice, relatively few studies develop methods to evaluate it; RL research instead often reports uncertainty in the estimated mean performance. In this paper, we outline the limitations of conventional uncertainty and variation estimates, particularly their misalignment with purpose and the risk of underreporting. We then propose an alternative percentile-based statistic and visualization method, min-max IPR and run-wise percentile highlighting, respectively. These percentile-based tools are easy to interpret and rely on standard properties of sample percentiles, providing rich information about run-to-run performance variation. We demonstrate this through three case studies. First, we show that LayerNorm and penultimate-layer normalizations narrow performance variation in PPO, whereas the variation is mostly unchanged in SAC. Second, we compare PPO, SAC, TD-MPC, and TD-MPC2, and show TD-MPC exhibits the least variation while being the most data efficient among the four. Finally, in a comparison of DQN and Rainbow on five Atari environments, we show that both algorithms exhibit similar levels of performance variation.

</details>


### [191] [A Rolling-Window Framework for Churn Prediction and Behavioral Driver Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06776)
*Muhammad Jawad Mufti,Omar Hammad,Haitham Saleh,Muqaddas Gull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于滚动行为窗口的时序显式客户流失预测框架，在30天观测窗口与30天评估窗口的明确时序分离下，结合特征工程与序列学习方法，实现实例级、可部署的持续流失风险评估。在大规模非契约服务平台数据集上，特征模型准确率达87.6%、ROC-AUC为0.94，序列模型召回率高达96.1%，且在未来未见数据上仍保持83%以上准确率和0.91以上AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有客户流失预测方法多依赖简化的时序假设或单点行为表征，难以支持持续风险评估、可解释性及实际部署需求，尤其在非契约性、按使用付费的服务环境中，流失行为无法直接观测，须从行为不活跃中推断。因此，需要一种能够随时间演化、反复进行实例级风险估计的时序显式框架。

Method: 提出时序显式流失预测框架：采用固定的30天滚动行为观察窗口提取客户行为特征，随后以30天未来评估窗口判定流失结果，确保行为证据与流失标签之间的严格时序分离。框架内部统一整合两类建模方法——基于特征的机器学习方法与基于序列的深度学习方法（用于捕捉时序脱离模式），两者共享相同的时序设计，实现重复的实例级流失风险估计。

Result: 在非契约服务平台的真实大规模数据集上，特征模型准确率87.6%、ROC-AUC 0.94；序列模型召回率最高达96.1%，能有效捕捉时序脱离模式。在未来未见数据上的评估显示：无模型重训练条件下，准确率保持83%以上，ROC-AUC维持在0.91以上，验证了框架在时序偏移下的稳健性。

Conclusion: 精心设计的时序框架——而非单纯依赖模型复杂度——是实现稳健、可解释、可部署的流失预测的关键。该框架为动态服务环境中的流失导向决策支持提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction is a central task in customer analytics, particularly in non-contractual, pay-per-use service environments where disengagement is not explicitly observed and must be inferred from behavioral inactivity. Existing churn prediction approaches often rely on simplified temporal assumptions or single-point representations of customer behavior, which limit their ability to support continuous risk assessment, interpretability, and realistic deployment over time. This study proposes a temporally explicit churn prediction framework that models customer behavior using rolling behavioral windows, enabling repeated and instance-level churn risk estimation as customer activity evolves. Customer behavior is summarized within a fixed 30-day observation window, followed by a 30-day future churn evaluation window, ensuring a clear temporal separation between behavioral evidence and churn outcomes. The framework integrates feature-based and sequence-based learning approaches within a unified temporal design. The proposed approach is evaluated on a large-scale, real-world dataset from a non-contractual service platform. Empirical results demonstrate strong and stable predictive performance, with accuracy reaching 87.6% and ROC-AUC of 0.94 for the feature-based model, while the sequence-based model achieves recall as high as 96.1% by capturing temporal disengagement patterns. Evaluation on future unseen data confirms meaningful robustness under temporal shift, with accuracy remaining above 83% and ROC-AUC exceeding 0.91 without model retraining. Overall, the findings highlight that carefully designed temporal framing, rather than model complexity alone, is critical for achieving robust, interpretable, and deployment-ready churn prediction. The study provides a practical foundation for churn-oriented decision support in dynamic service environments.

</details>


### [192] [Federated Foundation Models over Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06786)
*Kasra Borazjani,Fardis Nadimi,Payam Abdisarabshali,Owen Palinski,Allan Salihovic,Dinh Nguyen,Minghui Liwang,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态多任务联邦基础模型（M3T FedFMs）引入车载网络的愿景，结合基础模型的表达能力与联邦学习的隐私保护特性，探讨其训练范式、应用场景、部署挑战及未来研究方向，并通过Waymo数据集案例验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载网络需兼顾强大的多模态多任务智能处理能力与用户隐私保护，而当前多模态多任务基础模型（M3T FMs）与联邦学习（FL）的融合方向几乎未被探索，亟需填补这一空白，推动下一代车载智能发展。

Method: 首先阐述M3T FedFMs的基本训练与微调原理；其次梳理其在车载网络中的代表性用例；然后识别车载环境对M3T FedFMs部署的关键约束并提出前瞻性研究方向；最后在Waymo Open Dataset真实数据集上进行案例研究，并开源实现代码以促进复现与研究。

Result: 通过Waymo真实车载数据集的案例研究，初步验证了M3T FedFMs在车载网络中的应用潜力，证明了该方向的可行性，并已将代码开源至GitHub供社区复现与拓展。

Conclusion: M3T FedFMs有望成为下一代车载智能的关键使能技术，能够在保护隐私的前提下充分发挥多模态多任务基础模型的表达能力；但车载环境的独特约束仍需进一步研究克服，论文提出的前瞻性方向为后续工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a forward-looking vision for integrating the emerging multi-modal multi-task federated foundation models (M3T FedFMs) into vehicular networks, with the goal of unifying the expressive power of multi-modal multi-task foundation models (M3T FMs) with the privacy-preserving and distributed learning capabilities of federated learning (FL). Given the largely underexplored nature of this research direction, we first introduce the fundamental training/fine-tuning principles of M3T FedFMs. We then discuss a range of their representative use cases in vehicular networks, illustrating the significant potential of M3T FedFMs to enable next-generation vehicular intelligence. Afterwards, we identify key constraints inherent to vehicular environments that challenge the practical deployment of M3T FedFMs, and articulate a set of forward-looking research directions to address these challenges. Furthermore, through a case study conducted on a real-world vehicular dataset (i.e., Waymo Open Dataset), we demonstrate the promise of M3T FedFMs for vehicular networks and release our implementation to facilitate reproducibility and stimulate research in this emerging area (repository: https://github.com/KasraBorazjani/vehicular-fedfm)

</details>


### [193] [Interpreting Learning Under Competing Models: Joint and Stepwise Approaches for Dynamic Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06804)
*Yawen Ma,Sahoko Ishida,Kate Cain,Gabriel Wallin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了在动态认知诊断模型中，联合估计Q矩阵与学习过程 vs. 先确定Q矩阵再分析学习的两种策略，发现这一方法选择会显著改变关于学习者技能发展的实质性结论。联合分析在题项-技能结构不确定且题项池跨年级变化时更为可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化学习环境记录了学习者对每个题项的响应，为研究具体技能（而非总分）的发展提供了可能。但当每个题项测量哪些潜在技能（即Q矩阵）未知时，研究者面临一个关键决策：是联合估计Q矩阵与学习过程，还是先确定Q矩阵再研究学习。本文旨在揭示这一方法论选择如何影响关于学习者发展的实质性结论。

Method: 采用动态认知诊断模型（Dynamic Cognitive Diagnostic Models），分析两款阅读游戏在二年级至三年级的纵向数据（测量词汇与阅读理解技能）；利用题项文本嵌入（item-text embeddings）作为未知Q矩阵的先验信息；设置两种分析策略：联合分析（joint analysis）与偏差校正的逐步分析（bias-corrected stepwise analysis）；通过模拟研究识别两种分析何时产生分歧。

Result: 两种方法一致认为大多数学习者朝着掌握两种技能的方向发展，但对于三年级时有多少学习者仅部分熟练存在分歧，这将改变阅读进展的报告方式。模拟研究表明，当题项-技能结构不确定且题项池在年级间变化时，联合分析更为可靠。

Conclusion: 在Q矩阵不确定的情境下，联合估计Q矩阵与学习过程的分析策略更可靠；方法选择会实质性地影响关于学习者技能发展的结论，研究者在报告学习进展时需审慎考虑这一决策。

Abstract: Digital learning environments record learners' responses to individual items, making it possible to study the development of specific skills rather than overall scores. Drawing conclusions about learning from these data requires a model that links responses to latent skills and tracks how mastery changes over time. When the skills measured by each item are unknown, the analyst must decide whether to estimate this structure, the Q-matrix, jointly with the learning process, or to establish it first and study learning afterwards. We show that this decision can change substantive conclusions about how learners develop. Using dynamic cognitive diagnostic models, we analyse data from two reading games measuring vocabulary and comprehension from Grade 2 to Grade 3, with item-text embeddings providing prior information for the unknown Q-matrix. A joint analysis and a bias-corrected stepwise analysis agree that most learners move toward mastering both skills, but disagree about how many remain only partially proficient at Grade 3, changing how reading progress would be reported. A simulation study identifies when the two analyses diverge and shows that joint analysis is more reliable when the item-skill structure is uncertain and the item pool changes between grades. We provide R code for both analyses.

</details>


### [194] [SCALE: Scalable Cross-Attention Learning with Extrapolation for Agentic Workflow Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06820)
*Zhifei Xu,Jierui Lan,Zixuan Liang,Aiji Liang,Jinxi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALE 提出了一种基于交叉注意力指针网络的 DRL 调度器，通过结构化表示正则化（SRR）解决集群规模泛化问题，在未见过的节点数量上无需微调即可保持高效调度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DRL 调度器绑定固定集群规模，集群节点数变化时需重新训练，这在实际异构集群中成本高昂且不灵活。核心挑战在于如何让调度策略对服务器数量具有不变性，同时克服因规模变化导致的注意力特征分布偏移。

Method: SCALE 采用交叉注意力指针网络架构，以任务特征作为 Query、服务器特征作为 Key/Value，天然支持任意数量服务器。针对规模增大时注意力特征发生分布偏移的问题，提出结构化表示正则化（SRR）：结合去相关损失和向标准正态分布的 KL 惩罚，约束特征统计量在不同输入规模下保持稳定。

Result: 在 16 节点上训练，直接泛化测试 32 和 48 节点。在 N=48 时，SCALE 相比无 SRR 的同架构基线平均响应时间降低 8.9%，验证了显式正则化对于弥合规模泛化差距的必要性。

Conclusion: 仅靠置换不变架构不足以保证跨规模泛化，必须通过显式正则化手段（如 SRR）稳定注意力特征的统计分布，才能实现无需微调的集群规模外推调度。

Abstract: Agentic Large Language Model (LLM) systems decompose complex tasks into workflow Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) whose primitives must be scheduled on heterogeneous clusters. Existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) schedulers are tied to a fixed cluster size and require retraining whenever the number of servers changes. We propose SCALE (Scalable Cross-Attention Learning with Extrapolation), a DRL scheduler that generalizes to unseen cluster scales without fine-tuning. SCALE employs a cross-attention pointer network where task features query against server features, so the architecture accepts any number of servers by construction. We observe, however, that permutation-invariant architecture alone does not guarantee good performance at new scales - the attention feature undergoes distribution shift as the server count grows. To counter this, we introduce Structured Representation Regularization (SRR): a decorrelation loss combined with a KL penalty toward the standard normal, which keeps feature statistics stable regardless of input size. Trained on 16 nodes and tested directly on 32 and 48 nodes, SCALE reduces average response time by 8.9% at N=48 relative to the same architecture without SRR, confirming that explicit regularization is necessary to close the scale-generalization gap.

</details>


### [195] [PandaAI: A Practical Agent CQ2 for Neuro-symbolic Data Analysis And Integrated Decision-Making in Quantitative Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06823)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PandaAI 是一个闭环神经符号 LLM 代理，结合市场状态建模与约束 alpha 生成，解决金融时序决策中的低信噪比与非平稳问题，在 CSI 300 数据上 Rank IC 提升 18.2%，最大回撤降低 25.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在金融时序决策中表现不佳，根本原因是金融数据的低信噪比（SNR）和非平稳性。同时，通用大语言模型虽具备推理能力，但直接应用于金融领域会产生“金融毒性”输出，缺乏专业严谨性。因此需要一种将 LLM 推理能力与金融领域知识深度融合的范式。

Method: 提出 PandaAI，一种闭环神经符号 LLM 代理。核心方法包括：(1) 微调领域专用 LLM，弥合通用语言能力与金融严谨性之间的鸿沟；(2) 构建模块化架构，将 LLM 嵌入闭环系统；(3) 引入市场状态建模（market regime modeling）以适应非平稳性；(4) 设计约束 alpha 生成机制，抑制 LLM 的金融毒性输出；(5) 双通道自适应方法，使模型具备显式风险感知能力。

Result: 在 CSI 300 股票数据上的广泛实验表明，PandaAI 相比最先进的时序模型，Rank IC 提升 18.2%，最大回撤（Maximum Drawdown）降低 25.7%。

Conclusion: PandaAI 所提出的约束 LLM 生成与双通道自适应方法，为 LLM 在高风险序贯决策场景中的部署提供了一种通用范式，验证了神经符号方法在金融领域的有效性。

Abstract: While deep learning has excelled in various domains, its application to sequential decision-making in finance remains challenging due to the low Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) and non-stationarity of financial data. Leveraging the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose \textbf{PandaAI}, a closed-loop neuro-symbolic LLM agent with market regime modeling and constrained alpha generation, which bridges general LLM reasoning with financial rigor and suppresses the financial toxicity of LLM-generated outputs. To bridge the gap between general linguistic capability and financial rigor, we fine-tune a domain-specific LLM. Furthermore, we integrate this LLM into a modular architecture and form a closed-loop system. Unlike traditional models that optimize isolated prediction metrics, \textbf{PandaAI} is designed as a neuro-symbolic agent that navigates the complex, real-world financial environment with explicit risk awareness. Extensive experiments on CSI 300 stock data show that \textbf{PandaAI} achieves a $18.2\%$ higher Rank IC and $25.7\%$ lower maximum drawdown than state-of-the-art time-series models. Our constrained LLM generation and dual-channel adaptation method provide a general paradigm for LLM deployment in high-stakes sequential decision-making scenarios.

</details>


### [196] [Architecture Shapes Transfer Specificity in Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06827)
*D Yang Eng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了坐标网络中的迁移学习，区分了迁移幅度与迁移特异性，发现SIREN倾向于广泛复用权重而ReLU更具源选择性，强调架构评估应同时考虑控制条件下的迁移特异性而非仅看重迁移幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坐标网络的迁移学习通常用warm-start增益衡量，但这种增益究竟反映源特定的结构化知识迁移，还是仅仅是通用的权重复用，尚不清晰。研究者旨在厘清不同INR架构在迁移中的特异性差异。

Method: 采用三种INR架构（SIREN、ReLU MLP、Fourier-feature MLP），通过三类实验设计：(1) 受控解析测试，包含独立种子随机对照组；(2) 2D lid-driven-cavity Navier-Stokes基准；(3) 1D PDE参考解套件（热方程、粘性Burgers、聚焦立方NLS）。PDE基准使用同族替代源控制与辅助消融实验。

Result: 在10种子控制的1D几何测试中，Fourier Features结构化迁移增益最高（33.1×），其次是SIREN（23.0×）和ReLU（10.7×），但ReLU选择性最强：随机控制迁移仅0.41×，而SIREN达14.24×。在1D双参数族中，ReLU在默认设置下结构化与控制分离最清晰，Fourier Features需带宽重调后才改善。Navier-Stokes及1D PDE套件中无单一架构在所有方程上占优，但SIREN普遍广泛复用权重，ReLU和部分Fourier Features更具源选择性。静态诊断指标仍弱，启发式标度律A_transfer ∝ 1/Δt²在1D审计中被拒绝。

Conclusion: 迁移特异性可作为坐标网络的有效诊断工具。在科学机器学习中选择架构时，应在显式控制条件下评估，而非仅依赖迁移幅度作为判断依据。

Abstract: Transfer in coordinate networks is often measured by warm-start gain, but whether that gain reflects source-specific structure or generic weight reuse is less clear. We study this question across three implicit neural representation (INR) families, SIREN, ReLU MLPs, and Fourier-feature MLPs, using controlled analytic tests, a 2D lid-driven-cavity Navier--Stokes benchmark, and 1D PDE reference-solution suites for heat, viscous Burgers, and focusing cubic NLS. The analytic tests use independent-seed random controls, while the PDE benchmarks use alternate same-family source controls and auxiliary ablations.
  Across settings, transfer magnitude and transfer specificity separate clearly. In a 10-seed controlled 1D geometric test, Fourier Features show the largest structured transfer ($33.1\times$), followed by SIREN ($23.0\times$) and ReLU ($10.7\times$), but ReLU is far more selective: random-control transfer is $0.41\times$ for ReLU versus $14.24\times$ for SIREN. On a controlled two-parameter 1D family, the ranking changes: ReLU gives the clearest structured-versus-control separation at default settings, whereas Fourier Features improve only after bandwidth retuning. In Navier--Stokes and the broader 1D PDE suite, no single architecture dominates every equation, yet the same pattern remains: SIREN often reuses weights broadly, whereas ReLU and, in some equations, Fourier Features are more source-selective. Static diagnostics remain weak, and the heuristic scaling law $A_{\text{transfer}} \propto 1/Δt^2$ is rejected in the implemented 1D audit.
  These results position transfer specificity as a useful diagnostic for coordinate networks and suggest that architecture selection in scientific machine learning should be evaluated under explicit control conditions, not by transfer magnitude alone.

</details>


### [197] [Hearing the Unspoken: Language Model Priors for Acoustic Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06833)
*Jiani Xie,Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Semantic Gambit攻击，利用大语言模型实时预测上下文来突破实时ASR系统的因果性限制，将词错误率推高至35.6%，是现有最优攻击的三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时ASR系统因因果约束（仅能基于已接收的部分音频做决策）形成了对攻击者的信息瓶颈，现有攻击手段受此限制效果有限。作者旨在突破这一因果性限制，揭示低延迟LLM工具可被系统性利用来颠覆实时ASR流水线。

Method: 提出Semantic Gambit攻击方法：利用大语言模型（LLM）实时生成预测性上下文，为攻击者补充未来可能出现的语义信息，从而在攻击决策时突破因果约束，使对抗扰动更具前瞻性和破坏力。

Result: 实验表明，该攻击将语料级词错误率（WER）提升至35.6%，较当前最优攻击方法提高了三倍。

Conclusion: 常见的低延迟LLM工具可被武器化，用以系统性颠覆实时ASR系统，这揭示了实时语音识别流水线面临的严重安全威胁。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems operating in real-time settings must process acoustic input under strict temporal constraints, where transcription decisions are inherently made on incomplete information. This causal constraint serves as an information bottleneck on attackers, significantly limiting attack performance. Our new Semantic Gambit attack breaks this causal limitation by augmenting the adversary with predictive context derived from a Large Language Model in real-time. Our experiments show that this form of augmentation can elevate the corpus-level Word Error Rate to 35.6% -- a three-fold increase over the current state-of-the-art. Ultimately, this work reveals how common, low-latency LLM tooling can be exploited to systematically subvert real-time ASR pipelines.

</details>


### [198] [The Geometry of Last-Layer Model Stealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06854)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用几何学视角分析了通过已有方法窃取机器学习模型的可行性，明确了完美复制Transformer网络最后一层的确切条件，同时揭示了仅凭最终输出无法完全逆向工程隐藏网络的固有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究机器学习模型在已有的模型窃取攻击方法下的脆弱性边界，厘清攻击者究竟能从目标模型中提取哪些信息、无法获取哪些信息，从而为模型安全防护提供理论依据。

Method: 采用几何学理论框架，对Transformer网络的各层进行逐层分析：推导出完美复制最后一层（输出层）所需满足的精确数学条件；深入隐藏层分析时，从几何角度论证仅通过最终输出逆向重建隐藏层参数的内在局限性。

Result: （1）给出了完美复制Transformer网络最后一层的充分必要条件；（2）证明了隐藏层无法仅通过观察最终结果被完全逆向工程，存在明确的、不可逾越的理论界限；（3）系统性地勾勒出模型可窃取与不可窃取部分的清晰边界。

Conclusion: 模型窃取攻击的能力存在明确的几何学边界：攻击者可以精确复制输出层，但隐藏层的完整逆向工程在仅依赖最终输出的条件下本质上不可行。该研究为理解和评估机器学习模型的窃取风险提供了严谨的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper uses geometry to explain how a machine learning model can be stolen using an already existing well-known method. The author has shown the exact conditions required to perfectly copy the final layer of a transformer network. When looking deeper into the hidden layers the author has explained clear limits. The author has also demonstrated that a hidden network cannot be fully reverse engineered just by looking at the final results. The research clearly maps out what can and cannot be stolen from a model.

</details>


### [199] [Modeling Nonlinear Feature Interactions with Product-Unit Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06861)
*Ziyuan Li,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究乘积单元残差网络（PURe），通过显式建模特征间交叉耦合，在预测精度、鲁棒性、样本效率及交互可解释性方面优于标准MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLP仅隐式捕获非线性特征交互，导致表征纠缠，损害模型鲁棒性和可解释性。需要一种能够显式建模特征间乘性耦合关系的架构。

Method: 提出PURe架构，将乘性乘积单元与残差连接相结合，显式建模跨特征耦合并稳定优化。在交互驱动的合成基准及两个真实数据集上系统评估，比较实值与复值变体（匹配参数量），并利用SHAP交互分析揭示所学交互模式。

Result: PURe在预测精度上具有竞争力或更优表现；对高斯特征噪声的鲁棒性更强；在低数据场景下样本效率更高；SHAP分析显示其学习的交互模式更集中且结构更连贯。

Conclusion: PURe通过显式乘积交互机制，在不牺牲预测性能的前提下，显著提升了模型鲁棒性、小样本泛化能力和交互层面的可解释性，为科学和工程中需要理解非线性特征耦合的任务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Understanding nonlinear feature interactions is crucial in science and engineering, yet standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) often capture such interactions only implicitly, leading to entangled representations that can impair robustness and interpretability. We investigate product-unit residual networks (PURe) that integrate multiplicative product units with residual connections to explicitly model cross-feature couplings while stabilizing optimization. We conduct a systematic evaluation on an interaction-driven synthetic benchmark and two real-world datasets, assessing predictive accuracy, robustness to Gaussian feature noise, and performance under limited training data, and we compare real- and complex-valued variants under a matched parameter budget. Beyond accuracy, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based interaction analyses show that PURe learns more concentrated and structurally coherent interaction patterns than MLP baselines. Overall, PURe achieves competitive or improved performance, better robustness and sample efficiency in low-data regimes, and enhanced interaction-level interpretability.

</details>


### [200] [Product units in gated recurrent units improve nuclear-mass prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06866)
*Ziyuan Li,Paulo S. A. Freitas,John W. Clark,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于复数域加性-乘性积单元门控循环网络（AM-PU-GRU）的原子核质量预测方法，在AME2016和AME2020数据集上取得了插值RMSE 0.227±0.004 MeV、外推RMSE 0.179±0.015 MeV的最优性能，超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于机器学习的原子核质量预测方法在捕捉核素序列的长程依赖关系方面仍有提升空间，特别是实值模型难以同时建模幅度与相位动态特征，需要探索更强的序列建模架构以提升对核图表中未知区域的预测能力。

Method: 提出AM-PU-GRU模型，在门控循环单元（GRU）中集成乘性交互（multiplicative interactions）与积单元变换（product-unit transformations），并将计算扩展至复数域，以联合捕捉幅度与相位动力学特性。实验基于AME2016和AME2020数据集进行插值和外推评估，并以WS4和SEMF作为理论先验输入验证鲁棒性。

Result: AM-PU-GRU在插值任务上取得RMSE 0.227±0.004 MeV，外推任务上取得RMSE 0.179±0.015 MeV，均优于其他SOTA机器学习模型、实值GRU基线及积单元消融变体，且对不同理论先验（WS4、SEMF）表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 复数域积单元循环网络为基于序列的原子核质量预测建立了新的基准，验证了复数表示与乘性积单元变换在核物理序列建模中的有效性。

Abstract: The prediction of masses of atomic nuclei using machine learning can complement theoretical models and advance the exploration of poorly known domains of the nuclear chart. We propose a machine learning technique based on gated recurrent units (GRU), which have demonstrated competitive performance in nuclear-mass prediction by exploiting long-term dependencies. By integrating multiplicative interactions and product-unit transformations within recurrent units, we report significant improvements in nuclear-mass prediction. Computations are performed in the complex domain to jointly capture amplitude and phase dynamics. For interpolation and temporal-extrapolation tasks based on the atomic mass evaluation (AME2016 and AME2020), the complex additive-multiplicative product-unit gated recurrent unit (AM-PU-GRU) model consistently achieves the lowest prediction errors, with an interpolation RMSE of 0.227 $\pm$ 0.004 MeV and an extrapolation RMSE of 0.179 $\pm$ 0.015 MeV. These results surpass other state-of-the-art machine learning models and also outperform the real-valued GRU baseline and product-unit ablation variants, while remaining robust to different theoretical priors, including WS4 and SEMF. Our findings establish complex-valued product-unit recurrent networks as a new benchmark for sequence-based nuclear-mass prediction.

</details>


### [201] [Evidence-Grounded Ensemble Diagnosis of 802.11 Packet Captures: A Multi-Stage Pipeline with Deterministic Reliability Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06871)
*Jerome Henry,Swadhin Pradhan,Miroslav Popovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROBE提出一种多阶段流水线，通过确定性PCAP文本归一化、多模型集成与证据锚定调和，解决了LLM在802.11抓包诊断中虚构协议事件、置信度失校准及评估循环偏差三大问题，将证据F1从专家基线0.871提升至0.957。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统802.11抓包诊断依赖专家协议知识，效率低、不一致且不可扩展。现有LLM方法虽看似可行，但存在三类关键失效：(1) 对截断抓包虚构不存在的协议事件；(2) 产生无区分度的置信度分数（71%的报告均为0.95）；(3) 因模型参与生成金标准导致评估循环偏差。PROBE旨在系统性地解决上述三个问题。

Method: PROBE采用四级流水线架构：(1) 确定性PCAP到文本的归一化转换，具备帧级可验证性；(2) 多轮多次、多候选集成，搭配可选跨模型第二意见与渐进式混淆机制；(3) 判据感知的证据框架，将"未发现故障证据"本身视为贡献性证据；(4) 完全确定性的综合可靠性评分，融合证据有效性、运行间稳定性及跨模型一致性，摒弃LLM自评。

Result: 在87个企业Wi-Fi抓包（104对抓包-审查者）上：单次LLM分析将加权证据F1从专家基线0.871提升至0.912，但35%案例遗漏关键帧；朴素集成投票因多数票放大保守判据，F1降至0.842，50%已确认故障被误判为"无问题"或"证据不足"；PROBE的证据锚定调和方案达到0.957 F1，96%自动接受率，最坏情况F1下限高于0.70。LLM自报置信度始终聚集于0.95，证实其无信息含量。

Conclusion: PROBE通过证据锚定集成调和机制，在802.11抓包诊断任务上显著超越单模型和朴素集成方法，实现了高F1、高自动接受率及有意义的可靠性估计。所提出的模型无关评估框架（逐字段断言匹配）消除了模型共生成金标准的循环偏差，为该类任务的严格评估设立了新标准。

Abstract: Diagnosing 802.11 packet captures requires expert protocol knowledge, is slow, inconsistent across engineers, and unscalable. LLM-based approaches sound plausible but fabricate protocol events absent from captures (especially truncated traces), produce uncalibrated confidence scores, and suffer evaluation bias when golden references are co-produced by the model under test. We introduce PROBE (Protocol Reasoning Over evidence-Based Ensembles), a multi-stage pipeline addressing all three failures. It integrates (i) deterministic PCAP-to-text normalization with frame-level verifiability, (ii) multi-run, multi-candidate ensembles with optional cross-model second opinion and progressive obfuscation, (iii) a verdict-aware evidence framework treating absence of failure evidence as contributing evidence, and (iv) a fully deterministic composite reliability score from evidence validity, run-to-run stability, and cross-model agreement without LLM self-assessment.
  On 87 enterprise Wi-Fi captures (104 capture-reviewer pairs), single-pass LLM analysis raises weighted evidence F1 from 0.871 (expert baseline) to 0.912 but misses critical frames in 35% of cases. Naive ensemble voting drops below baseline (0.842) as majority voting amplifies conservative verdicts: 50% of confirmed failures are misclassified as 'no issue' or 'insufficient evidence.' Adding evidence-grounded reconciliation achieves 0.957 F1, a 96% auto-accept rate, and a worst-case floor above 0.70. LLM self-reported confidence clusters at 0.95 regardless of difficulty (71% report exactly 0.95), confirming it is uninformative. We also introduce a model-agnostic evaluation framework using per-field assertion matching, eliminating circular bias from model-co-produced golden references.

</details>


### [202] [GlucoFM-Bench: Benchmarking Time-Series Foundation Models for Blood Glucose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06881)
*Baiying Lu,Zhaohui Liang,Ryan Pontius,Shengpu Tang,Temiloluwa Prioleau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM-Bench 是一个全面基准，评估了预训练时序基础模型（TSFM）与监督深度学习模型在血糖预测上的表现。在零样本和少样本场景下，Chronos-2 和 TimesFM 等 TSFM 表现出色；但在全量数据训练时，轻量级 LSTM 最优。T1D 人群和高低血糖区间仍是难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血糖预测对糖尿病管理至关重要，但现有研究对时序基础模型（TSFM）在血糖预测中的评估严重不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统比较 TSFM 与监督深度学习模型在不同数据条件下的表现。

Method: 构建 GlucoFM-Bench 基准：涵盖8种代表性架构（预训练 TSFM、时序大语言模型、任务专用深度学习模型），在15个公开糖尿病数据集（1117人，包括 T1D、T2D、前驱糖尿病及非糖尿病人群）上，系统评估零样本、少样本和全量样本三种协议下，不同上下文长度和预测时域的表现。

Result: 预训练 TSFM（尤其 Chronos-2 和 TimesFM）在零样本和少样本迁移中表现强劲，最佳零样本模型性能与最佳全量监督模型差距在5%以内。但在数据充足时，轻量 LSTM 全量训练后比 TSFM 优 4-21%。分层分析显示，T1D 队列及高低血糖区间仍是挑战。

Conclusion: GlucoFM-Bench 为血糖预测基础模型的评估与改进提供了标准化、可复现的基准，并揭示了当前 TSFM 在特定人群和临床关键区间上的不足，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Blood glucose forecasting models are foundational for modern diabetes management systems, as reliable short-term predictions can enable proactive interventions, support automated insulin delivery, and reduce the risk of hypo- and hyperglycemic events. From a modeling perspective, glucose forecasting poses unique challenges due to heterogeneous physiological dynamics across diabetes populations. Traditional machine learning and deep learning models have been extensively evaluated for glucose prediction, yet recent time-series foundation models (TSFMs) remain much less studied in this setting. To bridge this gap, we present GlucoFM-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating state-of-the-art TSFMs alongside supervised deep learning models for blood glucose forecasting. We assess eight representative architectures, including pre-trained TSFMs, time-series large language models, and task-specific deep learning models, across 15 publicly available diabetes-relevant datasets comprising 1,117 individuals with type 1 diabetes, type 2 diabetes, prediabetes, and no diabetes. Models are evaluated under zero-shot, few-shot, and full-shot protocols, with systematic variation in context length and prediction horizon. Across datasets, pre-trained TSFMs, especially Chronos-2 and TimesFM, show strong zero-shot and few-shot transfer, with the best zero-shot model performing within 5% of the best full-shot supervised model. Yet, when task-specific data are abundant, a lightweight LSTM remains strongest, outperforming TSFMs by 4--21% under full-shot training. Stratified analyses reveal persistent challenges in T1D cohorts and hypo-/hyperglycemic ranges, highlighting the need for evaluation beyond aggregate error metrics. Together, GlucoFM-Bench provides a standardized and reproducible foundation for evaluating, comparing, and improving foundation models for blood glucose forecasting.

</details>


### [203] [Data-Constrained Language Model Pretraining: Improved Regularization and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06888)
*Zhiwei Xu,Shihao Wu,Hanseul Cho,Wei Hu,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究数据受限、算力充裕场景下的语言模型预训练，提出掩码输入正则化（MIR）和SoftQ缩放定律：MIR通过随机掩码的辅助损失提升模型性能，SoftQ能更准确地刻画重复数据下模型规模与数据量的交互关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练算力增长快于自然语言数据供给，预训练正进入"数据受限、算力充裕"的新范式，模型需在有限数据集上多轮训练。传统缩放定律（如Chinchilla）假设数据充足且单遍训练，其加性形式无法正确建模数据重复场景下模型规模与数据量的交互，需要新的正则化方法和缩放定律来指导这一新范式下的训练。

Method: 研究沿两个轴线展开：(1) 正则化——提出掩码输入正则化（MIR），在标准自回归预训练基础上增加对随机掩码输入的下一token预测辅助损失，利用扩散语言模型的核心机制来增强自回归训练，无需架构修改或推理开销；(2) 缩放定律——提出SoftQ缩放定律，采用耦合形式建模模型规模与数据量的交互关系，以替代Chinchilla等传统加性缩放定律。

Result: 在72M至1.4B参数模型上的实验表明：(1) MIR叠加在强权重衰减之上，验证损失优于仅使用强权重衰减的自回归模型，1.4B规模下下游任务也有提升；(2) SoftQ在数据受限场景下的拟合效果显著优于传统缩放定律，并能将MIR的收益等效为约1.3倍唯一训练数据量。

Conclusion: 在数据受限、算力充裕的预训练范式下，MIR是一种低成本有效的正则化手段，SoftQ提供了更准确的缩放关系建模工具，两者共同为数据受限预训练提供了正则化和扩展性的指导框架。

Abstract: Classical scaling laws for language model pretraining balance model size against training dataset size under a fixed compute budget, assuming abundant data and a single pass over the corpus. As training compute grows faster than the supply of natural language data, pretraining is likely to enter a data-constrained, compute-rich regime where models train for multiple epochs over a finite dataset. We study data-constrained pretraining along two axes, regularization and scaling. For regularization, we study masked-input regularization (MIR), an auxiliary next-token prediction loss on randomly masked inputs. MIR tests whether the random masking central to diffusion language models can benefit autoregressive pretraining without architectural changes or inference overhead. Across 72M to 1.4B parameter models, we find that MIR added on top of strong weight decay improves validation loss over autoregressive strong-weight-decay-only models, with downstream gains at 1.4B. For scaling, we propose SoftQ, a scaling law that couples model size and data size to capture their interaction under repeated data. Classical alternatives such as the Chinchilla law use an additive form that decouples these terms, making them misspecified in the data-constrained regime. We find that SoftQ fits data-constrained experiments substantially better than these alternatives, and estimates MIR's gains as equivalent to roughly 1.3 times as much unique training data. We release our code at https://github.com/yixinw-lab/dc_pretrain.

</details>


### [204] [GRASP: Geometry-aware Residual Alignment for Scalable Pretraining Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06892)
*Yue Min,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有可扩展数据归因方法基于加性假设，无法捕捉子集间的冗余与互补效应。本文提出GRASP，一种基于子集级反事实效用预测的交互感知代理模型，通过二次几何惩罚项显式建模子集交互，结合低维特征草图与有限下置信界选择协议实现预训练规模效率。实验表明GRASP在反事实子集保真度上使任务级秩相关性翻倍，同时将构建成本降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可扩展数据归因方法（如TRAK、Data Shapley变体）普遍采用加性假设，为每个训练样本分配孤立的效用分数。这一假设忽略了数据子集中普遍的冗余性（多个样本提供重叠信息）与互补覆盖性（样本组合产生协同效应），导致在子集级别的反事实预测中表现不佳。因此，需要一种能够显式建模子集交互的新归因范式。

Method: 本文提出GRASP（GRoup-Aware Subset Prediction）：(1) 理论层面，从光滑性下界出发，论证了子集效用函数不可简单加性分解，进而引入二次几何惩罚项来显式捕捉成对子集交互效应；(2) 效率层面，采用低维特征草图（feature sketches）压缩高维表示，并设计严格有限下置信界（Lower-Confidence Bound）选择协议，避免依赖隐式oracle调参即可实现预训练规模数据的可扩展性；(3) 整体框架将归因重新定义为子集级反事实效用预测任务，而非逐样本分数分配。

Result: (1) 在广泛的子集重训练评估中，GRASP显著优于现有可扩展基线方法；(2) 反事实子集保真度的任务级秩相关性提升超过两倍；(3) 前置的artifact构建成本降低近一个数量级；(4) 下游诊断实验表明，该评分机制可成功迁移至语言模型数据策展与跨域视觉数据选择任务。

Conclusion: GRASP通过显式建模子集交互，突破了传统加性数据归因的局限，在反事实预测精度和计算效率上均取得显著提升。其子集级评分机制具有良好的跨任务迁移能力，为大规模预训练语料库的优化策展奠定了鲁棒基础。

Abstract: Scalable data attribution methods typically assign isolated utility scores to individual training examples. This prevalent additive assumption fundamentally fails to capture critical subset dynamics, including data redundancy and complementary coverage. In this work, we reframe attribution as subset-level counterfactual utility prediction and introduce GRASP, an interaction-aware surrogate. Grounded in a theoretical smoothness lower bound, GRASP explicitly models subset interactions through a quadratic geometric penalty. To achieve pretraining-scale efficiency without relying on hidden oracle tuning, we couple low-dimensional feature sketches with a strictly finite lower-confidence bound selection protocol. Extensive subset-retraining evaluations demonstrate that GRASP decisively outperforms existing scalable baselines. It more than doubles the task-level rank correlation for counterfactual subset fidelity while reducing upfront artifact construction costs by nearly an order of magnitude. Downstream diagnostics further show that this scoring mechanism transfers to language model curation and cross-domain vision selection, establishing a robust foundation for optimizing massive pretraining corpora.

</details>


### [205] [TALAN: Task-Aligned Latent Adaptation Networks for Targeted Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06902)
*Chengkai Zhang,Ziteng Liu,Junpu Wang,Zeyi Tao,Yang Wang,Sagar Chordia,Qin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TALAN 是一种序列条件化的潜在侧路径，插入到 Transformer 残差流中，与低秩适配器在单次 SFT 循环中联合训练。它在四项 STEM/代码基准上，基于 Qwen3 系列和 Llama 模型，相比 LoRA/DoRA 基线取得了正向且低开销的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标后训练方法存在两难：低秩适配器（如 LoRA/DoRA）参数高效但任务是全局的、缺乏输入感知能力；激活干预方法具备输入感知，但通常需要独立的探针、向量或推理时引导。作者试图结合两者优势，设计一种既输入感知又可与适配器协同训练的轻量架构。

Method: 提出 TALAN（Task-Aligned Latent Adaptation Networks）：在 Transformer 残差流中插入一个序列条件化的潜在侧路径。其工作机制为：(1) 将活跃序列压缩为潜在记忆，(2) 通过混合器将记忆重新混合为 token 级扰动，(3) 通过受控残差更新写回主路径。TALAN 沿六个轴配置：插入位置、记忆大小、混合器类型、写回规则、可训练范围及梯度缩放。它与低秩适配器在同一 SFT 循环中联合训练，无需额外探针或推理时引导。

Result: 在四个 Qwen3 系列骨干模型和四项 STEM/代码基准上，TALAN 相较匹配的 LoRA 基线平均提升 +1.41 分，在所有四个骨干和全部 16 个模型-基准单元上均非负；相较 DoRA 平均提升 +1.85 分，13/16 单元为正。配对种子检查确认正向平均效应但方差不可忽略。成本极低：可训练参数占比 <1%，推理开销仅为匹配 LoRA 的 1.01–1.02 倍。在 Llama-3.2-1B 上的迁移实验也呈阳性。内部分析显示 TALAN 扰动幅度比适配器更新小 80–1700 倍，方向近乎正交，但该小幅度正交扰动沿深度传播并放大。

Conclusion: TALAN 提供了一种实用的平台，用于在标准适配器后训练框架内研究可引导的激活级适应。它证明了小幅度、正交的激活扰动可以有效传播并增强模型能力，同时保持极低的参数和推理开销。

Abstract: Targeted post-training aims to improve reasoning, math, and code without degrading strengths. Low-rank adapters are efficient but task-global; activation interventions are input-aware but often require separate probes, vectors, or inference-time steering. We introduce TALAN (Task-Aligned Latent Adaptation Networks), a sequence-conditioned latent side path inserted into a transformer's residual stream and co-trained with a low-rank adapter in one SFT loop. TALAN compresses the active sequence into latent memory, remixes it into token-level perturbations, and writes them back through a controlled residual update. It is configured along six axes: insertion location, memory size, mixer, writeback rule, trainability scope, and gradient scale.
  Across four Qwen3-family backbones and four STEM/code benchmarks, TALAN improves matched LoRA and DoRA baselines. With LoRA, it yields a +1.41 point cross-model mean gain, positive on all four backbones and non-negative on all 16 model-benchmark cells. With DoRA, it yields a +1.85 point mean gain, positive on all backbones and on 13 of 16 cells. Paired seed checks support positive average effects but show nontrivial variance, so we treat them as sensitivity checks. Cost is small: <1% trainable parameters relative to the backbone and 1.01-1.02x inference overhead versus matched LoRA. A Llama-3.2-1B transfer probe is also positive under LoRA and rsLoRA across seven paired seeds, supporting a transfer beyond Qwen.
  Internal-state analyses suggest TALAN is a small complementary activation intervention. The matched adapter update is 80-1,700x larger than the TALAN perturbation, yet their directions have near-zero cosine; per-layer measurements show this small orthogonal perturbation propagates and amplifies through depth. TALAN offers a practical platform for studying steerable activation-level adaptation within standard adapter-based post-training.

</details>


### [206] [The Fine-Tuning Trap: Evaluating Negative Transfer and the Role of PEFT in Sub-1B Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06920)
*Rahul Nair,Chun Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对135M-1B参数规模的五个小型语言模型在数学推理任务上进行微调基准测试，发现全量微调在300M以下模型中会导致"负迁移"，性能甚至低于零样本基线；LoRA和DoRA表现相当但各有优势领域，建议对齐后的sub-1B模型默认使用参数高效微调，避免在小于500M参数的模型上使用全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上部署小型语言模型需要高效的微调策略，既能适配新任务又不损害通用能力。当前缺乏对sub-1B模型微调方法的系统性对比，尤其是不清楚全量微调与参数高效微调在小模型上的稳定性差异。

Method: 在数学推理任务上对五个135M-1B参数的模型进行基准测试，对比三种微调范式：Full Fine-Tuning（全量微调）、LoRA、DoRA（Weight-Decomposed LoRA），同时引入零样本和5-shot上下文学习作为基线。

Result: 核心发现：(1) 全量微调在300M以下模型中造成灾难性遗忘，准确率低于零样本基线；(2) LoRA与DoRA性能相当但各有侧重——DoRA在复杂推理（GSM8K）上更优，LoRA在模式匹配（OrcaMath）上更强；(3) 在对齐模型（Qwen2.5-0.5B）上，LoRA超越全量微调；(4) 最小架构（SmolLM2-135M）上，简单的5-shot上下文学习即可超过全量微调。

Conclusion: 建议对所有对齐后的sub-1B模型默认采用PEFT方法，并强烈反对在任何小于500M参数的架构上使用全量微调，以防止灾难性遗忘。PEFT不仅是效率偏好，更是稳定性需求。

Abstract: Deploying Small Language Models (SLMs) on edge devices requires efficient fine-tuning strategies that adapt models to new tasks without degrading their general capabilities. In this study, we benchmark five sub-1B models (135M-1B) on mathematical reasoning tasks and uncover a critical vulnerability: Full Fine-Tuning (Full FT) actively harms performance in models under 300M parameters, often dropping accuracy below zero-shot baselines. This "negative transfer" makes Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) not just an efficiency preference, but a stability requirement. We find that while Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and Weight-Decomposed LoRA (DoRA) perform comparably, their strengths vary by task; DoRA excels in complex reasoning (GSM8K), while LoRA dominates pattern matching (OrcaMath). In particular, Full FT is outperformed by LoRA on aligned models (Qwen2.5-0.5B) and even by simple 5-shot In-Context Learning on the smallest architectures (SmolLM2-135M). Based on these findings, we recommend defaulting to PEFT for all aligned sub-1B models and caution against Full FT for any architecture smaller than 500M parameters to prevent catastrophic forgetting. Reproduction of this work can be found at https://github.com/gulguluu/tiny-slm-finetune-compare.

</details>


### [207] [From Sampled Outcomes to Capability Distributions: Rethinking Supervision for LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06924)
*Guannan Lai,Haoran Hu,Long Chen,Zhenguo Li,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有LLM路由方法用单次响应作为能力标签，但LLM生成具有随机性，单次监督只是噪声观察。本文提出DARS框架，从分布视角构建路由监督，同时考虑输入侧（语义等价查询变体）和输出侧（随机生成）的不确定性，提供更可靠的路由监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成本质上是随机的，现有路由方法将模型对查询的单次响应作为其能力标签来训练路由器，这种单次监督仅提供了查询-模型对行为的噪声观察而非可靠的能力估计。这种系统性噪声使学习到的路由策略不可靠，需要从分布视角重新审视路由监督的构建方式。

Method: 提出DARS（Distribution-Aware Routing Supervision）框架，核心思路是不依赖单一生成响应，而是从分布视角构建路由监督。具体从两个维度捕捉不确定性：(1) 输入侧——考虑语义等价的查询表述变体如何影响模型性能；(2) 输出侧——考虑同一查询下多次随机生成的结果差异。基于这些分布感知的观察，构建更可靠的路由监督信号。

Result: 在多种任务上的实验表明，单次标签在模型选择上具有误导性，而分布感知监督提供了更稳定的标签，并改善了学习到的路由行为。

Conclusion: 可靠的LLM路由应超越单次响应观察，建立在查询级模型能力分布的基础上。DARS框架通过分布感知的监督构建方式，有效缓解了单次监督引入的系统性噪声问题。

Abstract: Existing LLM routing methods typically treat a model's single response to a query as its capability label for training routers. However, because LLM generation is inherently stochastic, such single-shot supervision provides only a noisy observation of a query-model pair's behavior rather than a reliable capability estimate. We show that this assumption introduces systematic noise into routing supervision, making learned routing policies less reliable. To address this issue, we propose DARS (Distribution-Aware Routing Supervision), a framework that constructs routing supervision from a distributional view of model behavior. Instead of relying on a single generated response, DARS considers uncertainty from both the input side and the output side, capturing how semantically equivalent query formulations and stochastic generations affect model performance. Based on these distribution-aware observations, DARS builds more reliable supervision signals for routing. Experiments across diverse tasks show that single-shot labels can be misleading for model selection, while distribution-aware supervision provides more stable labels and improves learned routing behavior. Our results suggest that reliable LLM routing should move beyond single-response observations and be grounded in query-level model capability distributions.

</details>


### [208] [Uniform Stability and Generalization Error of GD and SGD on Fixed-Point Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06934)
*Jonghyun Shin,Sejun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了梯度下降（GD）和随机梯度下降（SGD）在离散参数空间中的泛化误差、均匀稳定性和均匀参数稳定性，其中每步更新涉及确定性或随机舍入。结果表明，确定性舍入会使GD的泛化误差恶化，而SGD配合确定性舍入可获得非平凡的均匀稳定性保证；随机舍入则引入与维度相关的泛化误差，这在实值优化中不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解离散参数空间（如量化神经网络、低精度训练）中优化算法的泛化行为。现有稳定性理论多针对实值连续空间，离散舍入操作引入的误差如何影响泛化、稳定性的理论理解尚不充分。

Method: 对凸、Lipschitz、光滑损失函数，分析GD和SGD在离散参数空间中确定性舍入与随机舍入下的泛化误差上界与匹配下界，以及均匀稳定性和均匀参数稳定性的紧界。

Result: 确定性舍入使GD泛化误差从O(T/n)恶化至O(T/√n)，均匀稳定性变为Ω(T)，稳定性界空洞化；SGD+确定性舍入在一维获紧界O(T/n)，高维获O(T²/n)；随机舍入引入随维度增长的泛化误差；随机舍入下均匀参数稳定性上界在损失可分解为坐标函数和时是紧的。

Conclusion: 离散参数空间中的舍入策略对泛化和稳定性有本质影响：确定性舍入损害GD但SGD可缓解；随机舍入引入维度依赖的泛化误差，揭示了离散优化中不同于实值优化的新现象，对低精度训练实践有指导意义。

Abstract: We analyze generalization error, uniform stability, and uniform argument stability of gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD) over discrete parameter spaces, where each update involves deterministic or stochastic rounding. We show that deterministic rounding degrades the generalization error of GD on convex, Lipschitz, and smooth loss functions, increasing the rate from $O(T/n)$ to $O(T/\sqrt{n})$, and establish matching lower bounds. We further prove that uniform stability of GD becomes $Ω(T)$, showing that stability-based generalization bounds are vacuous in this setting. In contrast, for the same losses, stochastic gradient descent with deterministic rounding admits nontrivial uniform stability guarantees, which differ qualitatively from the real-valued case and exhibit distinct dependencies on the number of iterations and the dimension: we prove tight bounds $O(T/n)$ for one dimension and $O(T^2/n)$ for higher dimensions. We also show that stochastic rounding can introduce generalization error that increases with the dimension; such a phenomenon is absent in standard real-valued optimization and in the deterministic rounding case. Finally, we provide upper bounds on uniform argument stability for stochastic rounding schemes and show that these bounds are tight when the loss can be represented as a sum of coordinate-wise functions.

</details>


### [209] [GenPO++: Generative Policy Optimization with Jacobian-free Likelihood Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06967)
*Ke Hu,Shutong Ding,Panxin Tao,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenPO++ 提出一种可逆生成策略优化框架，利用历史状态作为辅助记忆，在高阶可逆ODE求解器中实现精确且无雅可比的似然比计算，解决了流式策略在无偏on-policy强化学习中似然评估困难的问题，在多项任务上取得竞争性或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生成策略（flow-based policies）具有表达力强、支持多模态动作分布的优势，但现有方法在基于似然的on-policy学习中，要么使用有偏的动作密度比近似替代，要么通过虚拟动作增广来恢复精确似然，前者引入偏差、后者增加策略空间和计算开销。因此需要一种既能保持流式策略表达力，又能精确、高效计算动作似然比的方法。

Method: GenPO++ 将历史状态作为辅助记忆，构建高阶可逆ODE求解器。该可逆生成策略映射的对数行列式仅由固定的求解器系数决定，从而在不改变原始动作维度的前提下，实现精确且无需计算雅可比行列式的似然比计算，避免了动作比率偏差和虚拟动作开销。

Result: 在大规模仿真控制、微调任务及真实世界机器人操作任务上的评估表明，GenPO++ 在性能上与最先进的on-policy RL方法相当或更优，同时提升了训练稳定性和计算效率。

Conclusion: GenPO++ 成功将流式生成策略应用于无偏的on-policy强化学习，通过可逆ODE设计实现了精确高效的似然比计算，在多个任务上验证了其有效性、稳定性和效率优势。

Abstract: Generative policies provide expressive and multimodal action distributions, making them attractive for reinforcement learning (RL) in complex continuous-control tasks. Among them, flow-based policies are especially appealing because they generate actions through deterministic transport maps. However, applying such generative policies to likelihood-based on-policy learning remains limited by the difficulty of evaluating the probability of executed actions. Existing flow RL methods either replace the true action-density ratio with approximate surrogates, which can introduce biased updates, or recover exact likelihoods through dummy-action augmentation, which enlarges the policy space and increases computation. In this work, we propose GenPO++, a reversible generative policy optimization framework that uses history states as auxiliary memory in a high-order reversible ODE solver, yielding exact inversion without changing the original action dimension. The resulting generative policy map has a log-determinant determined only by fixed solver coefficients, enabling exact and Jacobian-free likelihood-ratio computation. This design preserves the expressiveness of generative flow policies while avoiding both action ratio bias and dummy-action overhead. We evaluate GenPO++ on large-scale simulated control, fine-tuning, and real-world robotic manipulation tasks, where it achieves competitive or superior performance over state-of-the-art on-policy RL methods, while improving training stability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [210] [Accelerating Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimisation via Predictive-Gradient Catalysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06984)
*Alma Rahat,Tinkle Chugh,Jonathan Fieldsend,Richard Allmendinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用高斯过程预测梯度作为辅助信号的多目标贝叶斯优化加速机制，通过将局部平稳性信息融入现有采集函数来加速收敛，在DTLZ基准测试上展现出显著的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）在有限评估预算下收敛速度较慢，现有采集函数虽符合Pareto兼容性，但未能充分利用代理模型的梯度信息来引导搜索。本文旨在利用高斯过程预测梯度中的局部平稳性信息来增强现有采集函数，从而在有限预算下更快逼近全局Pareto前沿。

Method: 提出一种通用的"预测梯度催化"（predictive gradient catalysis）加速机制，不替代现有Pareto兼容采集函数，而是用代理模型梯度衍生的局部平稳性信息对其进行增强。研究了两种催化剂实例：（1）自适应的基于多重梯度下降算法的催化剂（MGDA）；（2）预定义权重的变体，适用于预算紧张时的聚焦探索。在DTLZ基准套件（2目标、10决策变量）上进行实验验证。

Result: 在代理模型足够精确的情况下，特别是对于平稳问题，预测梯度催化相较于EHVI、AugTch、tMPoI、SAF等采集函数能够带来显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: 预测梯度催化是一种通用、有效的MOBO加速策略，核心前提是代理模型需具备足够精度。该方法可灵活嵌入多种现有采集函数，对平稳优化问题尤为有效。

Abstract: This paper presents a general acceleration mechanism for multi-objective Bayesian optimisation (MOBO) that leverages Gaussian process predictive gradients as auxiliary signals. Rather than replacing existing Pareto-compliant acquisition functions, the proposed approach augments them with local stationarity information derived from surrogate-derived gradients, enabling faster convergence toward the global Pareto set under limited evaluation budgets. Two catalyst instantiations are investigated: an adaptive Multiple-Gradient Descent Algorithm-Based Catalyst (MGDA) and a predefined-weight variant that enables focused exploration when budgets are tight. Experiments on the DTLZ benchmark suite (using 2 objectives and 10 decision variables) show that predictive gradient catalysis can deliver significant acceleration compared to other acquisition functions (EHVI, AugTch, tMPoI, SAF) when surrogates are accurate, particularly for stationary problems.

</details>


### [211] [Heterogeneous Effects of Green Finance on Urban Decarbonization: Evidence from 285 Cities in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06986)
*Xueyang Li,Jinlei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 绿色金融显著降低城市碳强度，其中绿色债券和绿色投资效果最强，主要通过能源结构优化等路径发挥作用，且效应在不同发展水平和资源禀赋的城市间存在显著异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管绿色金融已成为低碳城市转型的关键工具，但其实际的脱碳效应及传导机制仍不清晰，需要实证研究来揭示绿色金融是否以及如何降低城市层面的碳强度。

Method: 采用计量经济学模型与基于机器学习（SHAP）的分析相结合的方法，包括中介效应分析以识别传导机制，以及空间计量模型捕捉空间溢出效应。

Result: 绿色金融显著降低碳强度，绿色债券和绿色投资影响最强且存在空间溢出效应；效应在四五线城市最为显著；传导路径按重要性依次为能源结构优化、产业升级、外商直接投资和技术创新；SHAP分析显示绿色债券、基金和信贷的脱碳贡献最大，且在技术能力低、工业依赖度高、以煤为主的能源结构的城市边际效应更强。

Conclusion: 研究为建设多层次、区域差异化的绿色金融体系以促进包容性低碳转型提供了理论支持和政策指导。

Abstract: While green finance has become a key instrument for low-carbon city transitions, its actual decarbonization effects and transmission mechanisms remain unclear. This study employs econometric models and machine learning-based analysis to examine whether and how green finance reduces city-level carbon intensity. Results show that green finance significantly lowers carbon intensity, with green bonds and green investment having the strongest impacts and evident spatial spillovers. The effects vary by development level, being most pronounced in Fourth- and Fifth-tier cities. Mediation analysis reveals that green finance operates mainly through energy structure optimization, followed by industrial upgrading, foreign direct investment, and technological innovation. SHAP analysis confirms substantial differences across financial instruments, with green bonds, funds, and credit contributing most to decarbonization. Moreover, the marginal impact is stronger in cities with low technological capacity, high industrial dependency, and coal-based energy mixes. These findings provide theoretical support and policy guidance for building a multi-level, regionally differentiated green finance system to promote inclusive low-carbon transitions. Keywords: Green Finance; Carbon Intensity; Decarbonization Effect; Machine Learning; City

</details>


### [212] [Accelerating Reproducible Research in Synthetic EHR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06990)
*Jalen Jiang,Chufan Gao,Ethan Rasmussen,Stephen Z. Xie,Jimeng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个轻量级端到端的合成电子健康记录（EHR）基准测试框架，统一数据加载、模型训练和评估流程，在完整ICD-9粒度下复现并比较了MedGAN、CorGAN、PromptEHR、HALO及GPT-2基线，提供架构无关的隐私-效用评估套件及自举置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成EHR生成模型因代码库分离、数据加载器不兼容、库依赖冲突及评估协议不一致，导致无法进行公平的头对头比较。需要一个统一的基准测试框架来解决这些工程障碍。

Method: 基于PyHealth社区库构建统一流水线，涵盖数据摄入、标准化模型训练和架构无关评估三个阶段。复现四种强基线（MedGAN、CorGAN、PromptEHR、HALO）并新增GPT-2基线，在完整ICD-9词汇粒度下进行纵向诊断代码生成。提出统一的隐私-效用评估套件，适用于GAN和Transformer两类生成器，并报告所有指标的自举置信区间。

Result: 成功统一复现多个基线模型并实现公平比较；揭示了现有模型在长尾诊断代码上的性能普遍较差；提供了架构无关的评估套件，可对GAN和Transformer模型同等适用。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了合成EHR模型运行、扩展和评估的工程门槛，为社区驱动的可复现性研究和基准测试提供了起点。框架具备超出诊断代码的扩展能力，未来可支持更多EHR模态。

Abstract: The generation of high-fidelity synthetic Electronic Health Records (EHR) is crucial for advancing medical research while preserving patient privacy. However, head-to-head comparison of existing generative models is hindered by disjointed codebases, incompatible data loaders, conflicting library dependencies, and inconsistent evaluation protocols. To address these gaps, we introduce a lightweight, end-to-end benchmarking framework for reproducible synthetic EHR evaluation, organized as a unified pipeline spanning data ingestion, standardized model training, and architecture-agnostic evaluation. Our current implementation targets the generation of longitudinal ICD diagnosis codes -- the most commonly studied modality in this literature -- and is built on the community-maintained PyHealth library. We reimplement and unify strong baselines (MedGAN, CorGAN, PromptEHR, HALO) under full ICD-9 vocabulary granularity, and add a lightweight GPT-2 baseline from the general-purpose sequence-modeling literature. We contribute a rigorous, architecture-agnostic privacy-utility evaluation suite that applies identically to GAN- and transformer-based generators, and report bootstrapped confidence intervals across all metrics. We further analyze the poor long-tailed performance of existing models and discuss the extensibility of our framework beyond diagnosis codes. By lowering the engineering barrier to running, extending, and evaluating under a single pipeline, we introduce a starting point for community-driven reproducibility and benchmarking synthetic EHR models.

</details>


### [213] [RASFT: Rollout-Adaptive Supervised Fine-Tuning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07006)
*Yongliang Miao,Fengyuan Liu,Wei Shi,Yanguang Liu,Fei Sun,Na Zou,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RASFT 提出一种策略感知的监督微调框架，根据模型自身在问题上的可解性（通过在线 rollout 估计）动态调整专家监督强度：模型薄弱时加强专家引导，模型已具备可靠推理能力时则放松模仿并纳入正确的自生成轨迹，同时引入裁剪逆比率约束策略漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SFT 将单条专家推理轨迹视为唯一目标，但推理并非简单的路径模仿——机械地跟随单一示范可能导致模型过拟合于表面形式，抑制模型自身的推理分布。因此需要一种能够根据模型当前能力自适应调节监督信号的微调方法。

Method: 提出 Rollout-Adaptive SFT (RASFT) 框架，核心包括：(1) 对每个问题执行在线 rollout，估计当前策略对该问题的可解性；(2) 根据可解性动态调整监督策略——模型薄弱时强化专家引导，模型已能正确推理时放松对专家轨迹的刚性模仿，并纳入模型自身生成的正确轨迹；(3) 引入冻结参考模型与当前策略之间的裁剪逆比率（clipped inverse ratio），约束训练过程中的过度策略漂移，保留有用的推理先验。

Result: 在多个模型上，于六个数学推理基准和两个代码推理基准上进行实验，RASFT 的整体性能优于标准 SFT、SFT 变体以及代表性强化学习方法。

Conclusion: RASFT 通过策略感知的自适应监督，有效兼顾了利用专家知识与保护模型自身推理能力，为推理任务中的 LLM 微调提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a prevailing method for adapting large language models to reasoning tasks by imitating offline expert demonstrations, often treating a single expert trajectory as the target behavior. However, reasoning is not simple path imitation: rigidly following one demonstrated solution may overfit to surface forms and suppress the model's own reasoning distribution. We propose Rollout-Adaptive Supervised Fine-Tuning (RASFT), a policy-aware SFT framework that calibrates expert supervision according to problem-level solvability estimated from verified on-policy rollouts. For each problem, RASFT strengthens expert guidance when the current policy struggles, while relaxing rigid imitation and incorporating correct self-generated trajectories when the model already exhibits reliable reasoning behavior. To preserve useful reasoning priors, RASFT further introduces a clipped inverse ratio between the frozen reference model and the current policy to constrain excessive policy drift. Experiments across multiple models on six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two code reasoning benchmarks show that RASFT achieves better overall performance than SFT, SFT variants, and representative RL methods. The code is available at https://github.com/zjd1sq/RASFT.

</details>


### [214] [A Geometric View for Understanding Concept Learning and Neuron Interpretation in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07007)
*Chenhao Zhang,Chris Lin,Su-In Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的数学框架，用集合论和几何视角形式化稀疏自编码器中的概念学习与神经元解释问题，定义了检测、分离、逼近三种学习层次，并给出几何条件、误差界和容量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前稀疏自编码器虽能提升神经网络可解释性，但缺乏对"概念"和"学习"的原则性定义，导致概念学习的理论理解不足，难以系统分析特征分裂、吸收等常见现象。

Method: 将概念形式化为数据点集合，将概念学习定义为人类定义概念与模型诱导概念之间的集合对齐问题；提出检测、分离、逼近三种递增的学习层次，推导其几何条件与误差界；利用形式概念分析连接概念学习与神经元解释，构建概念格刻画多对多映射；在合成数据上用ReLU和Top-K SAE进行实验验证。

Result: 给出了概念可被单神经元或多神经元单元表示的几何条件和容量约束；从集合论角度解释了特征分裂、特征吸收、特征家族和层次概念等现象；揭示了概念学习与神经元解释不必一致，可通过概念格组织其多对多结构；实验揭示了SAE大小和稀疏度对概念学习的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为SAE的概念学习提供了严格的理论基础，统一了解释多种现象，并揭示了概念学习与神经元解释之间的本质区别与联系，为后续可解释性研究提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We propose a unified mathematical framework for a geometric understanding of concept learning and neuron interpretation in sparse autoencoders (SAEs). While SAEs improve interpretability of neural networks by learning sparse feature representations, a principled definition of ''concept'' and ''learning'' remains unclear. We formalize concepts as sets of data points and cast concept learning as a set-alignment problem between human-defined and model-induced concepts. This formulation distinguishes three increasingly strong notions of learning -- detection, separation, and approximation -- and yields geometric conditions, error bounds, and capacity constraints for when concepts can be represented by individual neurons or multi-neuron units. It also provides a set-theoretic account for common SAE phenomena, including feature splitting, feature absorption, feature families, and hierarchical concepts. Finally, we connect concept learning and neuron interpretation through formal concept analysis, showing that the two directions need not agree and that their many-to-many structure can be organized by concept lattices. Experiments on synthetic data with ReLU and Top-$K$ SAEs illustrate the theory and reveal the effects of SAE size and sparsity on concept learning.

</details>


### [215] [CF-JEPA: Mask-free forward prediction with asymmetric encoder utilization for time-series representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07031)
*Jaehoon Lee,Sunghyun Sim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CF-JEPA提出无掩码的多时域前向预测框架，利用在线编码器与EMA目标编码器的不对称性分别处理分类和预测/异常检测，在分类、预测、异常检测三大任务上全面取得领先或接近领先的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列自监督学习的两大主流范式——对比方法（正负样本对构建困难）和掩码方法（破坏时间连续性）——均存在根本性缺陷。JEPA架构虽有潜力，但现有时间序列JEPA变体仍依赖掩码，继承了连续性问题。因此需要一种无掩码、能直接利用时间序列内在时序顺序的框架。

Method: 提出CF-JEPA（Crop-based Forward JEPA），一种无掩码框架：用随机裁剪作为上下文视图，在表示空间中沿时间正向预测短期、中期和长期未来表示，直接利用时序数据的天然顺序作为学习信号。同时发现单次训练中在线编码器与EMA目标编码器之间存在不对称性——前者产生高秩判别特征，后者产生平滑的低秩时序特征。据此将分类任务路由至在线编码器，预测与异常检测路由至EMA目标编码器。

Result: 在126个UCR分类数据集、26个UEA分类数据集、8个变压器温度预测基准和KPI/Yahoo异常检测上进行评估：UCR和UEA上取得自监督基线中最高的平均准确率和排名；多变量预测MSE降低27%（无额外训练成本）；单变量预测和KNN评分的异常检测排名第二。

Conclusion: CF-JEPA通过无掩码裁剪与前向预测策略成功解决了时间序列表示学习中掩码破坏连续性的问题，并巧妙利用双编码器不对称性实现了分类与预测任务的统一框架，在多个基准上验证了其有效性，尤其多变量预测MSE降低27%具有显著实用价值。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) for time-series representation learning is dominated by two paradigms: contrastive methods, which face challenges in constructing positive or negative pairs, and masking-based methods, which disrupt the temporal continuity of time-series signals. Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) offers a promising alternative by predicting in representation space rather than reconstructing raw inputs. However, existing time-series JEPA variants still rely on masking and therefore inherit its continuity problem.
