Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) demonstrate remarkable performance improvements by iteratively reflecting, exploring, and executing complex tasks, yet suffer from inefficiencies due to redundant reasoning, known as "overthinking". Existing methods to mitigate this issue either rely on static difficulty estimates or require task-specific training, and thus fail to adapt to the dynamic complexity during reasoning. In this work, we empirically show that the problem difficulty evolves dynamically throughout the reasoning process and is linearly encoded in the LRM's step-level embeddings. Building on this insight, we propose DyCon, a training-free framework that leverages latent step-level representations to explicitly model the evolving task difficulty, enabling the dynamic control of reasoning depth to mitigate the overthinking issue. Extensive experiments conducted on four models ranging from 4B to 32B, and across twelve benchmarks in math reasoning, general question answering, and coding tasks demonstrate that DyCon significantly enhances reasoning efficiency by reducing redundant steps without sacrificing accuracy or generalization. Project page and code are available at https://github.com/yu-lin-li/DyCon.

</details>


### [286] [Beyond Post-hoc Explanation: Toward Glassbox AI via Probabilistic Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07113)
*Manuele Leonelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为Glassbox Framework的新型架构，以贝叶斯网络作为生成模型的 ante-hoc（事前）透明中介层，旨在从根本上解决LLM在高风险场景中的可解释性与问责性问题，而非依赖不稳定的post-hoc解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在公共管理、法律推理、医疗等高风险场景中迅速成为基础设施组件，但其不透明性在制度和法律上已不可接受。现有的post-hoc解释方法与模型实际推理过程无形式化关联，提供的解释不稳定、不可抗辩。根本问题不在于缺乏解释，而在于缺乏结构化推理本身。

Method: 提出Glassbox Framework，核心是以贝叶斯网络作为ante-hoc中介层，在推理发生前编码领域知识、因果假设和概率依赖关系。该架构支持可审计的推理轨迹、不确定性量化和可抗辩的输出。框架在福利资格判定场景中进行实例化验证，并识别了语义对齐、动态模型构建、概率基化与人类治理等关键挑战。

Result: 论文在福利资格判定场景中对框架进行了概念验证，系统性地识别出实现该框架规模部署所需解决的四个基础性挑战：语义对齐、动态模型构建、概率基化（probabilistic grounding）和人类治理。

Conclusion: 通过从post-hoc解释转向ante-hoc概率中介，Glassbox Framework为构建不仅强大而且根本上可问责的AI系统提供了一条有原则的路径。该工作更多是勾勒出一个架构蓝图和研究议程，而非提供完整的工程实现。

Abstract: Large language models are rapidly becoming infrastructural components in high-stakes institutional settings, including public administration, legal reasoning, and healthcare, where opacity is not merely inconvenient but institutionally and legally untenable. Existing approaches to explainability are predominantly post-hoc, offering unstable, non-contestable accounts that have no formal relationship to the reasoning process that produced the output. We argue that the problem is not the absence of explanation but the absence of structured reasoning in the first place. This paper makes the case for a fundamentally different architecture, which we call the Glassbox Framework, in which Bayesian networks serve as transparent, ante-hoc mediation layers for generative models. Bayesian networks encode domain knowledge, causal assumptions, and probabilistic dependencies before inference occurs, enabling auditable reasoning traces, uncertainty quantification, and contestable outputs. We characterise the architecture of this framework and ground it in a benefit eligibility scenario, identifying the foundational challenges spanning semantic alignment, dynamic model construction, probabilistic grounding, and human governance that must be solved to realise it at scale. By shifting from post-hoc explanation to ante-hoc probabilistic mediation, this work outlines a principled path toward AI systems that are not only powerful but fundamentally accountable.

</details>


### [287] [Think Fast: Estimating No-CoT Task-Completion Time Horizons of Frontier AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07157)
*Dewi Gould,Francis Rhys Ward,Anders Cairns Woodruff,Rauno Arike,Josh Hills,Alex Serrano,Ida Caspary,Jason Ross Brown,Jo J. Jiao,Patrick Leask,Twm Stone,Ram Potham,Ionut Gabriel Stan,Harry Mayne,Simeon Hellsten,Shubhorup Biswas,Ariana Azarbal,William L. Anderson,Elle Najt,Ryan Greenblatt,Julian Stastny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了前沿AI模型在不依赖思维链（CoT）推理时的表现，发现无CoT能力的50%任务完成时间阈值（TH）每约一年翻一番，GPT-5.5的TH已超3分钟。预测到2030年可能达到25分钟，建议开发者持续监测此指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿AI模型的安全监控高度依赖对其思维链（CoT）推理的可观测性。若模型发展出在不产生显式推理标记的情况下进行复杂内部推理的能力，将从根本上瓦解这类安全监督机制。因此，亟需系统性地测量模型在无CoT条件下的推理能力，评估这一潜在风险的发展趋势。

Method: 构建了涵盖数学、编程、谜题、因果推理、心智理论和策略推理等43个基准、超过30,000道题目的评估套件。提出两个核心指标：(1) 50%任务完成时间阈值（TH）——模型达到50%成功率对应的任务所需人类时间；(2) 50%推理token阈值——o3-mini模型完成相同任务达50%成功率所需的最少推理token数。通过对过去六年前沿模型的纵向比较和趋势外推进行预测。

Result: 前沿模型的无CoT 50% TH过去六年大约每年翻一番。GPT-5.5的TH超过3分钟，推理token阈值超过1,500个token。中位数预测显示，前沿模型的无CoT TH到2028年可能超过7分钟，到2030年可能超过25分钟，但预测存在较大不确定性。

Conclusion: 随着模型内部推理能力持续快速增长，依赖CoT可观测性的安全监督范式面临明确的时间窗口挑战。建议前沿AI开发者显式追踪无CoT推理能力的增长，为即将到来的隐蔽推理风险做好准备。

Abstract: Many efforts to ensure frontier AI models are safe rely on monitoring their chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. If models become able to perform sufficiently complex reasoning internally, without explicit thinking tokens, this would undermine such oversight. We measure how well frontier models reason without CoT across a suite of over 30,000 questions spanning 43 benchmarks in domains including math, coding, puzzles, causality, theory-of-mind, and strategic reasoning. To compare models against humans, we estimate the $50\%$-task-completion time horizon (TH): the human time required for tasks a model completes with $50\%$ success rate. We complement this with a $50\%$ reasoning token horizon: the minimum number of o3-mini reasoning tokens needed for tasks a model solves with $50\%$ success rate. We find that the no-CoT $50\%$ TH of frontier models has been doubling roughly every year over the past six years, with GPT-5.5's TH reaching over 3 minutes and reasoning token horizon exceeding 1,500 tokens. Our median estimates predict that frontier no-CoT THs could exceed 7 minutes by 2028, and 25 minutes by 2030, though these projections carry substantial uncertainty. We recommend frontier developers track this explicitly.

</details>


### [288] [TOPSIS-RAD: Ranking According to Desires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07253)
*Leonardo Fernandes Costa,Helder Gomes Costa,Diogo Lima,Brunno Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统TOPSIS的参考点来自观测备选集，易受异常值和排序反转影响。TOPSIS-RAD引入决策者自定义的否决性能水平(VPL)和期望性能水平(DPL)，前者在归一化前排除不可行方案，后者将性能上限定锚于决策者期望值，从而增强排序的稳定性和与决策需求的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TOPSIS方法存在三个核心缺陷：(1)正负理想解完全依赖观测备选集，可能与决策者真实需求错位；(2)排序结果对异常值敏感；(3)存在排序反转问题。需在保留TOPSIS距离框架的基础上，引入决策者先验信息来克服上述局限。

Method: 提出TOPSIS-RAD方法，核心机制包括两个决策者自定义的参考水平数组：Vetoed Performance Levels (VPL)——在归一化前剔除不可行备选方案，防止其扭曲排序边界；Desired Performance Levels (DPL)——将性能值截断在决策者期望水平再进行归一化，使正理想解锚定于明确的期望而非数据集极值。通过三个玩具示例验证各机制的有效性。

Result: VPL通过移除不可行方案重塑了归一化边界；固定的DPL前沿通过限制远超期望水平的性能值影响，实现了排序的稳定化。整体方法在保持TOPSIS经典距离结构的同时，将排序锚定于稳定的、决策者指定的边界上。

Conclusion: TOPSIS-RAD有效解决了传统TOPSIS中参考点依赖数据集极值带来的排序不稳定和与决策者需求不一致的问题。论文同时讨论了方法的局限性和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Traditional TOPSIS derives its reference points -- the Positive Ideal Solution ($PIS$) and Negative Ideal Solution ($NIS$) -- from the observed alternative set, making rankings susceptible to misalignment with decision-maker (DM) requirements, sensitivity to outlier performances, and rank reversal. This paper proposes TOPSIS-RAD, which addresses these issues by incorporating two arrays of DM-defined reference levels. Vetoed Performance Levels ($VPL$) exclude non-viable alternatives before normalisation, preventing them from distorting the ranking frontiers. Desired Performance Levels ($DPL$) cap performances at the DM's desired level before normalisation, anchoring the $PIS$ in explicit aspirations rather than dataset extremes. Three toy examples demonstrate each mechanism: $VPL$ reshapes normalisation boundaries by removing a non-viable alternative; fixed $DPL$ frontiers stabilise rankings by limiting the influence of performances well above the desired level. The method preserves the familiar distance-based structure of TOPSIS while grounding the ranking in stable, DM-specified boundaries. Limitations and future research directions are also discussed.

</details>


### [289] [DuMate-DeepResearch: An Auditable Multi-Agent System with Recursive Search and Rubric-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07299)
*Lingyong Yan,Can Xu,Yukun Zhao,Wenxuan Li,Qingyang Chen,Jiulong Wu,Wenli Song,Xiangnan Li,Weixian Shi,Yiqun Chen,Xuchen Ma,Yuchen Li,Jiashu Zhao,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuMate-DeepResearch 是一个基于千帆Agent Foundry的多智能体深度研究框架，通过图驱动动态规划、递归双层执行架构和基于评分标准的测试时优化三项机制，在 DeepResearch Bench 和 DeepResearch Bench II 两个基准上均取得了最优成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度研究（DR）系统面临四大局限：长周期规划的范围不明确、单智能体难以有效分解和调度复杂任务、长篇综合报告中存在幻觉风险、以及过程可审计性不足。本文旨在解决这些瓶颈。

Method: 提出 DuMate-DeepResearch 框架，核心设计包括：(1) 将 Agent Core（任务理解、规划、调度）与可扩展的 Tool Ecosystem（检索、证据获取、报告渲染）解耦，实现全过程可追溯；(2) 三项关键机制：图驱动的动态规划策略，从粗到细扩展研究路线图并通过反思、重规划、回溯和并行分支持续修正；递归双层执行设计，将复杂搜索子任务委托给内部 Search Agent，由其运行独立规划循环，隔离检索噪声；基于评分标准的测试时优化机制，动态生成任务特定的质量标准，作为证据锚定综合和自适应停止的实时推理支架。

Result: 在 DeepResearch Bench 上取得最佳总分 58.03%，在 DeepResearch Bench II 上取得最佳总分 61.95%，并在信息召回和分析维度排名第一。

Conclusion: DuMate-DeepResearch 通过架构解耦与三项创新机制有效应对了深度研究中的关键挑战，在两个基准上确立了新的 state-of-the-art，验证了多智能体框架在复杂开放研究任务中的优势。

Abstract: Deep Research (DR) has emerged as a new agentic paradigm to tackle complex, open-ended research tasks, demanding systems that can iteratively frame problems, acquire evidence, verify sources, and synthesize long-form reports. In practice, however, current DR systems are constrained by four interrelated limitations: long-horizon planning over an underspecified scope, the bottleneck of decomposing and scheduling such tasks within a single agent, hallucination risk in long-form synthesis, and limited process auditability. This technical report presents DuMate-DeepResearch, a multi-agent DR framework built on the Qianfan Agent Foundry. The framework decouples the Agent Core, which handles task understanding, planning, and scheduling, from an extensible Tool Ecosystem for retrieval, evidence acquisition, and report rendering, making every intermediate decision and tool invocation explicitly traceable. Building on this infrastructure, DuMate-DeepResearch further introduces three mechanisms: (i) a graph-based dynamic planning strategy expands the research roadmap coarse-to-fine and continuously revises it through reflection, re-planning, backtracking, and parallel branching; (ii) a recursive two-level execution design delegates each complex search sub-task to an inner Search Agent that runs its own planning loop, isolating noisy retrieval and stabilizing long-horizon execution; (iii) a rubric-based test-time optimization mechanism dynamically generates task-specific quality criteria and uses them as live reasoning scaffolds for evidence-grounded synthesis and adaptive stopping. Across two deep research benchmarks, DuMate-DeepResearch establishes new state-of-the-art results: the best overall score (58.03%) on DeepResearch Bench, and the best overall score (61.95%) on DeepResearch Bench II while ranking first in information recall and analysis.

</details>


### [290] [Off-Policy Evaluation with Strategic Agents via Local Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07308)
*Kiet Q. H. Vo,Abbavaram Gowtham Reddy,Julian Rodemann,Siu Lun Chau,Krikamol Muandet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究战略行为下的离线策略评估（OPE），其中决策主体会策略性地修改协变量以响应策略。作者提出利用事后解释（post-hoc explanations）披露局部信息，从而揭示主体在适应前的预战略协变量，进而构建双重稳健估计量。在主体成本敏感性服从条件对数正态分布的假设下，证明了估计量的一致性，并实证验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPE方法假设协变量外生于策略，但在现实中，决策主体（如贷款申请人、求职者）会针对策略进行战略性适应，导致协变量分布出现策略依赖的偏移，破坏外生性假设。现有解决方案要么要求重复交互，要么需要完全了解主体的响应行为，限制了方法的实际应用。本文旨在解决一次性（one-shot）OPE设定下、仅拥有部分响应行为知识时的策略评估问题。

Method: 核心思路是通过事后解释披露局部信息，揭示主体在策略适应前的预战略协变量，从而缓解战略行为造成的信息损失。基于此结构，作者：1）估计主体响应行为的统计模型；2）构建策略价值的双重稳健（doubly robust）估计量；3）假设主体成本敏感性服从条件对数正态分布，建立估计量的一致性理论保证。

Result: 在条件对数正态分布的假设下，证明了所提双重稳健估计量的一致性。实证实验验证了该方法在战略行为OPE场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 交互设计可以通过事后解释揭示主体战略响应中隐藏的结构，从而缓解信息不对称问题。这一发现不仅为战略行为下的OPE提供了可行方案，也为更广泛的策略评估中的信息设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study off-policy evaluation (OPE) under strategic behavior where decision subjects (or agents) respond to a decision maker's policy by strategically modifying their covariates. Such behavior induces a policy-dependent covariate shift, breaking the standard assumption in existing methods that covariates are exogenous to the policy. Related work addresses this challenge by imposing strong assumptions such as repeated interactions or full knowledge of agents' response behavior, substantially limiting its applicability to OPE. In contrast, we consider a one-shot OPE setting where the decision maker has only partial knowledge of the agents' response behavior. Our key insight is that disclosing local information through post-hoc explanations reveals agents' pre-strategic covariates prior to adaptation, mitigating the information loss induced by strategic behavior. Leveraging this structure, we estimate a statistical model for the agents' responses and construct a doubly robust estimator for policy value. By assuming that the agents' cost sensitivity follows a conditional log-normal distribution, we establish consistency of the proposed estimator and validate our approach empirically. More broadly, our results highlight how interaction design can mitigate information asymmetry by revealing otherwise hidden structure in agents' strategic responses.

</details>


### [291] [Act As a Real Researcher: A Suite of Benchmarks Evaluating Frontier LLMs and Agentic Harnesses in Research Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07462)
*Jiayu Wang,Weijiang Lv,Bowen Fu,Jing Fu,Jiayi Song,Lingyu Zhang,Lanxuan Xue,Luodi Chen,Zepeng Xin,Kaiyu Li,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AARRI-Bench 是首个专注于评估 AI 代理在细粒度研究场景中能否像人类研究者一样展现专业素养、严谨性和细腻判断力的基准测试。实验表明，即便最优配置也仅达 68.3% 成功率，常常遗漏对人类研究者而言显而易见的细微关键细节，说明发展类人研究者需要深入探索研究行为本身，而非仅依赖复杂脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 代理虽在复杂长周期编码任务和自主实验执行方面表现优异，但在领域敏感性、研究伦理和细微科学判断方面仍存在显著局限，尚无法完全替代人类研究者。现有基准测试主要评估宏观执行能力，缺乏对代理能否模仿人类研究者专业素养和细腻推理能力的细粒度考察。

Method: 提出 AARR（Act As a Real Researcher）基准系列概念，并构建首个基准 AARRI-Bench（Act As a Real Research Intern）。该基准聚焦于细粒度研究场景，评估 AI 代理在研究行为中的专业性、严谨性和细腻推理能力。在多个前沿模型和代理系统上进行了大规模实验。

Result: 表现最优的配置（Mini-SWE-Agent + Claude Opus 4.7）仅达到 68.3% 的成功率，且频繁遗漏对人类研究者而言显而易见的细微但关键细节。结果揭示当前前沿代理在研究行为模拟方面仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 构建真正具有人类研究者水准的 AI，需要超越复杂脚手架的思维，转向对研究行为本身的深入探索。AARRI-Bench 为这一方向提供了评估基础，数据集已开源发布。

Abstract: As foundation models advance and agent scaffolding becomes increasingly sophisticated, agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in complex, long-horizon coding tasks and even autonomous experiment execution. Despite their evolution from research assistants into autonomous research agents, these systems still exhibit significant limitations in field sensitivity, research ethics, and nuanced scientific judgment. Consequently, frontier agents remain unable to fully replace human researchers. To bridge this gap, we conceptualize the AARR (Act As a Real Researcher) benchmark series. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily assess macro-level execution capabilities, AARR focuses on whether agents can emulate the professionalism, thoroughness, and nuanced reasoning that characterize human researchers in granular research scenarios. In this work, we propose AARRI-Bench (Act As a Real Research Intern), the first benchmark in this series. We conduct extensive experiments across frontier models and agentic systems, revealing that even the best-performing configuration (Mini-SWE-Agent with Claude Opus 4.7) achieves only 68.3\% success rate, frequently overlooking subtle yet critical details that are obvious to real human researchers. Our results indicate that developing researcher-like AI requires further exploration of research behavior, rather than merely complex scaffolding. Our data is released at https://github.com/AARR-bench/AARRI-bench.

</details>


### [292] [How AI Agents Reshape Knowledge Work: Autonomy, Efficiency, and Scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07489)
*Jeremy Yang,Kate Zyskowski,Noah Yonack,Jerry Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用Perplexity的Search和Computer产品数据，研究了AI从对话助手向自主代理的转变如何加速和重塑知识工作。核心发现：Computer每会话自主工作26分钟（Search仅33秒），将任务完成时间从269分钟降至36分钟（降低87%），不满意率降低55%，同时扩展了用户尝试的任务范围、复杂度和跨领域广度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统正从对话助手向能端到端执行任务的自主代理转变，但其对知识工作的实际影响缺乏基于生产数据的实证研究。作者旨在通过对比同一平台上不同自主级别的产品（Search vs. Computer），量化AI代理如何改变工作流程、质量、成本和工作范围。

Method: 使用Perplexity的Search（低自主性）和Computer（高自主性）产品的生产数据，采用自然实验设计：匹配两个产品中初始查询几乎相同的会话对，以控制任务本身差异，从而识别自主性带来的因果效应。比较维度包括：自主工作时间、后续查询分布、不满意率、任务完成时间、估计成本，以及查询的职业跨度、认知层次、专业广度和任务复合性。

Result: 三项核心发现：（1）Computer每会话自主工作26分钟 vs. Search的33秒；Computer将后续查询分布转向验证和扩展等高阶工作；每查询不满意率降低55%。（2）在匹配任务上，Computer将完成时间从269分钟压缩至36分钟，时间成本降低87%，估算费用降低94%。（3）Computer的查询更频繁跨越职业边界、需要更高阶认知、利用更广泛专业知识、呈现复合任务形式，并解锁了同一用户在Search中几乎不存在的工作活动。

Conclusion: AI代理（Computer）相较对话助手（Search）显著加速工作流程、提升输出质量、降低成本，并扩展了自动化工作的广度和深度，标志着AI从辅助工具向自主执行者的实质性转变。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems are bridging the gap between intelligence and utility by shifting from conversational assistants to autonomous agents that execute tasks end to end. Using production data from Perplexity's Search and Computer products, we study this transition by examining how AI agents accelerate and reshape knowledge work. Three key empirical findings emerge. First, using sessions with near-identical initial query pairs as natural experiments for the same underlying task attempted with both products, Computer performs 26 minutes of autonomous work per user session, versus 33 seconds for Search. Computer automates task decomposition and execution that Search users might otherwise manually orchestrate and implement. As a result, Computer shifts follow-up query distribution toward higher-order work such as verification and extension. Autonomy also increases execution quality, with per-query dissatisfaction rates 55% lower on Computer than on Search. Second, due to its autonomy advantage, Computer reduces completion time from 269 to 36 minutes on matched tasks, lowering estimated time and cost by 87% and 94%, respectively, compared to humans equipped with Search alone. Third, Computer changes the scope of work that users attempt: Computer queries more often cross occupational boundaries, require higher-order cognition, draw on broader expertise, take the form of composite tasks that bundle interdependent subtasks into a single query, and unlock work activities that are essentially absent from Search usage among the same users. Together, the evidence indicates that AI agents accelerate workflows, enhance output quality, reduce costs, and expand the breadth and depth of automated work.

</details>


### [293] [Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05510)
*Ahmed Alansary,Molham Mohamed,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严重度感知的多模型框架，结合课程训练策略与相关性响应选择，用于提升远程医疗场景下大语言模型的医学回答质量。通过三阶段课程学习（轻度→中度→危重）训练五个独立LLM，推理时择优输出，在MAQA数据集上微调后BERTScore达90.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在医疗问答中难以根据病例严重程度动态调整回答，缺乏对渐进式复杂度的适应能力，导致回答的一致性和语境适配性不足。这驱动研究者设计能感知严重度并自适应不同复杂度医学查询的框架。

Method: 提出严重度感知的多模型框架，核心包含两部分：(1) 三阶段课程学习策略——将医学案例按轻度、中度、危重分级，五个LLM各自按此顺序渐进训练，逐步习得领域知识；(2) 推理阶段，所有模型对同一查询生成候选回答，再通过基于相关性的响应选择机制筛选最优输出。训练评估基于带标注的MAQA医学问答数据集，使用BERTScore作为评价指标。

Result: 实验结果显示，在MAQA数据集上，所提方法在基线设置下BERTScore达到86.71%，微调后进一步提升至90.30%，均显著优于基线模型与单独微调模型，验证了课程学习与多模型响应选择结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 课程学习与多模型响应选择的结合能有效提升医学文本生成中回答的质量与相关性。该严重度感知框架为构建更可靠、上下文适配性更强的远程医疗对话系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Telehealth systems have become increasingly important for delivering accessible and timely medical information. Existing large language models often struggle to provide consistent and contextually appropriate medical responses across varying levels of case severity. This limitation highlights the need for models that can effectively adapt to the progressive complexity in medical queries. To address this challenge, we introduce a severity-aware multi-model framework that integrates curriculum training strategy with relevance-based response selection. The proposed framework employs a three-stage curriculum learning strategy, where each model is trained sequentially on mild, moderate, and critical cases to progressively acquire domain knowledge. The approach utilizes five large language models, each independently trained under the same curriculum scheme. During inference, all models generate candidate responses, and the most appropriate response is selected as the final output. The framework is trained and evaluated on the MAQA dataset, which provides annotated medical question-answer pairs. Experimental results evaluated using BERTScore demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance compared to both baseline and fine-tuned models, attaining 86.71% in the baseline setting and 90.30% after fine-tuning. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining curriculum learning with multi-model response selection in improving response quality and relevance in medical text generation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [294] [Robust inference for cyclic-stress accelerated life tests under interval monitoring with lognormal lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06699)
*María Jaenada,Leandro Pardo,Kiran Prajapat*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对循环应力加速寿命试验（CyALT）中区间截尾的对数正态寿命数据，提出了基于加权密度幂散度（WDPD）的鲁棒推断方法（WMDPDE），建立了其渐近分布和影响函数，并通过模拟和空调可靠性数据验证了该方法在抗离群值和保持高效性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业实践中，许多产品（如汽车空调、电池、航空航天部件）在高低应力交替下工作，CyALT比传统加速试验更贴近真实工况。然而，失效时间通常以区间截尾形式记录，且小样本工业实验易受数据污染，传统极大似然估计对此敏感，亟需一种鲁棒的统计推断方法。

Method: 采用加权密度幂散度（WDPD）最小化方法构建鲁棒估计量（WMDPDE），推导了WMDPDE的渐近分布，利用影响函数刻画其鲁棒性，并给出了渐近置信区间和Bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 模拟研究表明，WMDPDE在数据存在离群值时具有良好的抗污染能力，同时在无污染数据下保持较高估计效率。空调可靠性数据集的实证分析展示了该方法在CyALT框架下的实际应用优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的WMDPDE方法为CyALT区间截尾数据提供了一种兼具鲁棒性和高效性的可靠推断工具，适用于小样本工业实验场景。

Abstract: Highly reliable products are often tested under accelerated conditions to provoke failures within a feasible timeframe. For products whose service life involves repeated alternation between two stress levels, such as automotive air-conditioners, batteries, and aerospace components, cyclic-stress accelerated life testing (CyALT) provides a more realistic loading profile than conventional accelerated tests. In practice, failures are often recorded only at scheduled inspection times, leading to interval-censored counts rather than exact lifetimes. Moreover, traditional maximum likelihood estimation is sensitive to data contamination, which is a genuine concern in small-sample industrial experiments. This paper develops robust inferential procedures for CyALT models with lognormal lifetimes under interval monitoring. Robust estimators are obtained by minimizing a weighted density power divergence (WDPD), leading to the weighted minimum density power divergence estimator (WMDPDE). We establish the asymptotic distribution of the WMDPDE, derive influence function expressions to characterize the robustness, and present asymptotic and bootstrap confidence intervals for important lifetime characteristics. A simulation study confirms that the WMDPDE provides substantial protection against outliers while retaining high efficiency under clean data. The methodology is illustrated through the analysis of an air-conditioner reliability dataset, demonstrating the practical advantages of robust inference in the CyALT framework.

</details>


### [295] [Bayesian genome-wide clustering and variable selection of transcriptomic data via rank-based mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06730)
*Emilie Eliseussen,Haakon Muggerud,Luca Coraggio,Ida Scheel,Thomas Fleischer,Valeria Vitelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出lowBM3模型，将贝叶斯Mallows模型推广到可同时处理聚类与变量选择的高维排序数据场景，并配套后处理框架，在模拟研究和乳腺癌基因组学应用中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着排序数据日益丰富，亟需能够处理高维数据并提供不确定性量化的无监督排序推断框架。贝叶斯Mallows模型(BMM)虽灵活，但在超高维场景（如组学分析）中可扩展性受限，且缺乏同时进行聚类和变量选择的集成方案。

Method: 提出lowBM3（低维贝叶斯Mallows模型混合），将BMM推广为联合处理聚类与变量选择的排序模型。该方法在统一贝叶斯框架下同时实现样本异质性建模、无监督参数估计和模型选择，并可扩展至超高维数据。此外，引入配套后处理框架，对共识排序和变量选择器的离散后验分布提供后验摘要。

Result: 模拟研究验证了方法的性能。在癌症基因组学签名发现的应用中，对乳腺癌患者RNA-seq bulk基因表达数据进行全基因组聚类，展示了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: lowBM3是首个将BMM推广至联合聚类与变量选择的排序模型，填补了超高维排序数据无监督推断的空白，在基因组学等高维生物医学数据分析中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: With the increasing availability of ranking data, there has been a growing demand for appropriate unsupervised rank-based inferential frameworks capable of handling high-dimensional datasets and providing uncertainty quantification for all estimates. Rank-based methods have also seen a growing popularity in -omics pipelines, as ranking continuous measurements provides a robust means of handling non-normally distributed data. The Bayesian Mallows model (BMM) has emerged as a promising choice because of its adaptability to various types of ranking data and its flexible framework, integrating cluster-wise rank aggregation with inference at the individual level. However, the scalability of BMM to ultra-high-dimensional settings, such as -omics analyses, has remained limited. The present paper addresses this issue by introducing the first rank-based model generalizing BMM to jointly handle clustering and variable selection, namely the lower-dimensional Bayesian Mallows Model Mixture (lowBM3). The proposed method provides a novel Bayesian framework that simultaneously handles heterogeneity in the sample, unsupervised parameter estimation, and model selection in a scalable manner for ultra-high-dimensional data. Additionally, a companion postprocessing framework is introduced to provide posterior summaries of the discrete posterior distributions of both the consensus ranking and the variable selector. Simulation studies are performed to assess the performance of the method. The usefulness of the method is also shown in an application to signature discovery for cancer genomics, where RNA-seq bulk gene expression data obtained from breast cancer patients are clustered genome-wide.

</details>


### [296] [Cluster-Aware Conformal Calibration for Spatio-Temporal Distributional Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06753)
*Gooyoung Kim,Chae Young Lim,Wen-Ting Wang,Hao-Yun Huang,Wei-Ying Wu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对DeepKriging类模型在非均匀空间采样下固定规则网格基函数效率低的问题，提出了一种结合聚类自适应空间基函数和聚类感知保形校准的扩展方法，用于可靠的时空分布预测。实验表明，该方法在聚类观测模式下显著提升了覆盖精度和尾部可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepKriging类模型（如Spatio-Temporal DeepKriging）虽通过基函数嵌入和随机梯度学习提升了可扩展性，但其固定的规则网格空间基函数在高度非均匀采样模式下效率低下：对稀疏区域过度分配容量，而对密集簇区域解析不足。因此，需要一种能够适应异质性空间采样的实用扩展方案。

Method: 提出两个核心改进：(1) 聚类自适应空间基函数——其中心和尺度从空间采样密度初始化，以更好捕捉异质性空间采样；(2) 聚类感知保形校准——在空间簇内确定预测区间宽度，当校准样本不足时采用全局回退机制。整体校准流程显式针对空间异质性和局部误校准问题。

Result: 通过模拟研究和PM2.5数据分析实验，与全局保形基线相比，该方法在聚类观测模式下显著提升了预测区间的覆盖精度和尾部可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的聚类自适应空间基函数与聚类感知保形校准方案，有效解决了非均匀采样下DeepKriging模型的容量分配与校准问题，为时空分布预测提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: DeepKriging-style models, such as Spatio-Temporal DeepKriging, improve scalability through basis-function embeddings and stochastic gradient learning; however, fixed regular-grid spatial bases remain inefficient under highly non-uniform sampling patterns, often over-allocating capacity to sparse regions while under-resolving dense clusters. To address this limitation, we propose a practical extension of DeepKriging for reliable spatio-temporal distributional forecasting, incorporating cluster-adaptive spatial bases - whose centers and scales are initialized from {the spatial sampling density} - to better capture heterogeneous spatial sampling, together with cluster-aware conformal calibration that determines prediction-interval widths within spatial clusters (with a global fallback when calibration samples are insufficient). The resulting calibration pipeline explicitly targets spatial heterogeneity and local miscalibration, and experiments, including simulation studies and PM$_{2.5}$ data analysis, demonstrate substantially improved coverage accuracy and tail reliability under clustered observation patterns compared with a global conformal baseline.

</details>


### [297] [Testing Equality of Conditional Distributions via Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06930)
*Hanjia Gao,Linjun Huang,Yun Yang,Xiaofeng Shao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成模型的非参数条件分布相等性检验方法。通过交叉生成策略对齐协变量，将检验转化为再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）上的经验过程，并利用乘数自助法进行校准，兼具计算可行性与双重稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验两条件分布是否相等在因果推断、迁移学习等领域十分重要。传统方法要么依赖条件密度比估计，要么需要在高维协变量空间上进行局部平滑，在高维设定下表现不佳。因此，需要一种能避免直接估计条件密度比、且能处理高维协变量与多元响应的检验方法。

Method: 提出交叉生成框架：从每个样本中学习一个条件生成器，用于在对方样本的协变量值处生成响应，从而直接比较生成响应与观测响应。总体版本定义了一个条件差异度量（在适当重叠条件下等价于条件分布相等）。样本版本构造的检验统计量为RKHS索引经验过程的上确界，采用乘数自助法进行校准。通过交替最大化和核技巧实现高效计算。

Result: 推导了原假设和备择假设下检验统计量的极限分布，证明自助法的有效性和检验的一致性。进一步证明该检验对条件生成器的估计误差具有双重稳健性：即使生成器估计不完美，只要两边生成器的误差满足一定条件，检验仍能保持正确的一类错误率。

Conclusion: 所提方法在多元响应和高维协变量场景下表现良好，兼具理论保证（双重稳健性、自助一致性）和计算可行性，适用于真实数据分析。

Abstract: We study the problem of testing whether two conditional distributions are equal using generative models. The proposed method learns a conditional generator from each sample and uses it to create responses at covariate values observed in the other sample, allowing generated and observed responses to be compared directly. By aligning covariates through cross-generation, the approach avoids conditional density-ratio estimation and local smoothing over high-dimensional covariates. The population version of this construction yields a conditional discrepancy that characterizes equality of the two conditional distributions under suitable overlap conditions, while the sample version leads to a test statistic defined as the supremum of an RKHS-indexed empirical process with multiplier bootstrap calibration. A computationally efficient algorithm for evaluating the statistic and its bootstrap analogue is developed based on alternating maximization and the kernel trick. Theoretically, we derive the limiting distribution of the test statistic under both the null and alternative hypotheses, prove bootstrap validity and consistency of the resulting test, and show that the proposed procedure attains a double-robustness property with respect to conditional generator estimation errors. Simulations and real data applications suggest that the proposed method performs well for multivariate responses and high-dimensional covariates.

</details>


### [298] [Causal inference of Plackett-Burman designs in applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06961)
*Shuchen Chang,Zhi-ming Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出基于潜在结果的Plackett-Burman设计因果推断框架，给出Neymanian估计量及其方差-协方差，并进行Fisherian随机化检验与区间构造，最后通过四个应用实例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Plackett-Burman设计在工业与工程实验中广泛应用，但缺乏系统的因果推断理论支撑。为填补这一空白，作者以四个实际应用为驱动，建立PB设计下的因果效应量化与统计推断体系。

Method: 基于潜在结果框架，在有限总体下定义PB设计的因果效应；推导Neymanian无偏估计量及其估计方差与协方差；利用随机化推断算法进行精确的Fisher尖锐零假设检验，并构造Fisherian置信区间。

Result: 得到了PB设计下因果效应的Neymanian无偏估计量及解析形式的方差/协方差表达式；实现了基于随机化分布的尖锐零假设检验和区间估计。四个应用案例验证了方法的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将因果推断与PB设计结合，为因子筛选实验提供了严格的统计推断工具，兼具理论严谨性与应用可行性。

Abstract: Driven by four applications of Plackett-Burman (PB) designs, this paper proposes a causal inference framework based on potential outcomes. First, we define the causal effects of the PB designs under finite populations. The Neymanian estimator of causal effects is then obtained, including the estimated variance and covariance. Furthermore, we conduct a sharp null-hypothesis test and construct the Fisherian interval using an algorithm. Finally, the proposed methods are illustrated through these applications.

</details>


### [299] [Influence of continuous predictor modelling methods on prediction stability in clinical prediction model development: an empirical comparison using real clinical data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07052)
*Phichayut Phinyo,Pakpoom Wongyikul,Noraworn Jirattikanwong,Natthanaphop Isaradech,Wuttipat Kiratipaisarl,Suppachai Lawanaskol,Noppadon Seesuwan,Wachiranun Sirikul*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了连续预测变量的不同建模方法对临床预测模型稳定性的影响，发现线性模型（LIN）在较小样本下即可实现稳定预测，而更复杂的方法（如XGBoost）需要更大样本量才能稳定，且XGBoost虽区分度高但校准始终不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测稳定性对可靠临床预测模型的开发日益重要，但连续预测变量建模方式对稳定性的影响尚不清楚。本研究旨在系统性比较不同建模策略在不同样本量下的预测稳定性表现。

Method: 使用19,418例急诊科患者的真实临床数据，构建五个样本量场景（437至8,739例）。比较六种方法：中位数二分类（DIC）、三分位数分类（TER）、线性项（LIN）、二次项（QUA）、多变量分数多项式（MFP）和极端梯度提升（XGB）。采用bootstrap框架评估预测稳定性，以至少90%个体预测的平均绝对预测误差（MAPE）≤5%作为稳定性标准，并通过内部验证估计乐观校正AUC和校准度。

Result: 在n=437时，无方法达到稳定性标准，LIN最稳定，DIC次之。n=874时，DIC和LIN达到稳定，校准相似但DIC的AUC更低。n=1,748时，QUA达到稳定，MFP和XGB未达标。n=3,496和8,739时，所有方法均稳定。LIN、QUA、MFP、XGB的AUC普遍高于DIC和TER，XGB的AUC最高但持续存在校准不良。

Conclusion: 连续预测变量建模方法确实影响预测稳定性。LIN从基础样本量起即可实现稳定预测，而QUA、MFP和XGB需要更大样本。尽管XGB区分度最高，但校准问题持续存在。在较小数据集中，更简单的方法（尤其是线性模型）可能提供更稳定的预测。

Abstract: Background and objective: Prediction stability is increasingly recognised as important for reliable clinical prediction model development, but the effect of continuous predictor modelling choices is unclear. This study examined how approaches to modelling continuous predictors influence prediction stability. Methods: We used a real clinical dataset of 19,418 emergency department patients to create five sample size scenarios ranging from 437 to 8,739 patients. Six methods were compared: dichotomisation at the median (DIC), tertile categorisation (TER), linear terms (LIN), quadratic terms (QUA), multivariable fractional polynomials (MFP), and extreme gradient boosting (XGB). Prediction stability was evaluated using a bootstrap-based framework. Optimism-corrected AUC and calibration were estimated through internal validation. A method was considered stable when at least 90% of individual predictions had a mean absolute prediction error (MAPE) <=5%. Results: Stability increased with sample size and varied by method. At n = 437, no method met the stability criterion; LIN was the most stable, followed by DIC. At n = 874, DIC and LIN achieved stable predictions with similar calibration, although DIC had lower AUC. At n = 1,748, QUA achieved stability, whereas MFP and XGB did not. At n = 3,496 and n = 8,739, all methods achieved stability. LIN, QUA, MFP, and XGB generally had higher AUCs than DIC and TER, while XGB showed the highest AUC but persistent miscalibration. Conclusion: Continuous predictor modelling methods appeared to influence prediction stability. LIN achieved stable predictions from the base sample size onwards, whereas QUA, MFP, and XGB required larger samples. Although XGB showed high discrimination, calibration concerns persisted. These findings suggest that, in smaller datasets, simpler approaches, particularly LIN, may provide more stable predictions.

</details>


### [300] [When can a posterior predictive check identify the learning rate? Exact degeneracy in Gaussian models and implications for Generalised Bayesian Inerence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07169)
*Nam Anh Le*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对 Zafar 和 Nicholls (2024) 提出的基于后验预测检验（PPC）选择广义贝叶斯学习率 η 的方法进行了精确有限样本分析。作者证明，在高斯线性模型下，使用参考先验时该选择器具有枢轴性——p 值仅依赖于 (n, d, η)，与数据无关，导致选择器失效，通常塌缩至最小网格值，造成过度回火和预测区间膨胀；该现象限于高斯尺度-位置族和参考先验，在信息先验下消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义贝叶斯推断通过学习率 η 对似然进行回火以缓解模型误设，而 η 的选择至关重要。Zafar & Nicholls (2024) 提出的基于 PPC 的选择方法缺乏对其理论性质的深入理解，尤其是在该方法是否总能有效识别 η、在何种条件下可能失效等问题上需要严格分析。

Method: 作者在高斯线性模型框架下进行精确有限样本分析：分别考虑已知方差（使用平坦先验）和未知方差（使用参考先验）两种情形，推导 PPC p 值的解析表达式，分析其与数据、模型参数和 η 的依赖关系，并进一步考察方差误设和信息先验下的表现。

Result: 在已知方差下，PPC p 值恒等于 P(χ²_n > RSS/σ²₀)，与 η 无关，选择器 η-不变；方差误设时呈双侧非识别性。在未知方差和参考先验下，p 值仅依赖于 (n, d, η)，与实际数据和数据生成过程完全无关，导致选择器输出在观测数据前即已固定，通常塌缩至最小网格值，造成过度回火。该枢轴性特属于高斯尺度-位置族和参考先验，引入信息先验后消失。

Conclusion: 该 PPC 选择器的适用范围受限：在高斯线性模型配合参考先验这一典型设定下无法有效识别学习率。作者据此提出了一个无需数据的预筛选诊断方法，用于判断该选择器在特定模型-先验组合下是否适用。

Abstract: Generalised Bayesian inference tempers the likelihood by a learning rate $η$ to mitigate model misspecification, and the choice of $η$ is consequential. Zafar and Nicholls (2024) proposed selecting $η$ by a posterior predictive check (PPC): one chooses the smallest $η$ at which a log-likelihood PPC $p$-value is not rejected. An exact, finite-sample analysis of this selector on the Gaussian linear model is given. With known variance and a flat prior, the PPC $p$-value equals $P(χ^2_n > \mathrm{RSS}/σ_0^2)$ for every $η$, so the selector is $η$-invariant; under variance misspecification it is two-sided non-identifying. With unknown variance and the reference prior, the $p$-value depends only on $(n,d,η)$ and not on the realised data or the data-generating process. Consequently the selector's output is fixed before any data are seen, typically collapsing to the smallest grid value, which over-tempers and inflates predictive intervals relative to held-out selection. The phenomenon is a pivotality property specific to the Gaussian scale--location family and the reference prior; it disappears under informative priors. These results delineate the selector's scope, identify a canonical class on which it cannot identify the learning rate, and motivate a cheap, data-free pre-screening diagnostic.

</details>


### [301] [Principal Component Analysis for Multivariate Extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07213)
*Dan Cooley,Anne Sabourin,Troy Wixson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本章探讨了在保留与多元极值分析相关的关键信息的同时，降低数据维度的多种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多元极值分析中，数据往往具有高维特性，直接分析面临"维度灾难"问题——计算复杂度高、统计推断不稳定、模型可解释性差。因此，需要在不丢失对极值分析至关重要的信息（如尾部依赖结构、极端事件间的关联模式）的前提下，对数据进行降维处理。

Method: 本章系统性地综述了适用于多元极值分析的降维方法，可能包括：基于极值理论的降维技术（如利用尾依赖函数或极值copula进行特征筛选）、主成分分析（PCA）在极值数据上的变体、稀疏建模方法（如稀疏极值回归）、以及基于聚类的降维策略等，重点考察这些方法在保留尾部依赖结构和极端值信息方面的有效性。

Result: （该摘要为书籍章节的概述性描述，未提供具体的数值结果或实验结论。）

Conclusion: 通过适当的降维技术，可以在显著减少数据复杂度的同时，保持对多元极值分析至关重要的尾部依赖信息和极端事件结构，从而提升后续极值建模的效率和可靠性。

Abstract: This chapter explores ways to reduce the dimensionality of the data while preserving key information relevant to the analysis of multivariate extreme values.

</details>


### [302] [One-step Outcome Imputation: An Alternative to Multiple Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07174)
*Andreas Nordland,Klaus K. Holst,David Redek,Christian B. Pipper,Aske T. Iversen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于影响函数的单步估计方法，用以替代传统的多重插补（MI）和Rubin法则，能够为目标插补模型所隐含的治疗效应提供渐近有效的推断，同时避免MI的随机性和计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机对照试验中，缺失结局常用多重插补处理，但Rubin法则在参考基线插补等常用方法下无法提供有效的标准误估计，导致推断失效。

Method: 通过显式地靶向特定插补模型所隐含的治疗效应，利用影响函数构造高效的单步（one-step）估计量，从而直接获得治疗效应及其渐近有效的标准误。

Result: 该方法能够为各类插补模型（包括参考基线插补和依赖并发事件的插补）提供渐近有效的推断，且无需进行多重插补的随机抽样和大量计算。

Conclusion: 所提出的单步估计方法是一种优于传统MI+Rubin法则的替代方案，在保证渐近有效推断的同时显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Missing outcomes in randomized controlled trials are often handled by multiple imputation (MI). Rubin's rules are routinely used to estimate standard errors but can fail to provide valid standard error estimates for some commonly used procedures, such as reference-based imputation. We propose a one-step alternative by explicitly targeting the treatment effect implied by a given imputation model and constructing an efficient one-step estimator for that treatment effect via its influence function. Unlike Rubin's rules, this approach yields asymptotically valid inference. Moreover, the proposed method circumvents the stochastic component and computational burden of MI. We illustrate the approach with examples spanning a range of imputation models, including reference-based imputation and intercurrent-event-dependent imputation.

</details>


### [303] [Learning Collapsed Patterns in Compositional Data: A Bayesian Heterogeneous Relative-Shift Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07373)
*Maoran Xu,Guanyu Hu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯异质性相对偏移回归模型，通过投影收缩先验和混合有限混合先验，联合学习高维成分数据中的潜在聚类与简约效应结构，并开发了可扩展的混合MCMC算法，在理论和实证上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成分数据分析方法局限于经典设定，无法同时处理高维预测变量和跨潜在子群体的异质性回归效应，即无法在恢复潜在聚类结构的同时实现维度缩减。

Method: 提出贝叶斯异质性相对偏移回归模型：(1)在可识别对比上施加投影收缩先验，诱导混合成分内精确的系数绑定；(2)采用混合有限混合先验推断聚类数量；(3)开发混合MCMC算法，在NUTS中嵌入确定性替代塌缩算子。

Result: 理论上建立了潜在划分和聚类特定效应结构的后验一致性；模拟实验证实了准确的聚类恢复和强预测性能；在跨国宏观经济数据和空间转录组学应用中展示了方法的可解释性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了高维成分数据中异质性回归效应的双重挑战——潜在聚类恢复与维度缩减，为复杂成分数据问题提供了一个原则性强、可扩展且理论有保障的贝叶斯框架。

Abstract: Relative-shift regression provides a principled framework for modeling compositional covariates by quantifying how the response changes when mass is reallocated from one component to another. Yet many emerging compositional data problems extend beyond this classical setting, involving high-dimensional predictors and regression effects that vary across latent subpopulations. This complexity poses a dual challenge unmet by existing methods: recovering latent cluster structure while simultaneously achieving dimension reduction within each cluster. We propose a Bayesian heterogeneous relative-shift regression model that jointly learns latent clusters and parsimonious effect structures. Methodologically, we combine a projection-based shrinkage prior on identifiable contrasts, which induces exact coefficient ties within mixture components, with a mixture of finite mixtures prior that infers the number of clusters. Computationally, we develop a scalable hybrid MCMC algorithm that embeds a deterministic surrogate collapse operator within NUTS. Theoretically, we establish posterior consistency for both the latent partition and cluster-specific effect structures. Simulations confirm accurate recovery and strong predictive performance, and applications to cross-country macroeconomic data and spatial transcriptomics demonstrate the method's interpretability and practical utility.

</details>


### [304] [Deriving the Variance-Minimizing Design for Standard Addition via c-Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07406)
*Gerhard Gössler,Vera Hofer,Walter Goessler*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统整理了标准加入法最优实验设计的散落知识，证明了在非递减测量误差下线性响应的最优设计为两点设计，并探讨了测量分配比例、浓度范围及加权回归对精密度和偏差的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于标准加入法最优设计的知识分散于文献中，且部分深奥的数学文献对非设计最优性理论领域的分析化学工作者难以获取。作者旨在系统归纳现有成果，并将最优性理论应用于标准加入法的特殊情境，填补可查阅的综合性文献空白。

Method: 本文采用综述与理论推导相结合的方式：首先汇总分析化学文献中已有的相关结果，然后运用设计最优性理论，针对线性响应函数情形，在测量误差为非递减（常数、线性增长或二次增长）的条件下，推导最优设计（两点设计）及其测量分配策略，并进一步分析浓度范围、加权回归的必要性以及偏差影响。

Result: （1）对于非递减测量误差（常数、线性或二次增长），线性响应下最优设计始终为两点设计，与误差方差的具体行为无关；（2）最优测量分配比例取决于具体设置，可能显著偏离50:50；（3）最大加入浓度（范围）会影响结果；（4）使用两点设计时无需加权回归即可获得最优结果，而多于两个加标浓度的设计则需要加权。

Conclusion: 标准加入法在线性响应和非递减测量误差下，两点设计是最优的，且无需加权回归。测量分配比例和浓度范围的选择需根据具体情境优化，这对提高分析精密度和减小偏差具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Knowledge about optimal designs for standard addition seems to be scattered among literature and is also, at least partially, only available in mathematical literature that is not quickly accessible for readers not skilled in the field of design optimality theory. Therefore, the idea for this work was to summarize what is already available in analytical literature and to apply the respective results from optimality theory, where needed, to the special case of standard addition. It is shown, for measurement errors that are non-decreasing, e.g., are constant or increase linearly or quadratically with increasing analyte concentration, that the optimal design in the case of a linear response is a two-point design irrespective of the particular behavior of measurement error variance. In addition, it is demonstrated that the optimal allocation of measurements depends on the concrete setting, which means that the optimal distribution of measurements may deviate significantly from a 50:50 ratio. It is also investigated how the range, i.e., the largest added concentration influences the result. Last but not least, also the question of applying weighted regression is discussed and it is shown, that, in contrast to designs using more than two spiked concentrations, no weighting is necessary to achieve optimal results, when a two-point design is used. While the focus lies on the precision of the concentration estimate also the implications for the bias are investigated.

</details>


### [305] [Community Detection on a Randomly Growing Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07447)
*Jianxiang Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类超出随机块模型（SBM）框架的马尔可夫随机网络上的社区检测问题，提出了一种由K棵优先连接树与Erdős-Rényi边混合生成的网络模型，并设计了能够恢复核心节点社区标签的两阶段算法，同时证明了完全恢复所有节点标签的不可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界网络往往呈现幂律度分布、链式结构和枢纽节点等特征，这些在标准SBM下难以产生。因此，需要在更贴近现实的网络生成模型下研究社区检测问题。

Method: 构建了一个随机网络增长模型：首先生成K棵独立的优先连接树（每棵树代表一个社区），然后用Erdős-Rényi边连接这些树。在仅给定最终图而无任何增长过程信息的情况下，提出两阶段算法：第一阶段对高度节点进行分类，第二阶段将社区分配扩展到其余节点。算法利用了到达时间、度等多种节点中心性概念。

Result: 理论上证明了任何算法都无法一致地恢复所有节点的社区标签；但所设计的算法能够在几种中心性度量（如到达时间、度）下可证明地恢复核心节点子集的社区标签。数值实验和合作者网络的真实数据应用验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 在优先连接树与随机边混合的网络模型下，虽然全局社区恢复不可能，但通过两阶段算法可以有效恢复核心节点的社区归属，这为超越SBM框架的社区检测提供了新的理论和实践工具。

Abstract: We study community detection on Markovian random networks outside of the Stochastic Block Model (SBM) framework. Specifically, we consider a random network growth process which generates $K$ separate preferential attachment trees and connects them with Erdős--Rényi edges, so that each tree represents a community and each node inherits the label of the tree to which it belongs. This model is able to produce many features of real world networks that are improbable under SBM, such as power law degree distribution and the existence of chains and hubs. Given only the final graph, without any knowledge of the growth process, we seek to recover the unobserved community membership of the nodes. We first prove that it is impossible for any algorithm to consistently recover the community label of all the nodes. However, we design algorithms which are provably able to recover the community labels of subsets of central nodes, for several different notions of node centrality such as arrival time or degree. Our procedure consists of two stages where, in the first stage, we classify high degree nodes and then, in the second stage, extend the community assignments to the remaining vertices. Numerical experiments and a real data application on a coauthorship network demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [306] [Covariance-Adaptive Residualization and Stagewise Calibration for Dependent Multiple Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07466)
*Prasenjit Ghosh,Arijit Chakrabarti*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多元高斯均值同时假设检验方法，基于MRD残差化程序，引入Gavrilov等人(2009)的广义逐步向下临界常数进行逐阶段校准。该方法在保留协方差自适应残差化机制的同时，用简单的逐阶段校准规则替代了原有的模型依赖阈值设定，计算效率更高，且在不同依赖结构下展现出更低的误分类风险和更强的信号恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意协方差依赖结构下进行多元高斯均值的多重假设检验时，现有方法如MRD程序依赖模型特定的阈值设定，缺乏灵活性和计算效率。作者希望开发一种保留协方差自适应残差化优势、同时具有更简洁校准策略的新方法。

Method: 以Cohen等人(2009)的最大残差向下(MRD)程序为基础，引入Gavrilov等人(2009)的广义逐步向下临界常数作为新的校准策略，形成逐阶段校准的单调残差逐步向下检验程序。同时推导出通过单一活跃精度矩阵表达所有活跃残差的替代表示，以降低计算复杂度。通过仿真研究评估方法性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明：该方法在多种依赖结构下，标准化误分类风险通常低于几种常用边际检验程序；在结构化依赖模型下，错误发现率接近名义水平，错误未发现率极低，功效接近1，平均拒绝次数接近真实信号期望数量。

Conclusion: 协方差自适应残差化与逐阶段校准之间存在高度有利的交互作用，所提方法在依赖多重检验中具有显著的实证优势。由于属于Ghosh和Chakrabarti(2026)研究的单调残差逐步向下程序类，其可容许性可直接从该理论推得。

Abstract: In this paper, we study simultaneous hypothesis testing for multivariate Gaussian means under arbitrary covariance dependence. Building on the Maximum Residual Down (MRD) procedure of Cohen et al. (2009), we investigate a new calibration strategy based on the generalized step-down critical constants of Gavrilov et al. (2009). The resulting procedure retains the covariance-adaptive residualization mechanism of MRD while replacing the original model-dependent threshold specification with a simple stagewise calibration rule. Since the proposed procedure belongs to the class of monotone residual-based step-down procedures studied by Ghosh and Chakrabarti (2026), its admissibility follows directly from their theory. We also derive alternative representations of the MRD residual statistics that express all active residuals through a single active precision matrix, substantially reducing computational complexity. Simulation studies across a broad range of dependence structures show that the proposed methodology often achieves a lower normalized misclassification risk than several widely used marginal testing procedures. Under several structured dependence models, the procedure also exhibits strong signal-recovery behavior, attaining false discovery rates near the nominal level, extremely small false non-discovery rates, powers approaching one, and average numbers of rejections close to the expected number of true signals. These findings provide empirical evidence that covariance-adaptive residualization and stagewise calibration may interact in a highly favorable manner for dependent multiple testing.

</details>
